  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在 102 名患者中,44 名(43%)在 HCT 后 1 年以上确诊。总体而言,28 人 (27%) 患有 LRTI,其中 8 人是从 URTI 发展而来。RSV 相关 死亡率 为 2/102 (2.0%);入住 ICU 的比例为 6/102 (5.9%)。
  • Moderna 疫苗接种 后出现症状的风险与辉瑞后相似。然而,对于 GSK 疫苗 来说,无论是单独服用还是与另一种 疫苗 一起服用,出现症状的风险都较高。没有明确的证据表明葛兰素史克的联合用药状态会带来不同的风险。

  • RSV阳性率在严格的NPI阶段(9.41%)较疫情前阶段(17.12%)有所下降,在政策过渡阶段达到最低水平(5.72%),并在疫情后阶段有所反弹(13.62%)。传统的冬季高峰转移到秋季和夏季,同时感染的人群转向3-6岁儿童。

  • Among 102 patients, 44 (43%) were diagnosed more than 1 year after HCT. Overall, 28 (27%) developed LRTI, including eight who progressed from URTI.
  • Neither marker differed between patients and controls.
  • The review concludes that the risk of symptoms after Moderna vaccination is similar to that after Pfizer.
  • Age-specific seroprevalence was estimated and compared with previously reported pre-pandemic estimates, and catalytic modelling was used to estimate the vaccination window during and after the COVID-19 pandemic.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 本文概述了随时间变化的呼吸道疾病水平,包括 RSV (呼吸道合胞病毒) 和相关主题的参考。它不报告特定的 暴发 或事件。 [news-6f1a267183d85705]。
  • 《感染与公共卫生杂志》上发表的一篇综合叙述性评论详细介绍了沙特阿拉伯 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的负担,强调了其在儿科呼吸道疾病发病率和入院率中的作用。该审查基于观察性调查、基于医院的 监测 报告、分子基因分型研究和健康经济模型。
  • 文章报道,韩国疾病管理和预防机构建议成年人,特别是 65 岁及以上的老年人使用 RSV 疫苗接种,因为他们患严重疾病的风险很高。它还强调,RSV 可导致成人严重下呼吸道疾病,尤其是患有基础疾病的成人。
  • 为瑞士联邦公共卫生办公室开发的综合卫生技术评估模型表明,与使用 尼塞韦单抗 且不进行预防的婴儿直接免疫相比,使用 RSVpreF 疫苗 进行 呼吸道合胞病毒 孕产妇免疫是一种具有成本效益的公共卫生策略。该研究由瑞士联邦公共卫生办公室于 2025 年委托进行。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Respiratory syncytial virus news developments.
  • The article provides an overview of respiratory illness levels over time, including references to RSV (Respiratory Syncytial Virus) and related topics. It does not report a specific outbreak or event.
  • A comprehensive narrative review published in the Journal of Infection and Public Health details the burden of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in Saudi Arabia, highlighting its role in pediatric respiratory morbidity and hospital admissions.
  • The article reports that the Korea Disease Control and Prevention Agency recommends RSV vaccination for adults, particularly those aged 65 and older, due to their high risk of severe disease. [news-241ff1ec5251343d].
  • A comprehensive health technology assessment model developed for the Swiss Federal Office of Public Health indicates that maternal immunisation against respiratory syncytial virus using the RSVpreF vaccine represents a cost-effective public health strategy when compared with direct infant immunisation using nirsevimab and with no prophylaxis.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Public health research & practice  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-10  |  DOI: 10.1071/pu25136  |  Date basis: published_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 4

2019-2023 年悉尼儿童医院网络收治的幼儿的增强型 呼吸道合胞病毒 监测。

Enhanced respiratory syncytial virus surveillance of young children admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, 2019-2023.
作者： Gemma L Saravanos, Kathryn Meredith, Shirley Wong, Rebecca Burrell, Adam Bartlett, Nusrat Homaira, Philip N Britton, Rama Kandasamy
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在全面描述被送往悉尼儿童医院网络的呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)疾病患儿的临床特征、管理及预后,确定严重疾病的总体负担,并识别特定人群进入重症监护病房(ICU)的风险因素。
设计与对象
本研究是一项在澳大利亚新南威尔士州首府地区两家三级儿科医院进行的多中心、回顾性队列研究研究,历时5年,涉及4414名<2岁因实验室确诊RSV感染而住院的儿童,包括所有ICU住院患儿以及非ICU住院患儿的随机样本。
核心方法
该研究涉及通过医院记录来识别参与者,主要结果指标是 RSV 住院和 ICU 入院的特征和 发病率 率。使用多元逻辑回归评估与 ICU 入住相关的因素。
主要结果
5 年期间,4414 名儿童因实验室确诊的 RSV 住院,其中 11.3% (499/4414) 入住 ICU。与非 ICU 样本 (n = 1000;3.3 个月 vs 7.1 个月,P < 0.0001) 相比,ICU 队列研究 中的中位年龄 (n = 499) 更年轻。
解释与创新
该研究发现,月龄下降、早产、土著身份和心脏病是导致入住 ICU 几率较高的因素,并强调了严重 RSV 疾病的特定风险群体。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为针对土著儿童、早产儿和患有心脏病的儿童的有针对性的预防计划提供信息,从而有可能减轻这些人群中严重 RSV 疾病的负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的数据缺失而受到限制,但由于全面的数据收集和统计分析,证据是强有力的。

Enhanced respiratory syncytial virus surveillance of young children admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, 2019-2023.

Authors: Gemma L Saravanos, Kathryn Meredith, Shirley Wong, Rebecca Burrell, Adam Bartlett, Nusrat Homaira, Philip N Britton, Rama Kandasamy
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: In this study, we aimed to comprehensively describe the clinical characteristics, management and outcomes of children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network (SCHN), determine the full burden of severe disease and identify population-specific risk factors for intensive care unit (ICU) admission. STUDY TYPE: Multi-centre, retrospective cohort study conducted over 5 years. METHODS: The study was conducted at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, before implementation of government-funded immunisation programs against RSV in children. Participants were children aged <2 years hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV from 1 January 2019 to 31 December 2023, comprising all ICU admitted, and a random sample of non-ICU admitted patients. The main outcome measures were characteristics and incidence rate of RSV hospitalisations and ICU admissions among children aged <2 years. RESULTS: Over a 5-year period, 4414 children were hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% (499/4414) were admitted to ICU. The median age was younger in the ICU cohort (n = 499) compared with the non-ICU sample (n = 1000; 3.3 vs 7.1 months, P < 0.0001). Factors associated with higher odds of ICU admission in multivariate logistic regression were decreasing month of age (OR 1.10, 95% CI 1.06-1.11), prematurity (OR 2.17, 95% CI 1.61-2.91), Indigenous identity (OR 4.10, 95% CI 2.30-7.56) and cardiac disease (OR 3.39, 95% CI 2.06-5.70). The average hospitalisation incidence rate was 1615 per 100,000 (95% CI 1565-1666), with higher rates among children aged <1 year, 2141 per 100,000 (95% CI 2061-2224) compared with those aged 1-2 years, 1088 per 100,000 (95% CI 1030-1148). CONCLUSIONS: RSV immunisation programs are expected to substantially reduce the burden of hospitalisation among Australian children in their first year of life. Children who are Indigenous, were born prematurely or have heart disease are at greatest risk of severe disease and may benefit from extended preventative programs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to comprehensively describe the clinical characteristics, management and outcomes of children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, determine the full burden of severe disease and identify population-specific risk factors for intensive care unit (ICU) admission.
Design and population
The study was a multi-centre, retrospective cohort study conducted over 5 years at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, involving 4414 children aged <2 years hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, comprising all ICU admitted and a random sample of non-ICU admitted patients.
Core methods
The study involved identifying participants through hospital records, with the main outcome measures being characteristics and incidence rate of RSV hospitalisations and ICU admissions. Factors associated with ICU admission were assessed using multivariate logistic regression.
Main results
Over a 5-year period, 4414 children were hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% (499/4414) were admitted to ICU. The median age was younger in the ICU cohort (n = 499) compared with the non-ICU sample (n = 1000; 3.3 vs 7.1 months, P < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified decreasing month of age, prematurity, Indigenous identity and cardiac disease as factors associated with higher odds of ICU admission, highlighting specific risk groups for severe RSV disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted preventative programs for Indigenous children, those born prematurely and children with cardiac disease, potentially reducing the burden of severe RSV disease in these populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for missing data, but the evidence is strong due to the comprehensive data collection and statistical analysis.

呼吸道合胞病毒 同种异体造血细胞移植后感染:15 年单中心结果 队列研究

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection After Allogeneic Hematopoietic Cell Transplantation: Outcomes in a 15‐Year Single‐Center Cohort
作者： Anna Nordlander, Vendela Brismar, Lovisa Lundh, Per T Ljungman
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是同种异体造血细胞移植 (allo-HCT) 后常见且可能严重的并发症。我们描述了 15 年单中心 队列研究 的结果。方法 对卡罗林斯卡大学医院(2010 年 1 月至 2024 年 4 月)所有经 PCR 确诊 RSV 感染的异基因 HCT 受者进行回顾性分析。根据 HCT 的时间评估结果;利巴韦林分析仅限于就诊时患有上呼吸道感染 (URTI) 且有进展为下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 风险的患者。结果 102 例患者中,44 例(43%)在 HCT 后 1 年以上确诊。总体而言,28 人 (27%) 患有 LRTI,其中 8 人是从 URTI 发展而来。RSV 相关 死亡率 为 2/102 (2.0%);入住 ICU 的比例为 6/102 (5.9%)。在 HCT 后 30 天内诊断出 RSV 时,从 URTI 进展为 LRTI 的频率比之后诊断的要高(4/11 [36%] vs. 4/71 [5.6%];p = 0.010)。在 43 名服用利巴韦林(主要是口服)的患者中,31 名患者处于 URTI 阶段;5/31 (16%) 的患者在开始治疗后出现进展,而 2/51 (3.9%) 未经治疗的患者出现进展 (p = 0.098)。LRTI 中的 CRP 高于单独的 URTI(中位数 50 与 17 mg/L;p = 0.011),但细菌合并感染也更频繁(32% 与结论)allo-HCT 后 RSV 相关 死亡率 较低,但发病率延长,特别是移植后早期。考虑到许多患者中 RSV 发生较晚,以及诊断和移植实践与历史相比的差异,应谨慎解释研究结果。队列研究。这项回顾性研究没有显示口服利巴韦林的保护作用,而 治疗(包括 RSV 疫苗接种)的前瞻性研究是有必要的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是同种异体造血细胞移植 (allo-HCT) 后常见且可能严重的并发症。本研究旨在评估该人群 RSV 感染的结果。
设计与对象
2010 年 1 月至 2024 年 4 月在卡罗林斯卡大学医院进行了一项回顾性单中心 队列研究 研究,分析了所有 PCR 确诊的 RSV 感染的异基因 HCT 受者。
核心方法
根据造血细胞移植(HCT)的时间评估结果;利巴韦林分析仅限于就诊时患有上呼吸道感染 (URTI) 且有进展为下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 风险的患者。
主要结果
在 102 名患者中,44 名(43%)在 HCT 后 1 年以上确诊。总体而言,28 人 (27%) 患有 LRTI,其中 8 人是从 URTI 发展而来。RSV 相关 死亡率 为 2/102 (2.0%);入住 ICU 的比例为 6/102 (5.9%)。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,allo-HCT 后 RSV 相关的 死亡率 较低,但发病率延长,尤其是移植后早期。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,allo-HCT 后 RSV 感染与长期发病相关,尤其是在移植后早期,这凸显了预防和 治疗 策略进一步研究的必要性。
局限与证据强度
这项回顾性研究没有显示口服利巴韦林的保护作用。考虑到许多患者较晚出现 RSV,并且与历史 队列研究 相比,诊断和移植实践存在差异,因此应谨慎解释研究结果。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection After Allogeneic Hematopoietic Cell Transplantation: Outcomes in a 15‐Year Single‐Center Cohort

Authors: Anna Nordlander, Vendela Brismar, Lovisa Lundh, Per T Ljungman
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a frequent and potentially severe complication after allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplantation (allo‐HCT). We describe outcomes in a 15‐year single‐center cohort. Methods All allo‐HCT recipients with PCR‐confirmed RSV infection at Karolinska University Hospital (January 2010–April 2024) were analyzed retrospectively. Outcomes were evaluated by time from HCT; ribavirin analyses were restricted to patients with upper respiratory tract infection (URTI) at presentation, at risk of progression to lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI). Results Among 102 patients, 44 (43%) were diagnosed more than 1 year after HCT. Overall, 28 (27%) developed LRTI, including eight who progressed from URTI. RSV‐associated mortality was 2/102 (2.0%); ICU admission occurred in 6/102 (5.9%). Progression from URTI to LRTI was more frequent when RSV was diagnosed within 30 days of HCT than later (4/11 [36%] vs. 4/71 [5.6%]; p = 0.010). Of 43 patients given ribavirin (predominantly oral), 31 were treated at the URTI stage; 5/31 (16%) progressed after initiation versus 2/51 (3.9%) untreated ( p = 0.098). CRP was higher in LRTI than in URTI alone (median 50 vs. 17 mg/L; p = 0.011), but bacterial co‐infection was also more frequent (32% vs. Conclusion RSV‐associated mortality after allo‐HCT was low, but morbidity was prolonged, particularly early after transplantation. Findings should be interpreted cautiously given the late occurrence of RSV in many patients and differences in diagnostics and transplant practice compared with historical cohorts. This retrospective study did not show a protective effect of oral ribavirin. Prospective studies of prevention and treatment, including RSV vaccination, are warranted.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a frequent and potentially severe complication after allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplantation (allo‐HCT). This study aimed to evaluate outcomes of RSV infection in this population.
Design and population
A retrospective single-center cohort study was conducted at Karolinska University Hospital from January 2010 to April 2024, analyzing all allo‐HCT recipients with PCR-confirmed RSV infection.
Core methods
Outcomes were evaluated by time from hematopoietic cell transplantation (HCT); ribavirin analyses were restricted to patients with upper respiratory tract infection (URTI) at presentation, at risk of progression to lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI).
Main results
Among 102 patients, 44 (43%) were diagnosed more than 1 year after HCT. Overall, 28 (27%) developed LRTI, including eight who progressed from URTI. RSV-associated mortality was 2/102 (2.0%); ICU admission occurred in 6/102 (5.9%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that RSV-associated mortality after allo‐HCT was low, but morbidity was prolonged, particularly early after transplantation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSV infection after allo‐HCT is associated with prolonged morbidity, especially in the early post-transplant period, highlighting the need for further research on prevention and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
This retrospective study did not show a protective effect of oral ribavirin. Findings should be interpreted cautiously given the late occurrence of RSV in many patients and differences in diagnostics and transplant practice compared with historical cohorts.

儿童 RSV 感染中的入院血清 GDF-15 和可溶性 CD8A:一项探索性横断面研究

Admission serum GDF-15 and soluble CD8A in pediatric RSV infection: an exploratory cross-sectional study
作者： Burak Ezer, Mustafa Gençeli, Talha Üstüntaş, Hilal Sena Çiftci, Enes Kasapoğlu, Anida Firzi Bala, Abdullah Akkuş, Özge Metin Akcan, Mehmet Özdemir, Mustafa Genceli
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染儿童和对照儿童之间的生长分化因子 15 (GDF-15) 和可溶性 CD8-α (sCD8A),并检查 GDF-15 是否与住院时间相关。
设计与对象
纳入了 32 名确诊 RSV 感染住院儿童和 14 名年龄相仿、无急性疾病的门诊对照儿童。该研究是一项探索性横断面分析。
核心方法
通过ELISA测定血清GDF-15和sCD8A。通过 Spearman 相关性和 Mann-Whitney U 检验评估关联性;根据年龄、C 反应蛋白 (CRP) 和呼吸支持进行泊松回归调整作为敏感性分析。
主要结果
Neither marker differed between patients and controls. Among patients, GDF-15 correlated with length of stay (r = .516, 95% CI .20-.73, p = .002); adjusted estimates were model dependent (Poisson incidence rate ratio 1.19, 95% CI 1.05-1.34; log-linear model p = .114). sCD8A correlated weakly and inversely with CRP (r = -.368, p = .038) and red cell distribution width (r = -.394, p = .026).
解释与创新
较高的入院 GDF-15 与较长的住院时间相关,但未使用经过验证的措施评估严重程度。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,GDF-15 可能有潜力作为预测 RSV 感染住院时间的生物标志物,但还需要进一步验证。
局限与证据强度
探索性关联并未建立预后价值,需要在更大的前瞻性 队列研究 中进行确认。由于该研究的观察性质和缺乏经过验证的严重性衡量标准,证据是中等的。

Admission serum GDF-15 and soluble CD8A in pediatric RSV infection: an exploratory cross-sectional study

Authors: Burak Ezer, Mustafa Gençeli, Talha Üstüntaş, Hilal Sena Çiftci, Enes Kasapoğlu, Anida Firzi Bala, Abdullah Akkuş, Özge Metin Akcan, Mehmet Özdemir, Mustafa Genceli
Original abstract (English)
AIMS: To compare growth differentiation factor-15 (GDF-15) and soluble CD8-alpha (sCD8A) between children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection and controls, and to examine whether GDF-15 is associated with length of hospital stay. PATIENTS & METHODS: Thirty-two children hospitalized with confirmed RSV infection and 14 outpatient controls of similar age without acute illness were enrolled. Serum GDF-15 and sCD8A were measured by ELISA. Associations were assessed by Spearman correlation and Mann-Whitney U tests; Poisson regression adjusted for age, C-reactive protein (CRP) and respiratory support served as a sensitivity analysis. Remaining analyses were exploratory and uncorrected for multiplicity. RESULTS: Neither marker differed between patients and controls. Among patients, GDF-15 correlated with length of stay (r = .516, 95% CI .20-.73, p = .002); adjusted estimates were model dependent (Poisson incidence rate ratio 1.19, 95% CI 1.05-1.34; log-linear model p = .114). sCD8A correlated weakly and inversely with CRP (r = -.368, p = .038) and red cell distribution width (r = -.394, p = .026). CONCLUSION: Higher admission GDF-15 was associated with longer hospitalization; severity was not assessed with a validated measure. These exploratory associations do not establish prognostic value and require confirmation in larger prospective cohorts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare growth differentiation factor-15 (GDF-15) and soluble CD8-alpha (sCD8A) between children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection and controls, and to examine whether GDF-15 is associated with length of hospital stay.
Design and population
Thirty-two children hospitalized with confirmed RSV infection and 14 outpatient controls of similar age without acute illness were enrolled. The study was an exploratory cross-sectional analysis.
Core methods
Serum GDF-15 and sCD8A were measured by ELISA. Associations were assessed by Spearman correlation and Mann-Whitney U tests; Poisson regression adjusted for age, C-reactive protein (CRP) and respiratory support served as a sensitivity analysis.
Main results
Neither marker differed between patients and controls. Among patients, GDF-15 correlated with length of stay (r = .516, 95% CI .20-.73, p = .002); adjusted estimates were model dependent (Poisson incidence rate ratio 1.19, 95% CI 1.05-1.34; log-linear model p = .114). sCD8A correlated weakly and inversely with CRP (r = -.368, p = .038) and red cell distribution width (r = -.394, p = .026).
Interpretation and novelty
Higher admission GDF-15 was associated with longer hospitalization, though severity was not assessed with a validated measure.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that GDF-15 may have potential as a biomarker for predicting hospitalization duration in RSV infection, though further validation is needed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The exploratory associations do not establish prognostic value and require confirmation in larger prospective cohorts. The evidence is moderate due to the study's observational nature and lack of validated severity measures.

呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) IgG 抗体的年龄特异性 患病率 为肯尼亚沿海幼儿 疫苗接种 窗口提供信息:COVID -19 大流行期间和之后的观察

Age-Specific Prevalence of Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) IgG antibodies to inform the toddler vaccination window in coastal Kenya: Observations during and after the COVID -19 pandemic
作者： Ernie Atieno Odero, Mary Wanjiku, Charles N. Agoti, Christopher A. Moxon, Brenda Kwambana-Adams, Robert S. Heyderman, Arnold W. Lambisia, Clement Lewa, Martin N. Mutunga, Esther Nyadzua Katama
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴幼儿下呼吸道感染的主要原因。确定 RSV 疫苗接种 的适当年龄需要考虑母源抗体减弱的年龄特异性模式和感染风险。然而,COVID-19 大流行期间 RSV 流通的中断可能改变了这些模式。重新评估 疫苗接种 时间对于评估和实施儿童 RSV 疫苗 至关重要。方法 本研究使用了 2020 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间入住基利菲县转诊医院 (KCRH) 儿科病房的 760 份血清样本,无论 诊断 为何。通过间接酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 对血清样品中 RSV 特异性 IgG 抗体滴度进行定量。对特定年龄的 血清流行率 进行了估计,并与之前报告的大流行前估计进行了比较,并使用催化模型来估计 COVID-19 大流行期间和之后的 疫苗接种 窗口。结果 参与者的中位年龄为 11 个月(IQR 5-24),79.6%(95% CI:76.7-82.5)血清呈阳性。血清流行率 在 3 岁儿童中为 100%。据估计,母体抗体以每年 1.73 的速度减少(95% CI 1.60–2.25),0–32 个月时的感染力 (FOI) 为每人年 1.75(95% CI 1.54–2.04),而 >32–144 个月时的感染力 (FOI) 为每人年 0.01(95% CI 0.01–0.29)。估计初次感染的平均年龄为 17.4 个月 (95% CI 13.5–19.0),最佳 疫苗接种 年龄为 8.7 个月 (95% CI 7.2–9.2)。结论 尽管 COVID-19 大流行对 RSV 传播 模式和特定年龄的 血清流行率 产生影响,但估计的最佳 疫苗接种 窗口基本保持不变,略有向老年组的转变。

解释与创新
估计初次感染的平均年龄为 17.4 个月 (95% CI 13.5–19.0),最佳 疫苗接种 年龄为 8.7 个月 (95% CI 7.2–9.2)。所提供的证据不报告基于证据的解释或在所提供的文本中明确报告的解释和新颖性的对比。

Age-Specific Prevalence of Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) IgG antibodies to inform the toddler vaccination window in coastal Kenya: Observations during and after the COVID -19 pandemic

Authors: Ernie Atieno Odero, Mary Wanjiku, Charles N. Agoti, Christopher A. Moxon, Brenda Kwambana-Adams, Robert S. Heyderman, Arnold W. Lambisia, Clement Lewa, Martin N. Mutunga, Esther Nyadzua Katama
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of lower respiratory tract infection in infants and young children. Determining the appropriate age for RSV vaccination requires consideration of age-specific patterns of maternal antibody waning and infection risk. However, disruptions in RSV circulation during the COVID-19 pandemic may have altered these patterns. Reassessment of vaccination timing is essential to inform the evaluation and implementation of a childhood RSV vaccine. Methods This study utilised 760 serum samples, collected from children admitted to the paediatric ward of Kilifi County Referral Hospital (KCRH), between January 2020 and December 2023, regardless of diagnosis. RSV-specific IgG antibody titers in the serum samples were quantified by indirect Enzyme- Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA). Age-specific seroprevalence was estimated and compared with previously reported pre-pandemic estimates, and catalytic modelling was used to estimate the vaccination window during and after the COVID-19 pandemic. Results Participants had a median age of 11 months (IQR 5–24), and 79.6% (95% CI: 76.7–82.5) were seropositive. Seroprevalence was 100% in children 3 years. Maternal antibodies were estimated to wane at a rate of 1.73 per year (95% CI 1.60–2.25), and the force of infection (FOI) at 0–32 months was 1.75 per person-year (95% CI 1.54–2.04) while that at >32–144 months was 0.01 per person-year (95% CI 0.01–0.29). Estimated average age at primary infection was 17.4 months (95% CI 13.5–19.0), and optimal vaccination age was 8.7 months (95% CI 7.2–9.2). Conclusion Despite the influence of the COVID-19 pandemic on RSV transmission patterns and age-specific seroprevalence, the estimated optimal vaccination window remained largely unchanged, with a slight shift towards the older age group.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of lower respiratory tract infection in infants and young children. Determining the appropriate age for RSV vaccination requires consideration of age-specific patterns of maternal antibody waning and infection risk.
Design and population
This study utilised 760 serum samples, collected from children admitted to the paediatric ward of Kilifi County Referral Hospital (KCRH), between January 2020 and December 2023, regardless of diagnosis. Participants had a median age of 11 months (IQR 5–24), and 79.6% (95% CI: 76.7–82.5) were seropositive.
Core methods
RSV-specific IgG antibody titers in the serum samples were quantified by indirect Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA). Maternal antibodies were estimated to wane at a rate of 1.73 per year (95% CI 1.60–2.25), and the force of infection (FOI) at 0–32 months was 1.75 per person-year (95% CI 1.54–2.04) while that at >32–144 months was 0.01 per person-year (95% CI 0.01–0.29).
Main results
Age-specific seroprevalence was estimated and compared with previously reported pre-pandemic estimates, and catalytic modelling was used to estimate the vaccination window during and after the COVID-19 pandemic. The supplied evidence does not report exact sample counts, percentages, effects, P values, confidence intervals or uncertainty for the main results.
Interpretation and novelty
Estimated average age at primary infection was 17.4 months (95% CI 13.5–19.0), and optimal vaccination age was 8.7 months (95% CI 7.2–9.2). The supplied evidence does not report evidence-grounded interpretation or contrast explicitly reported in the supplied text for the interpretation and novelty.
Scientific and public-health significance
Despite the influence of the COVID-19 pandemic on RSV transmission patterns and age-specific seroprevalence, the estimated optimal vaccination window remained largely unchanged, with a slight shift towards the older age group. The supplied evidence does not report specific implications for surveillance, diagnosis, treatment, vaccination, ecology, risk assessment, prevention or future study.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report design limitations, bias, confounding, sample size, controls, generalisability, abstract-only evidence or a justified high/moderate/low assessment. The evidence strength is high.

压力之下:从澳大利亚悉尼快速实施 呼吸道合胞病毒 孕产妇 疫苗 和婴儿 尼塞韦单抗 中吸取的经验教训。

Under pressure: lessons learnt from rapid implementation of the respiratory syncytial virus maternal vaccine and infant nirsevimab in Sydney, Australia.
作者： Isabella Townshend, Kate Griew, Rosemary Burke, Vendula Blaya-Novakova, Rebekah Dashwood, Sarah Everett, Leena Gupta
摘要中文翻译目标:分享悉尼地方卫生区 (SLHD) 推出 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防产品的经验教训,并提供两家妇产医院母亲和婴儿的免疫接种数据。服务类型:推出 RSV 母体 疫苗 和用于新生儿的可注射长效单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗,以保护他们免受 RSV 感染和住院治疗。方法:以计数和比例的形式对两家 SLHD 妇产医院的 RSV 免疫状况电子病历中的去识别化汇总数据进行描述性分析。实施研究综合框架被用作反思推出过程的镜头。结果:两家 SLHD 医院分娩妇女的 RSV 疫苗接种 覆盖率从推出第一个月(2025 年 2 月)的 4% 提高到 2025 年 6 月至 9 月的 70-74%。随着产前覆盖率的提高,需要 尼塞韦单抗 的活产婴儿数量减少。在符合条件的新生儿中,36%的家庭拒绝了尼塞韦单抗,但没有记录拒绝的原因。随着推广的进展,RSV 免疫状态的记录得到改善。经验教训:多学科领导者和临床医生的参与对于协调应对和计划成功推出至关重要。利用现有的教育结构、员工会议和使用在线预录音可以优化临床医生教育。新计划需要时间才能融入常规临床实践,而涉及为不同群体提供多种免疫产品的计划需要明确的信息传递和系统保障措施以防止错误。将免疫接种作为标准做法纳入医疗记录工作流程可以支持计划的采用和采用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在分享快速实施 RSV 预防产品的经验教训,并提供两家妇产医院母亲和婴儿的免疫接种数据。该研究解决了对有效策略的需求,以改善 RSV 疫苗 和 尼塞韦单抗 在临床环境中的采用。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在悉尼地方卫生区的两家妇产医院针对新生儿推出 RSV 孕产妇 疫苗 和 尼塞韦单抗。它分析了 2025 年 2 月至 2025 年 9 月的免疫状况数据。
核心方法
从电子病历中提取的匿名汇总数据被描述性分析为计数和比例。采用实施研究综合框架(Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research)评估推广过程。
主要结果
RSV 疫苗接种 在两家医院分娩的妇女的覆盖率从推出第一个月的 4% 增加到 2025 年 6 月至 9 月的 70-74%。随着产前覆盖率的提高,需要 尼塞韦单抗 的活产婴儿数量减少。在符合条件的新生儿中,36%的家庭拒绝了尼塞韦单抗,拒绝的原因没有记录。RSV 免疫状态的记录随着时间​​的推移而得到改善。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明协调一致的努力和清晰的信息对于项目的成功推出至关重要。该研究强调了将免疫接种纳入临床工作流程以支持计划采用的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为提高产妇环境中 RSV 预防产品免疫接种的策略提供参考。他们还深入探讨了在常规临床实践中实施新的 疫苗 和单克隆抗体所面临的挑战。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它依赖于去识别化的汇总数据,并且缺乏 尼塞韦单抗 拒绝的记录原因。由于分析的观察性质和缺乏对照组,证据的强度中等。

Under pressure: lessons learnt from rapid implementation of the respiratory syncytial virus maternal vaccine and infant nirsevimab in Sydney, Australia.

Authors: Isabella Townshend, Kate Griew, Rosemary Burke, Vendula Blaya-Novakova, Rebekah Dashwood, Sarah Everett, Leena Gupta
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To share lessons learnt from the Sydney Local Health District (SLHD) rollout of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prevention products, and present data on immunisation uptake for mothers and babies in two maternity hospitals. TYPE OF SERVICE: Rollout of the RSV maternal vaccine and the injectable, long-acting monoclonal antibody nirsevimab for newborn infants to protect them from RSV infection and hospitalisation. METHODS: Deidentified summary data from the electronic medical record on RSV immunisation status in two SLHD maternity hospitals were analysed descriptively as counts and proportions. The Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research was used as a lens to reflect on the rollout process. RESULTS: RSV vaccination coverage in women giving birth in two SLHD hospitals improved from 4% in the first month of rollout (February 2025) to a range of 70-74% from June to September 2025. As antenatal coverage improved, the number of live born babies requiring nirsevimab decreased. Among eligible newborns, 36% of families declined nirsevimab, with reason for refusal not recorded. Documentation of RSV immunisation status improved as the rollout progressed. LESSONS LEARNT: Engaging multidisciplinary leaders and clinicians is critical for a coordinated response and program rollout success. Leveraging existing education structures, staff meetings and using online prerecording can optimise clinician education. New programs take time to embed into routine clinical practice, and programs that involve delivering multiple immunisation products for distinct groups require clear messaging and systemic safeguards to prevent errors. Integrating immunisation into medical record workflows as standard practice can support program adoption and uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to share lessons from the rapid implementation of RSV prevention products and present data on immunisation uptake for mothers and babies in two maternity hospitals. The research addresses the need for effective strategies to improve RSV vaccine and nirsevimab adoption in clinical settings.
Design and population
The study involved the rollout of the RSV maternal vaccine and nirsevimab for newborn infants in two maternity hospitals within the Sydney Local Health District. It analysed immunisation status data from February 2025 to September 2025.
Core methods
Deidentified summary data from electronic medical records were analysed descriptively as counts and proportions. The Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research was used to evaluate the rollout process.
Main results
RSV vaccination coverage in women giving birth in the two hospitals increased from 4% in the first month of rollout to 70-74% from June to September 2025. The number of live born babies requiring nirsevimab decreased as antenatal coverage improved. Among eligible newborns, 36% of families declined nirsevimab, with reasons for refusal not recorded. Documentation of RSV immunisation status improved over time.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that coordinated efforts and clear messaging are essential for successful program rollout. The study highlights the importance of integrating immunisation into clinical workflows to support program adoption.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies for improving immunisation uptake for RSV prevention products in maternity settings. They also provide insights into the challenges of implementing new vaccines and monoclonal antibodies in routine clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on deidentified summary data and the lack of recorded reasons for nirsevimab refusal. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the analysis and the absence of control groups.

RSV-毛细支气管炎 肺部以外的全身表现

Systemic Manifestations of RSV-Bronchiolitis Beyond the Lungs
作者： Hina Naveed, Rabia Tabassum, Naveed Ashraf, Awais Abid, Abdul Salam Wazir, Shahzad Munir, Maham Zahid
摘要中文翻译目的:评估 PCR 确诊的 RSV 毛细支气管炎 婴幼儿的肺外表现,并探讨其与临床病情和院内结局的关系。方法:2025 年 11 月至 2026 年 2 月,在伊斯兰堡联邦政府综合医院儿科对 PCR 确诊的 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的 1-48 个月儿童进行了一项前瞻性观察性研究。患有其他病毒或细菌合并感染或慢性疾病的患者被排除在外。他们在住院期间接受了神经、心血管、胃肠道和代谢表现的评估。记录了人口统计数据、临床观察、实验室值、护理水平、呼吸支持、住院时间和结果。由于未前瞻性记录经过验证的 毛细支气管炎 严重程度评分,因此护理水平被用作临床敏锐度的实用衡量标准;入院代表低危重护理,HDU/ICU 入院代表高危重护理。数据分析在SPSS 24中进行,p值<0.05被认为是显着的。结果:共有 123 名 RSV-毛细支气管炎 儿童患者入组。29.3% 的患者观察到肺外表现,包括代谢表现,其次是胃肠道 (27.6%)、神经系统 (26.7%) 和心血管[A1] [D2] [HN3] (21%) 受累。代谢表现包括低钙血症(15.4%)、低钠血症(9.8%)、低钾血症(8.1%)、高钾血症(7.3%)和高钠血症(0.8%)。胃肠道受累表现为腹泻(11%)和呕吐(9%)。神经系统问题包括癫痫发作(18%)和意识水平改变(8%)。心血管表现包括心肌炎(11%)、杂音(9%)和心动过速(1%)。平均住院时间为6.2±3.7天。与无心脏受累的患者(5.8±3.0 天;p=0.020)相比,有心脏症状(包括心动过速(9.14±6.6 天)、杂音(6.18±3.0 天)和心肌炎(7.0±0.0 天)的患者)的平均住院时间明显更长。然而,胃肠道(p=0.127)和神经系统表现(p=0.127)患者的住院时间没有显着差异。在严重程度分层分析中,59.6%的病房患者和70.6%的HDU/ICU患者出现任何肺外表现(p=0.302);没有哪个系统显示两组之间存在统计学上的显着差异。在 6 例死亡病例中,50% 的患者患有相关的代谢紊乱。结论:RSV 毛细支气管炎 表现出 PCR 确诊的 RSV 毛细支气管炎 住院儿童的多系统受累,且不限于需要 HDU/ICU 护理的患者。心血管受累与较长的住院时间相关,而个体肺外系统的严重程度没有统计学上的显着差异。早期识别这些肺外表现对于改善临床结果和优化 治疗 至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 PCR 确诊的 RSV 毛细支气管炎 婴幼儿的肺外表现,并检查其与临床病情和院内结果的关系。
设计与对象
2025 年 11 月至 2026 年 2 月,伊斯兰堡联邦政府综合医院儿科进行了一项前瞻性观察研究,研究对象为 PCR 确诊的 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的 1-48 个月儿童。患有其他病毒或细菌合并感染或慢性疾病的患者被排除在外。
核心方法
参与者在住院期间接受神经、心血管、胃肠道和代谢表现的评估。记录了人口统计数据、临床观察、实验室值、护理水平、呼吸支持、住院时间和结果。护理水平被用作临床敏锐度的实用衡量标准,入院代表较低敏锐度护理,HDU/ICU 入院代表较高敏锐度护理。数据分析在 SPSS 24 中进行,p 值 < 0.05 被认为是显着的。
主要结果
共有 123 名 RSV 毛细支气管炎 儿童患者入组。29.3% 的患者有代谢表现,27.6% 的患者有胃肠道问题,26.7% 的患者有神经系统问题,21% 的患者有心血管问题。平均住院时间为6.2±3.7天。与没有心脏症状的患者相比,有心脏症状的患者住院时间明显更长(p=0.020)。胃肠道(p=0.127)和神经系统表现(p=0.127)的住院时间没有显着差异。在严重程度分层分析中,59.6%的病房患者和70.6%的HDU/ICU患者出现任何肺外表现(p=0.302)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,RSV 毛细支气管炎 涉及 PCR 确诊的 RSV 毛细支气管炎 住院儿童的大量多系统表现,这些表现不仅限于需要 HDU/ICU 护理的儿童。心血管受累与较长的住院时间相关,而个体肺外系统没有观察到基于严重程度的统计学差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了识别 RSV 毛细支气管炎 肺外表现对于改善住院儿童的临床结果和优化 治疗 策略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用护理水平作为临床敏锐度的实用衡量标准,而不是经过验证的 毛细支气管炎 严重程度评分,这可能会引入偏差。由于观察设计和潜在的混杂因素,证据的强度中等。

Systemic Manifestations of RSV-Bronchiolitis Beyond the Lungs

Authors: Hina Naveed, Rabia Tabassum, Naveed Ashraf, Awais Abid, Abdul Salam Wazir, Shahzad Munir, Maham Zahid
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To evaluate the extrapulmonary manifestations in infants and young children admitted with PCR- confirmed RSV bronchiolitis and to examine their relationship with clinical acuity and in-hospital outcomes. Methodology: A prospective observational study was carried out in the Department of Pediatrics, Federal Government Polyclinic Hospital, Islamabad from November 2025 to February 2026. in children aged 1-48 months with PCR-confirmed RSV bronchiolitis. Patients with other viral or bacterial co-infection or chronic medical conditions were excluded. They were assessed for neurological, cardiovascular, gastrointestinal, and metabolic manifestations during hospitalization. The demographic data, clinical observations, laboratory values, level of care, respiratory support, the length of hospital stay, and outcomes were documented. Because a validated bronchiolitis severity score was not prospectively recorded, level of care was used as a pragmatic measure of clinical acuity; ward admission represented lower-acuity care and HDU/ICU admission higher-acuity care. The analysis of data was performed in SPSS 24, and the p-value < 0.05 was considered significant. Results: A total of 123 pediatric patients with RSV-bronchiolitis were enrolled. Extrapulmonary findings were observed with metabolic manifestations in 29.3% patients followed by gastrointestinal(27.6%), neurological(26.7%) and cardiovascular[A1] [D2] [HN3] (21%) involvement. Metabolic manifestations included hypocalcemia(15.4%), hyponatremia(9.8%), hypokalemia(8.1%), hyperkalemia(7.3%) and hypernatremia(0.8%). Gastrointestinal involvement was in the form of diarrhea(11%) and vomiting(9%). Neurological problems included seizures(18%) and altered level of consciousness(8%). Cardiovascular manifestations included myocarditis(11%), murmur(9%) and tachycardia(1%). The mean duration of hospital stay was 6.2±3.7 days. The average hospital stay was significantly longer among patients with cardiac manifestations, including tachycardia (9.14±6.6 days), murmurs (6.18±3.0 days), and myocarditis (7.0±0.0 days), compared with those without cardiac involvement (5.8±3.0 days; p=0.020). However, no significant difference in hospital stay was observed in patients with gastrointestinal (p=0.127) and neurological manifestations (p=0.127). In severity-stratified analysis, any extrapulmonary manifestation occurred in 59.6% of ward patients and 70.6% of HDU/ICU patients (p=0.302); no individual system showed a statistically significant difference between the two groups. Amongst the six recorded deaths, 50% of patients had associated metabolic derangements. Conclusion: RSV bronchiolitis demonstrates substantial multisystem involvement in hospitalized children with PCR-confirmed RSV bronchiolitis which were not confined to patients requiring HDU/ICU care. Cardiovascular involvement was associated with a longer hospital stay, while no statistically significant severity-based difference was demonstrated for individual extrapulmonary systems. Early recognition of these extrapulmonary manifestations is crucial to improve clinical outcome and optimize treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the extrapulmonary manifestations in infants and young children with PCR-confirmed RSV bronchiolitis and to examine their relationship with clinical acuity and in-hospital outcomes.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted in the Department of Pediatrics, Federal Government Polyclinic Hospital, Islamabad from November 2025 to February 2026, involving children aged 1-48 months with PCR-confirmed RSV bronchiolitis. Patients with other viral or bacterial co-infections or chronic medical conditions were excluded.
Core methods
Participants were assessed for neurological, cardiovascular, gastrointestinal, and metabolic manifestations during hospitalization. Demographic data, clinical observations, laboratory values, level of care, respiratory support, length of hospital stay, and outcomes were documented. Level of care was used as a pragmatic measure of clinical acuity, with ward admission representing lower-acuity care and HDU/ICU admission higher-acuity care. Data analysis was performed in SPSS 24, with a p-value < 0.05 considered significant.
Main results
A total of 123 pediatric patients with RSV bronchiolitis were enrolled. Extrapulmonary findings were observed in 29.3% patients with metabolic manifestations, 27.6% with gastrointestinal issues, 26.7% with neurological problems, and 21% with cardiovascular involvement. The mean duration of hospital stay was 6.2±3.7 days. Patients with cardiac manifestations had significantly longer hospital stays compared to those without (p=0.020). No significant difference in hospital stay was observed for gastrointestinal (p=0.127) and neurological manifestations (p=0.127). In severity-stratified analysis, any extrapulmonary manifestation occurred in 59.6% of ward patients and 70.6% of HDU/ICU patients (p=0.302).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that RSV bronchiolitis involves substantial multisystem manifestations in hospitalized children with PCR-confirmed RSV bronchiolitis, which are not limited to those requiring HDU/ICU care. Cardiovascular involvement was associated with longer hospital stays, while no statistically significant severity-based difference was observed for individual extrapulmonary systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of recognizing extrapulmonary manifestations in RSV bronchiolitis to improve clinical outcomes and optimize treatment strategies for hospitalized children.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used level of care as a pragmatic measure of clinical acuity instead of a validated bronchiolitis severity score, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational design and potential confounding factors.

加拿大呼吸道合胞病毒 流行病学的九年监测:2016年至2025年的季节性模式

Nine-Year Surveillance of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Epidemiology in Canada: Seasonal Patterns from 2016 to 2025
作者： Qin Wang, Jing-Xian Hong, Xiao-Yan Dong
摘要中文翻译了解 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 患病率 的流行病学特征对于制定有效的公共卫生干预措施至关重要。这项观察性研究分析了来自加拿大的 5,666 687 条 RSV 监测 记录(世界卫生组织全球流感规划,2016-2025 年)。计算了每周和每年的阳性率,并可视化了九年趋势。包括发病在内的季节性指标对每个 RSV 季节的周、高峰周、抵消周和流行持续时间进行了量化。在大流行前时期(2016-2017 年至 2019-2020 年),RSV 季节性的强度和时间每年都有所不同。COVID-19 大流行严重扰乱了 RSV 的流通:2020-2021 年无明确流行季节的季节阳性率为 0.08%,而 2021-2022 年提出了最早发作(第 33 周)和最长持续时间(29 周)的两波浪潮。对于非药物干预 (NPI) 后时期 (2023)(2024)(2024)(2025),RSV 季节显示出较早的发病时间和持续时间较短的抵消。尽管时间指标相对于大流行前的基线发生了变化,但由于流行季节的样本量有限,应谨慎解释不显着的结果。大流行前的阳性率 (5.73%) 显着高于大流行时代 (4.85%) 和 NPI 后 (3.98%) 的值。总之,在 COVID-19 大流行之前,加拿大的 RSV 季节性每年都在变化。大流行导致 RSV 季节的开始、抵消和持续时间发生明显变化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
了解 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 患病率 的流行病学特征对于制定有效的公共卫生干预措施至关重要。这项观察性研究分析了来自加拿大的 5,666,687 条 RSV 监测 记录(世界卫生组织全球流感规划,2016-2025 年)。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2016 年至 2025 年加拿大的 5,666,687 条 RSV 监测 记录,涵盖大流行前、大流行和大流行后时期。
核心方法
计算每周和每年的阳性率,并可视化九年趋势。对每个 RSV 季节的季节性指标(包括发病周、高峰周、抵消周和流行持续时间)进行了量化。
主要结果
在大流行前时期(2016-2017年至2019-2020年),RSV季节性的强度和时间每年都在变化。COVID-19大流行严重扰乱了RSV的传播:2020-2021年季节的阳性率为0.08%,没有明确的流行季节,而2021-2022年则出现了两波,最早出现(第33周),持续时间最长(29周)。在 NPI 后时期(2023-2024 年至 2024-2025 年),RSV 季节出现较早,抵消时间较短。大流行前的聚合阳性率 (5.73%) 显着高于大流行时代 (4.85%) 和 NPI 后 (3.98%) 的值。
解释与创新
研究发现,在 COVID-19 大流行之前,加拿大的 RSV 季节性每年都在变化。这场大流行导致RSV季节的开始、抵消和持续时间发生了明显的变化。这些发现为了解大流行对 RSV 流行病学 的影响提供了见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 的 RSV 策略提供参考,特别是在未来大流行或类似干扰的情况下。
局限与证据强度
尽管时间指标相对于大流行前的基线发生了变化,但由于流行季节的样本量有限,应谨慎解释不显着的结果。

Nine-Year Surveillance of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Epidemiology in Canada: Seasonal Patterns from 2016 to 2025

Authors: Qin Wang, Jing-Xian Hong, Xiao-Yan Dong
Original abstract (English)
Understanding the epidemiological characteristics of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prevalence is essential for developing effective public health interventions.This observational study analyzed 5,666 687 RSV surveillance records from Canada (WHO Global Influenza Programme, 2016-2025).Weekly and annual positivity rates were computed, and nine-year trends were visualized.Seasonal metrics including onset week, peak week, offset week and epidemic duration were quantified for each RSV season.In the pre-pandemic period (2016-2017 to 2019-2020), RSV seasonality varied annually in intensity and timing.The COVID-19 pandemic substantially disrupted RSV circulation: the 2020-2021 season yielded a positivity rate of 0.08% without a defined epidemic season, while 2021-2022 presented two waves with the earliest onset (week 33) and longest duration (29 weeks).For the post-non-pharmaceutical intervention (NPI) period (2023)(2024)(2024)(2025), RSV seasons showed earlier onset and offset with shorter durations.Although timing metrics shifted relative to pre-pandemic baselines, non-significant results should be interpreted cautiously owing to limited sample size of epidemic seasons.Aggregated pre-pandemic positivity (5.73%) was significantly higher than pandemic-era (4.85%) and post-NPI (3.98%) values.In conclusion, prior to the COVID-19 pandemic, RSV seasonality in Canada varied annually.The pandemic caused observable shifts in the onset, offset, and duration of RSV seasons.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Understanding the epidemiological characteristics of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prevalence is essential for developing effective public health interventions. This observational study analyzed 5,666,687 RSV surveillance records from Canada (WHO Global Influenza Programme, 2016-2025).
Design and population
The study analyzed 5,666,687 RSV surveillance records from Canada from 2016 to 2025, covering the pre-pandemic, pandemic, and post-pandemic periods.
Core methods
Weekly and annual positivity rates were computed, and nine-year trends were visualized. Seasonal metrics including onset week, peak week, offset week, and epidemic duration were quantified for each RSV season.
Main results
In the pre-pandemic period (2016-2017 to 2019-2020), RSV seasonality varied annually in intensity and timing. The COVID-19 pandemic substantially disrupted RSV circulation: the 2020-2021 season yielded a positivity rate of 0.08% without a defined epidemic season, while 2021-2022 presented two waves with the earliest onset (week 33) and longest duration (29 weeks). For the post-NPI period (2023-2024 to 2024-2025), RSV seasons showed earlier onset and offset with shorter durations. Aggregated pre-pandemic positivity (5.73%) was significantly higher than pandemic-era (4.85%) and post-NPI (3.98%) values.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that prior to the COVID-19 pandemic, RSV seasonality in Canada varied annually. The pandemic caused observable shifts in the onset, offset, and duration of RSV seasons. These findings provide insights into the impact of the pandemic on RSV epidemiology.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for RSV, particularly in the context of future pandemics or similar disruptions.
Limitations and evidence strength
Although timing metrics shifted relative to pre-pandemic baselines, non-significant results should be interpreted cautiously owing to limited sample size of epidemic seasons.

呼吸道合胞病毒——第二个 RSV 季节期间儿童重症监护病房入院的相关儿童

Respiratory Syncytial Virus–Related Pediatric Intensive Care Unit Admissions in Children During Their Second RSV Season
作者： Regina M. Simeone, Laura Divens Zambrano, Margaret M. Newhams, Amanda B. Payne, Amber O. Orzel-Lockwood, Natasha B. Halasa, Jemima M. Calixte, Aline B. Maddux, Kathleen Chiotos, Satoshi Kamidani
摘要中文翻译重要性:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 住院率在 2 岁以下儿童中最高。对于进入第二个 RSV 季节的某些高危儿童,建议使用单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗。对于儿童在第二个 RSV 季节期间与 RSV 相关的呼吸衰竭的危险因素,人们对先前存在的健康状况知之甚少。目的:通过按 尼塞韦单抗 资格状态和既往健康状况对儿童进行分类,并比较他们的人口学和临床特征,描述因严重 RSV 入住儿科重症监护病房 (PICU) 的儿童。设计、设置和参与者:这项 队列研究 研究分析了 2023 年至 2024 年和 2024 年至 2025 年 RSV 季节入住 30 家儿科医院 PICU 的 8 至 23 个月大的美国儿童。所有儿童的呼吸道样本均呈 RSV 阳性,并接受高流量鼻插管、无创通气或有创机械通气的呼吸支持。分析基于 2023 年 8 月建议的美国第二季 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准。暴露:先前存在的健康状况可能与严重 RSV 疾病风险增加相关。患者被分为 4 个相互排斥的资格状态和既往健康状况组:(1) 符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准,(2) 具有其他已确定的 RSV 风险状况(有一些证据表明严重 RSV 风险增加),(3) 具有其他既往状况,(4) 没有既往状况。主要结果和措施:根据既往健康状况水平对患者人口特征和接受的呼吸支持水平进行比较。结果:总共纳入 574 名儿童(中位 [IQR] 年龄,1.2 [1.1-1.5] 岁;317 名男性 [55.2%])。总体而言,47 名患者 (8.2%) 符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准,76 名患者 (13.2%) 患有其他已确定的 RSV 风险状况,96 名患者 (16.7%) 患有其他既往病史,355 名患者 (61.8%) 没有既往病史。符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准的儿童需要有创机械通气的比例高于患有其他已确定的 RSV 风险状况的儿童、具有其他既往健康状况或没有既往健康状况的儿童(40.4% [47 名中的 19 名] vs 17.1% [76 名中的 13 名]、10.4% [96 名中的 10 名]、5.9% [355 名中的 21 名];P 代表趋势<.001)。结论和相关性:在因严重 RSV 入住 PICU 的儿童中,8.2% 符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准,13.2% 具有其他已确定的 RSV 风险状况。这些发现可能有助于为未来关于美国 尼塞韦单抗 资格的 RSV 第二季建议的审议提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决关于先前存在的健康状况作为儿童在第二个 RSV 季节期间与 RSV 相关的呼吸衰竭危险因素的知识差距,因为 RSV 住院率在 2 岁以下儿童中最高。
设计与对象
这项 队列研究 研究分析了 2023 年至 2024 年和 2024 年至 2025 年 RSV 季节期间入住 30 家儿科医院儿科重症监护室 (PICU) 的 8 至 23 个月大的美国儿童。
核心方法
该研究根据 尼塞韦单抗 资格状况和既往健康状况将患者分为四组,并使用 2023 年 8 月建议的美国第二季 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准比较了他们的人口统计和临床特征。
主要结果
总共包括 574 名儿童,其中 47 名(8.2%)符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准,76 名(13.2%)有其他已确定的 RSV 风险状况,96 名(16.7%)有其他既往病史,355 名(61.8%)没有既往病史。与其他组相比,符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准的儿童需要有创机械通气的比例较高(分别为 40.4% vs 17.1%、10.4% 和 5.9%;趋势 P <.001)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准的儿童因严重 RSV 需要有创机械通气的风险较高,这可能为美国未来关于 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准的审议提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于加深对高危儿童严重 RSV 危险因素的理解,有可能指导有针对性的干预措施并改善 尼塞韦单抗 使用的临床决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注特定年龄组和地理区域而受到限制,并且证据基于美国的 尼塞韦单抗 资格标准。由于 RSV 严重程度和 治疗 实践可能存在区域差异,因此这些发现的可信度为中等。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus–Related Pediatric Intensive Care Unit Admissions in Children During Their Second RSV Season

Authors: Regina M. Simeone, Laura Divens Zambrano, Margaret M. Newhams, Amanda B. Payne, Amber O. Orzel-Lockwood, Natasha B. Halasa, Jemima M. Calixte, Aline B. Maddux, Kathleen Chiotos, Satoshi Kamidani
Original abstract (English)
Importance: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) hospitalization rates are highest among children younger than 2 years. For certain high-risk children entering their second RSV season, the monoclonal antibody nirsevimab is recommended. Little is known regarding preexisting health conditions as risk factors for RSV-related respiratory failure in children during their second RSV season. Objective: To describe children admitted to the pediatric intensive care unit (PICU) for severe RSV by classifying them by their nirsevimab eligibility status and preexisting health conditions, and comparing their demographic and clinical characteristics. Design, Setting, and Participants: This cohort study analyzed US children aged 8 to 23 months admitted to the PICU in 30 pediatric hospitals in the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 RSV seasons. All children had an RSV-positive result from a respiratory sample and received respiratory support with high-flow nasal cannula, noninvasive ventilation, or invasive mechanical ventilation. Analyses were based on the US criteria for second-season nirsevimab eligibility as recommended in August 2023. Exposure: Preexisting health conditions potentially associated with increased risk of severe RSV disease. Patients were classified into 4 mutually exclusive groups of eligibility status and preexisting health conditions: (1) met nirsevimab eligibility criteria, (2) with other identified RSV risk conditions (with some evidence of increased risk for severe RSV), (3) with other preexisting conditions, and (4) with no preexisting conditions. Main Outcomes and Measures: Patient demographic characteristics and level of respiratory support received were compared by level of preexisting health conditions. Results: A total of 574 children (median [IQR] age, 1.2 [1.1-1.5] years; 317 males [55.2%]) were included. Overall, 47 patients (8.2%) met the nirsevimab eligibility criteria, 76 (13.2%) had other identified RSV risk conditions, 96 (16.7%) had other preexisting conditions, and 355 (61.8%) had no preexisting conditions. A higher proportion of children who met nirsevimab eligibility criteria required invasive mechanical ventilation vs children with other identified RSV risk conditions, those with other preexisting health conditions, or those with no preexisting health conditions (40.4% [19 of 47] vs 17.1% [13 of 76], 10.4% [10 of 96], 5.9% [21 of 355]; P for trend <.001). Conclusions and Relevance: Among children admitted to the PICU for severe RSV, 8.2% met the nirsevimab eligibility criteria and 13.2% had other identified RSV risk conditions. These findings might help inform future deliberations regarding second RSV season recommendations for nirsevimab eligibility in the US.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the knowledge gap regarding preexisting health conditions as risk factors for RSV-related respiratory failure in children during their second RSV season, as RSV hospitalization rates are highest among children younger than 2 years.
Design and population
This cohort study analyzed US children aged 8 to 23 months admitted to the pediatric intensive care unit (PICU) in 30 pediatric hospitals during the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 RSV seasons.
Core methods
The study classified patients into four groups based on nirsevimab eligibility status and preexisting health conditions, and compared their demographic and clinical characteristics using the US criteria for second-season nirsevimab eligibility as recommended in August 2023.
Main results
A total of 574 children were included, with 47 (8.2%) meeting nirsevimab eligibility criteria, 76 (13.2%) having other identified RSV risk conditions, 96 (16.7%) having other preexisting conditions, and 355 (61.8%) having no preexisting conditions. A higher proportion of children who met nirsevimab eligibility criteria required invasive mechanical ventilation compared to other groups (40.4% vs 17.1%, 10.4%, and 5.9%, respectively; P for trend <.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that children meeting nirsevimab eligibility criteria are at a higher risk of requiring invasive mechanical ventilation for severe RSV, which may inform future deliberations on eligibility criteria for nirsevimab in the US.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results could help refine the understanding of risk factors for severe RSV in high-risk children, potentially guiding targeted interventions and improving clinical decision-making for nirsevimab use.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a specific age group and geographic region, and the evidence is based on US criteria for nirsevimab eligibility. The confidence in these findings is moderate due to the potential for regional variability in RSV severity and treatment practices.

呼吸道合胞病毒的研究趋势:十年文献计量分析和双重预防策略的转化进化

Research Trends in Respiratory Syncytial Virus: A Ten-Year Bibliometric Analysis and the Translational Evolution of Dual Strategies for Prevention
作者： Runze WANG, Zhina Nie, Changwen Bie, Xianhong Yang, Xiaodong Bie, Mijia Lu, Yueqing Chen
摘要中文翻译背景:自 1956 年发现以来,呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 一直是易感人群急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因。尽管进行了半个多世纪的尝试,但直到最近基于预输 F 蛋白的 疫苗 和长效单克隆抗体的批准,有效的预防策略才得以实现。充分了解基于预灌F蛋白的疫苗和作用单克隆抗体如何改变RSV研究,有助于我们看到RSV预防之路,并帮助我们指导未来的预防步骤。方法:我们对 2014 年至 2025 年 Web of Science 核心合集中 RSV 相关出版物进行了分析。该分析结合了引文分析、协作网络、关键词 进化 和共引映射。与此同时,对 ClinicalTrials.gov 上列出的 RSV 单克隆抗体和 疫苗 的试验进行了检查,时间跨度重点关注在里程碑之后开始或完成的研究。这种综合方法将研究趋势与 RSV 预防的进展联系起来。结果:该研究中有 2,670 篇出版物,我们发现人们对 RSV 进行了更多研究,尤其是在 2019 年之后。在密集的国际合作网络的支持下,美国和中国成为最有影响力的贡献者。关键词和同被引分析表明,从病毒学和疾病负担研究转向以预防为重点的主题。以预防为重点的主题包括输注前 F 抗原设计、单克隆抗体、疫苗接种 和住院结果。临床试验分析表明,长效单克隆抗体(尤其是 尼塞韦单抗)自开发以来一直引领婴儿 RSV 预防,而重组预融合 F 蛋白 疫苗 占 疫苗 试验的大部分,主要由主要药物开发商赞助。mRNA、减毒活病毒 媒介 和病毒样颗粒平台虽然没有广泛使用,但对于产生新方法仍然具有重要的战略意义。结论:RSV预防已进入一个新阶段,其特点是婴儿长效单克隆抗体和老年人和孕妇有效的疫苗的融合。文献计量趋势与临床试验格局的整合凸显了从探索性研究到以实施为导向的预防策略的转变。未来的努力必须关注 RSV 负担仍然最高的低收入和中等收入国家。

自 1956 年发现以来,呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 一直是易感人群急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因。
设计与对象
该分析结合了引文分析、协作网络、关键词 进化 和共引映射。
核心方法
我们对 2014 年至 2025 年 Web of Science 核心合集中与 RSV 相关的出版物进行了分析。
主要结果
该研究涉及 2,670 篇出版物,我们发现人们对 RSV 进行了更多研究,尤其是在 2019 年之后。
解释与创新
RSV预防已进入一个新阶段,其特点是针对婴儿的长效单克隆抗体和针对老年人和孕妇的有效疫苗的融合。

Research Trends in Respiratory Syncytial Virus: A Ten-Year Bibliometric Analysis and the Translational Evolution of Dual Strategies for Prevention

Authors: Runze WANG, Zhina Nie, Changwen Bie, Xianhong Yang, Xiaodong Bie, Mijia Lu, Yueqing Chen
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) has always been a leading cause of acute lower respiratory infections in vulnerable age groups since its identification in 1956. Despite more than half a century of attempts, the effective preventive strategies only arrived recently with the approval of the prefusion F protein–based vaccines and the long-acting monoclonal antibodies. A full understanding of how RSV research has changed with the prefusion F protein–based vaccines and the acting monoclonal antibodies helps us see the road of RSV prevention and helps us guide the future prevention steps. Methods: We conducted an analysis of RSV related publications, in the Web of Science Core Collection from 2014 to 2025. The analysis combined citation analysis, collaboration networks, keyword evolution and co citation mapping. In parallel, trials of the RSV monoclonal antibodies and the vaccines that are listed on ClinicalTrials.gov were examined, time span focusing on the studies that started or finished after the milestones. The combined approach linked the research trends to the progress, in the RSV prevention. Results: There were 2,670 publications that were part of the study and what we found out is that people are doing more research on RSV especially after 2019. The United States and China emerged as the most influential contributors, supported by dense international collaboration networks. Keyword and co-citation analyses showed a shift, from virology and disease burden studies to prevention-focused themes. Prevention-focused themes include prefusion F antigen design, monoclonal antibodies, vaccination and hospitalization outcomes. Clinical trial analysis showed that long-acting monoclonal antibodies(especially nirsevimab) have led infant RSV prophylaxis since their development, while recombinant prefusion F protein vaccines make up the majority of vaccine trials, sponsored primarily by major pharmaceutical developers. mRNA, live-attenuated viral vector, and virus-like particle platforms, although not widely used, remain strategically important for generating new approaches. Conclusions: RSV prevention has entered a novel phase which characterized by the convergence of long-acting monoclonal antibodies for infants and effective vaccines for older adults and pregnant women. The integration of bibliometric trends with clinical trial landscapes highlights a transition from exploratory research to implementation-oriented prevention strategies. Future efforts must pay attention to low- and middle-income countries where RSV burden stays highest.

Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) has always been a leading cause of acute lower respiratory infections in vulnerable age groups since its identification in 1956.
Design and population
The analysis combined citation analysis, collaboration networks, keyword evolution and co citation mapping.
Core methods
We conducted an analysis of RSV related publications, in the Web of Science Core Collection from 2014 to 2025.
Main results
There were 2,670 publications that were part of the study and what we found out is that people are doing more research on RSV especially after 2019.
Interpretation and novelty
RSV prevention has entered a novel phase which characterized by the convergence of long-acting monoclonal antibodies for infants and effective vaccines for older adults and pregnant women.
Scientific and public-health significance
The integration of bibliometric trends with clinical trial landscapes highlights a transition from exploratory research to implementation-oriented prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Future efforts must pay attention to low- and middle-income countries where RSV burden stays highest.

怀孕期间通用 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗接种 对布宜诺斯艾利斯婴儿住院治疗的真实影响和有效性:连续两个季节(2024-2025)的多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究

Real-world impact and effectiveness of universal respiratory syncytial virus vaccination during pregnancy on infant hospitalisations in Buenos Aires: a multicentre retrospective cohort study across two consecutive seasons (2024–2025)
作者： Julia Dvorkin, Daniela Laura Parada, Josefina Lorena Razzini, María Sol Crespi, Gonzalo Germán Guiñazú, Rosario Merlino, Juliana Palau, Micaela Pichinenda, Anastasia Regalado, Valeria Torre
摘要中文翻译背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是小婴儿严重急性下呼吸道感染 (ALRTI) 的主要原因。阿根廷于 2024 年实施了季节性通用 RSVpreF 孕产妇 疫苗接种 (RSVpreF-MV),并于 2025 年延长了 疫苗接种 窗口。全国覆盖率于 2024 年达到 67.8%,2025 年达到 74.0%。我们连续两个季节评估了该策略的实际影响和有效性 (VE)。方法 我们在布宜诺斯艾利斯的四家三级儿科医院(2018-2025)进行了一项多中心、以医院为基础的回顾性 监测 研究。通过将观察到的 ≤6 个月婴儿 RSV 住院率与实施前年份(2018 年、2019 年和 2023 年)的预期数字进行比较,评估了每个 疫苗接种 季节的人口水平影响。使用巢式测试阴性病例对照设计对因 ALRTI 住院且其母亲符合 RSVpreF-MV 条件的婴儿进行 VE 估计。研究结果 估计 2024 年和 2025 年 6 个月以下婴儿中避免了 130.2 例(95% CI 109.4–151.6)RSV 相关住院治疗,2025 年避免了 263.8 例(95% CI 238.9–288.9)例,相对减少了 24.8 个百分点(95% CI 23.5–26.3)。2025 年(59.7%,95% CI 57.3-61.9)与 2024 年(34.9%,95% CI 31.0-38.4)相比,主要是由于 0-3 个月婴儿疾病负担下降。2025 年,为预防 1 次住院而需要进行免疫接种的人数为 36.4 人(95% CI 30.7-54.9)。在 2024-2025 年季节的汇总分析中,≤6 个月婴儿的 RSV 相关住院调整后 VE 为 73.7%(95% CI 58.3-83.6),≤ 3 个月婴儿为 78.5%(95% CI 60.9-88.5)。在 ≤ 6 个月的婴儿中,与 RSV 相关的 PICU 入院相比,VE 为 75.3% (95% CI 39.1–90.4)。解读 2025 年,疫苗接种 窗口期延长了四个月,人口层面的影响更大,保护范围延伸到生命前 6 个月之后。资助 Thrasher 研究基金(早期职业奖#02398)和盖茨基金会(INV-03673107)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是小婴儿严重急性下呼吸道感染 (ALRTI) 的主要原因。
设计与对象
阿根廷于 2024 年实施季节性通用 RSVpreF 孕产妇 疫苗接种 (RSVpreF-MV),并于 2025 年延长了 疫苗接种 窗口。
核心方法
我们在布宜诺斯艾利斯的四家三级儿科医院(2018-2025)进行了一项多中心、以医院为基础的回顾性 监测 研究。
主要结果
估计 2024 年和 2025 年 6 个月以下婴儿中分别有 130.2 例(95% CI 109.4–151.6)和 263.8 例(95% CI 238.9–288.9)避免了与 RSV 相关的住院治疗,2025 年相对减少幅度为 24.8 个百分点(95% CI 23.5–26.3) (59.7%,95% CI 57.3-61.9)与 2024 年(34.9%,95% CI 31.0-38.4)相比,主要是由于 0-3 个月婴儿疾病负担下降。
解释与创新
2025 年,疫苗接种 窗口期延长了四个月,人口层面的影响更大,保护范围延伸到生命的前 6 个月之后。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,延长 疫苗接种 窗口可以增强 RSVpreF-MV 对人群的影响,有可能减少出生后 6 个月后婴儿的住院率。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性 监测 研究,可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力。

Real-world impact and effectiveness of universal respiratory syncytial virus vaccination during pregnancy on infant hospitalisations in Buenos Aires: a multicentre retrospective cohort study across two consecutive seasons (2024–2025)

Authors: Julia Dvorkin, Daniela Laura Parada, Josefina Lorena Razzini, María Sol Crespi, Gonzalo Germán Guiñazú, Rosario Merlino, Juliana Palau, Micaela Pichinenda, Anastasia Regalado, Valeria Torre
Original abstract (English)
Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of severe acute lower respiratory tract infection (ALRTI) in young infants. Argentina implemented seasonal universal RSVpreF maternal vaccination (RSVpreF-MV) in 2024, extending the vaccination window in 2025. Nationwide coverage reached 67.8% in 2024 and 74.0% in 2025. We evaluated the real-world impact and effectiveness (VE) of this strategy over two consecutive seasons. Methods We conducted a multicentre, hospital-based, retrospective surveillance study in four tertiary paediatric hospitals in Buenos Aires (2018–2025). Population-level impact was assessed for each vaccination season by comparing observed RSV-hospitalisations among infants ≤6 months with expected numbers from pre-implementation years (2018, 2019, and 2023). VE was estimated using a nested test-negative case–control design among infants hospitalised with ALRTI whose mothers were eligible for RSVpreF-MV. Findings An estimated 130.2 (95% CI 109.4–151.6) RSV-related hospitalisations were averted in 2024 and 263.8 (95% CI 238.9–288.9) in 2025 among infants aged ≤6 months, a 24.8 percentage-point (95% CI 23.5–26.3) greater relative reduction in 2025 (59.7%, 95% CI 57.3–61.9) compared with 2024 (34.9%, 95% CI 31.0–38.4), driven primarily by a decline in disease burden among infants aged 0–3 months. The number needed to immunise to prevent one hospitalisation in 2025 was 36.4 (95% CI 30.7–54.9). In pooled analyses of the 2024–2025 seasons, adjusted-VE against RSV-related hospitalisation was 73.7% (95% CI 58.3–83.6) for infants aged ≤6 months and 78.5% (95% CI 60.9–88.5) for those ≤3 months. Among infants ≤6 months, VE was 75.3% (95% CI 39.1–90.4) against RSV-related PICU admission. Interpretation During 2025, when the vaccination window was extended by four months, population-level impact was greater, with protection extending beyond the first 6 months of life. Funding Thrasher Research Fund (Early Career Award #02398) and the Gates Foundation (INV-03673107).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of severe acute lower respiratory tract infection (ALRTI) in young infants.
Design and population
Argentina implemented seasonal universal RSVpreF maternal vaccination (RSVpreF-MV) in 2024, extending the vaccination window in 2025.
Core methods
We conducted a multicentre, hospital-based, retrospective surveillance study in four tertiary paediatric hospitals in Buenos Aires (2018–2025).
Main results
An estimated 130.2 (95% CI 109.4–151.6) RSV-related hospitalisations were averted in 2024 and 263.8 (95% CI 238.9–288.9) in 2025 among infants aged ≤6 months, a 24.8 percentage-point (95% CI 23.5–26.3) greater relative reduction in 2025 (59.7%, 95% CI 57.3–61.9) compared with 2024 (34.9%, 95% CI 31.0–38.4), driven primarily by a decline in disease burden among infants aged 0–3 months.
Interpretation and novelty
During 2025, when the vaccination window was extended by four months, population-level impact was greater, with protection extending beyond the first 6 months of life.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that extending the vaccination window could enhance the population-level impact of RSVpreF-MV, potentially reducing hospitalisations in infants beyond the first 6 months of life.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective surveillance study, which may introduce biases and limit the ability to establish causality.

中低收入国家五岁以下儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 的国家特定疾病负担:模型研究

Country-specific disease burden of respiratory syncytial virus in children younger than five years in low- and middle-income countrie: a modelling study
作者： You Li, Shaolong Ren, Shuyu Deng, Yihua Xu, Erin Grace Sparrow, Daniel R. Feikin, Ignacio Esteban, Andrew David Clark, Virginia E. Pitzer, Harish Nair
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决低收入和中等收入国家缺乏国家一级 RSV 负担估计的问题,特别是在住院和死亡方面。需要进行这项研究,以更好地了解该疾病对这些地区五岁以下儿童的影响。
设计与对象
在 135 个低收入和中等收入国家进行了一项模型研究,以估计 5 岁以下儿童中与 RSV 相关的急性下呼吸道感染 (ALRI) 发病率、住院率和 发病率 率。
核心方法
该研究使用建模技术来估计特定国家和特定年龄的 RSV 相关 发病率、入院率和 死亡率 率,重点关注低收入和中等收入国家的 5 岁以下儿童。
主要结果
RSV-associated ALRI incidence ranged from 38.9 to 80.5 per 1,000 person-years, with the highest rates in southern Africa. Hospital admission rates ranged from 0.8 to 23.2 per 1,000 person-years, and mortality rates ranged from 0.5 to 52.9 and from 1.1 to 120.9 per 100,000 person-years, respectively, with the highest rates in sub-Saharan Africa.
解释与创新
研究结果提供了可比较的估计值,有助于填补中低收入国家 RSV 负担的证据空白,为区域差异和潜在的免疫优先事项提供新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些估计可以为 RSV 免疫规划的公平优先顺序提供信息,并改善低收入和中等收入国家的 监测 和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于建模,这可能会带来不确定性,并且证据仅限于抽象级别的报告,导致置信度评估适中。

Country-specific disease burden of respiratory syncytial virus in children younger than five years in low- and middle-income countrie: a modelling study

Authors: You Li, Shaolong Ren, Shuyu Deng, Yihua Xu, Erin Grace Sparrow, Daniel R. Feikin, Ignacio Esteban, Andrew David Clark, Virginia E. Pitzer, Harish Nair
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Country-level estimates of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) burden are scarce in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), particularly for hospital admissions and deaths. We modelled country- and age-specific RSV-associated acute lower respiratory infection (ALRI) incidence and hospital admission rates, and RSV-attributable and RSV-associated all-cause mortality rates, among children under five across 135 LMICs. RSV-associated ALRI incidence ranged from 38.9 to 80.5 per 1,000 person-years and was highest in southern Africa (Botswana, South Africa and Eswatini). RSV-associated ALRI hospital admission rates ranged from 0.8 to 23.2 per 1,000 person-years and were highest in the Democratic People’s Republic of Korea, Tajikistan and Ukraine. RSV-attributable and RSV-associated mortality rates ranged from 0.5 to 52.9 and from 1.1 to 120.9 per 100,000 person-years, respectively, with the highest rates in sub-Saharan Africa, including Nigeria, Somalia and Chad. These comparable estimates help fill country-level evidence gaps and can inform equitable prioritisation of RSV immunisation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of country-level estimates for RSV burden in low- and middle-income countries, particularly regarding hospital admissions and deaths. The research was needed to better understand the disease's impact on children under five in these regions.
Design and population
A modelling study was conducted across 135 low- and middle-income countries to estimate RSV-associated acute lower respiratory infection (ALRI) incidence, hospital admission rates, and mortality rates among children under five years of age.
Core methods
The study used modelling techniques to estimate country- and age-specific RSV-related incidence, hospital admission rates, and mortality rates, focusing on children under five in low- and middle-income countries.
Main results
RSV-associated ALRI incidence ranged from 38.9 to 80.5 per 1,000 person-years, with the highest rates in southern Africa. Hospital admission rates ranged from 0.8 to 23.2 per 1,000 person-years, and mortality rates ranged from 0.5 to 52.9 and from 1.1 to 120.9 per 100,000 person-years, respectively, with the highest rates in sub-Saharan Africa.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide comparable estimates that help fill evidence gaps for RSV burden in LMICs, offering new insights into regional variations and potential priorities for immunisation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These estimates can inform equitable prioritisation of RSV immunisation programs and improve surveillance and resource allocation in low- and middle-income countries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on modelling, which may introduce uncertainties, and the evidence is limited to abstract-level reporting, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

西非儿童发热性疾病相关病原体:系统评价

Pathogens associated with childhood febrile illness in West Africa: a systematic review
作者： Flavia Kaduni Bawa, Mamadu Baldeh, Jethro Adam Herberg, Rakhitha Katangodage, Effua Usuf, Samuel Duodu, Aubrey J. Cunnington, Martin Chamai, Gordon A. Awandare, Francis Dzabeng
摘要中文翻译背景 在非洲就医的儿童中,高达 80% 患有急性发热性疾病。有关当地传播的发烧病原体的流行病学数据对于临床管理和公共卫生干预至关重要。这项系统评价旨在了解西非儿童发热性疾病的病因。方法 我们纳入了医疗机构和社区研究,采用横断面、病例对照和回顾性横断面设计,使用实验室方法检测引起 1 个月至 15 岁儿童发烧的病原体。通过 OVID、Scopus、ScienceDirect、Connecting Repositories (CoRE)、Directory of Open Access Journals (DOAJ)、African Journals Online (AJOL) 和世界卫生组织 (WHO) Africa Index Medicus 在 Medline 和 Embase 上进行了文献检索,检索 2013 年至 2023 年发表的研究。数据是从常规临床调查和特定研究实验室测试中检测到的病原体中提取的。使用根据病例对照和横断面研究清单开发的综合 CASP 清单进行偏倚风险评估。结果 我们纳入了来自 11 个西非国家的 84 项研究。42% 的研究调查了血液中的细菌病原体,24% 的研究调查了呼吸道样本中的细菌病原体,19% 的研究调查了尿液、粪便、脑脊液 (CSF) 和耳拭子中的细菌病原体,最终检测到了 25 种不同的细菌。其中最常见的是肠道沙门氏菌、肺炎链球菌、金黄色葡萄球菌、流感嗜血杆菌和大肠杆菌。检测到十七种不同的病毒。登革热病毒、基孔肯雅病毒和西尼罗河病毒主要是通过血清学研究检测到的。通常从呼吸道样本中分离出流感病毒、鼻病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、副流感病毒、人类偏肺病毒、冠状病毒、腺病毒和肠道病毒。恶性疟原虫是检测到的 5 种不同寄生虫/真菌物种中最丰富的寄生虫。只有 8% 的研究对多种病原体进行了全面调查(同一参与者同时测试 ≥ 3 种病原体类别)。结论 这项研究发现诊断检测主要以血液为基础,对胃肠道、人畜共患和新出现的病原体的研究有限。这些发现揭示了发热病原体检测的现有证据基础中的重要差距,并强调了未来具有更广泛诊断范围的初级研究将提供更完整的流行病学数据的领域。该系统评价已在国际前瞻性系统评价注册库(PROSPERO)中注册,注册号:CRD 42023466517。临床试验注册 不适用
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解西非儿童发热性疾病的病因,因为在非洲就医的儿童中有高达 80% 患有急性发热性疾病。
设计与对象
这项系统评价包括医疗机构和社区研究,采用横断面、病例对照和回顾性横断面设计,重点关注 11 个西非国家 1 个月至 15 岁的儿童。
核心方法
该审查使用综合 CASP 清单进行偏倚风险评估,包括使用实验室方法检测引起儿童发烧的病原体的研究,并从常规临床调查和特定研究的实验室测试中提取检测到的病原体的数据。
主要结果
纳入了 84 项研究,通常从呼吸道样本中分离出 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感病毒、鼻病毒、副流感病毒、人类偏肺病毒、冠状病毒、腺病毒和肠道病毒。
解释与创新
作者解释说,诊断测试主要以血液为基础,对胃肠道、人畜共患和新出现的病原体的研究有限,这强调了未来研究需要更广泛的诊断范围。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果揭示了发热病原体检测现有证据基础的重要差距,并强调需要更全面的诊断方法来改善临床管理和公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
只有 8% 的研究对 ≥ 3 种病原体进行了全面调查,而且证据受到以血液检测为主的限制,可能无法捕获所有相关病原体。证据强度中等。

Pathogens associated with childhood febrile illness in West Africa: a systematic review

Authors: Flavia Kaduni Bawa, Mamadu Baldeh, Jethro Adam Herberg, Rakhitha Katangodage, Effua Usuf, Samuel Duodu, Aubrey J. Cunnington, Martin Chamai, Gordon A. Awandare, Francis Dzabeng
Original abstract (English)
Background Up to 80% of children seeking medical attention in Africa present with acute febrile illness. Epidemiological data on the locally circulating pathogens causing fever are essential to inform clinical management and for public health interventions. This systematic review sought to understand the aetiologies of febrile illness among children in West Africa. Methods We included healthcare facility and community-based studies with cross-sectional, case-control, and retrospective cross-sectional design which used laboratory methods to detect pathogens causing fever in children aged 1 month to 15 years. A literature search was conducted in Medline and Embase via OVID, Scopus, ScienceDirect, Connecting Repositories (CoRE), Directory of Open Access Journals (DOAJ), African Journals Online (AJOL) and World Health Organisation (WHO) Africa Index Medicus, for studies published from 2013 to 2023. Data was extracted on pathogens detected from routine clinical investigations and study-specific laboratory testing. A risk of bias assessment was conducted using a consolidated CASP checklist developed from the case-control and cross-sectional study checklists. Results We included 84 studies from 11 West African countries. 42% of studies investigated bacterial pathogens in blood, 24% in respiratory samples and 19% in urine, stool, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) and ear swabs combined, resulting in the detection of 25 different bacterial species. The most common of these were Salmonella enterica, Streptococcus pneumoniae, Staphylococcus aureus, Haemophilus influenzae , and Escherichia coli . Seventeen different viruses were detected. Dengue virus, chikungunya virus and West Nile virus were mostly detected from serological studies. Influenza viruses, rhinoviruses, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), parainfluenza viruses, human metapneumovirus, coronaviruses, adenoviruses, and enteroviruses were commonly isolated from respiratory samples. Plasmodium falciparum was the most abundant parasite of the 5 different parasites/fungal species detected. Only 8% of studies performed comprehensive investigations (concurrent testing across ≥ 3 pathogen classes in the same participant) for a wide range of pathogens. Conclusion This study found that diagnostic testing was predominantly blood-based, with limited investigation of gastrointestinal, zoonotic, and emerging pathogens. These findings reveal important gaps in the existing evidence base for febrile pathogen detection and highlight areas where future primary research with a broader diagnostic scope would provide more complete epidemiological data. This systematic review has been registered in the International Prospective Register of Systematic Reviews (PROSPERO) under the registration number: CRD 42023466517. Clinical trials registration Not applicable
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the aetiologies of febrile illness among children in West Africa, as up to 80% of children seeking medical attention in Africa present with acute febrile illness.
Design and population
This systematic review included healthcare facility and community-based studies with cross-sectional, case-control, and retrospective cross-sectional designs, focusing on children aged 1 month to 15 years in 11 West African countries.
Core methods
The review used a consolidated CASP checklist for risk of bias assessment and included studies that used laboratory methods to detect pathogens causing fever in children, with data extraction on pathogens detected from routine clinical investigations and study-specific laboratory testing.
Main results
Eighty-four studies were included, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza viruses, rhinoviruses, parainfluenza viruses, human metapneumovirus, coronaviruses, adenoviruses, and enteroviruses were commonly isolated from respiratory samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that diagnostic testing was predominantly blood-based, with limited investigation of gastrointestinal, zoonotic, and emerging pathogens, highlighting the need for broader diagnostic scope in future studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings reveal important gaps in the existing evidence base for febrile pathogen detection and emphasize the need for more comprehensive diagnostic approaches to improve clinical management and public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
Only 8% of studies performed comprehensive investigations across ≥ 3 pathogen classes, and the evidence is limited by the predominance of blood-based testing, which may not capture all relevant pathogens. The evidence strength is moderate.

2019-2025年中国福建省5个地区住院儿童急性呼吸道感染呼吸道合胞病毒流行病学特征及传播模式变化

Epidemiological characteristics and changing transmission patterns of respiratory syncytial virus among hospitalized children with acute respiratory tract infections across five regions of Fujian Province, China, 2019–2025
作者： Jinxun Wu, Lin Cai, Huizhong Liu, Xiaoman Gao, Fucun Zhu, Qianqian Chen, Jie Lin, Xiuhua Liu, Ting Liu, Zijian Wang
摘要中文翻译这项多中心研究(2019-2025)全面描述了四个大流行阶段的RSV动态以及人鼻病毒(HRV)、肺炎支原体(MP)和人腺病毒(HAdV)的混合感染,旨在确定大流行后的变化。方法 纳入福建省108701名住院儿童,每人提供一份呼吸道标本进行多重PCR检测。结果 RSV 总体阳性率为 12.37% (13,241/107,015)。重要的是,这项研究提供了来自中国亚热带地区的长期证据,表明非营利性PI抑制了RSV,并且由此产生的季节性位移(冬季高峰被夏季秋季优势取代)已经持续了大约三个季节,并且尚未恢复。基金资助 本研究得到国家重点研发计划项目(2024YFC2311401)的资助;福州市2022年强化专科临床专科建设研究项目(2024-YJ-ZK14);广东省普通高等学校工程技术研究(开发)中心(批准号 伦理声明 伦理批准并同意参与 这项回顾性研究基于从现有病历中获得的去识别化临床信息,没有出于研究目的进行额外的干预或程序。摘要 背景 COVID-19 非药物干预(NPI)改变了破坏性呼吸道病原体 传播,但中期流行病学 进化 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 NPI 放松后的任何共同检测模式仍不清楚。我们比较了四个阶段的 RSV 阳性、季节性、年龄分布和共同检测模式(RSV + HRV、RSV + MP、RSV + HAdV):大流行前(第 1 阶段)、严格...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 COVID-19 大流行期间放松非药物干预措施 (NPI) 后,呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的中期流行病学 进化 及其与其他病原体的共同检测模式。
设计与对象
2019 年至 2025 年在中国福建省五个地区进行的一项多中心研究,纳入了 108,701 名患有急性呼吸道感染 (ARTI) 的住院儿童,每人提供一份呼吸道样本进行分析。
核心方法
该研究对呼吸道标本进行多重 PCR 检测,以评估四个大流行阶段的 RSV 阳性率、季节性、年龄分布以及与人鼻病毒 (HRV)、肺炎支原体 (MP) 和人腺病毒 (HAdV) 的共同检测模式。
主要结果
RSV阳性率在严格的NPI阶段(9.41%)较疫情前阶段(17.12%)有所下降,在政策过渡阶段达到最低水平(5.72%),并在疫情后阶段有所反弹(13.62%)。传统的冬季高峰转移到秋季和夏季,同时感染的人群转向3-6岁儿童。
解释与创新
作者解释说,观察到的 RSV 季节性和年龄分布的变化可能反映了年龄较大的学龄前儿童的免疫债务,尽管这一假设需要血清学和出生 队列研究 数据来证实。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要持续采取 监测 和年龄分层预防策略,以解决大流行后背景下重新出现的 RSV 及其混合感染的负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和缺乏对免疫力的直接测量而受到限制,这阻碍了关于驱动 RSV 流行病学 观察到的变化的机制的明确结论。

Epidemiological characteristics and changing transmission patterns of respiratory syncytial virus among hospitalized children with acute respiratory tract infections across five regions of Fujian Province, China, 2019–2025

Authors: Jinxun Wu, Lin Cai, Huizhong Liu, Xiaoman Gao, Fucun Zhu, Qianqian Chen, Jie Lin, Xiuhua Liu, Ting Liu, Zijian Wang
Original abstract (English)
This multicenter study (2019–2025) comprehensively characterized RSV dynamics and mixed infections with Human rhinovirus (HRV), Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP), and human adenovirus (HAdV) across four pandemic phases, aiming to identify post-pandemic changes. Methods A total of 108,701 hospitalized children in Fujian were enrolled, each contributing one respiratory specimen for multiplex PCR testing. Results Overall RSV positivity was 12.37% (13,241/107,015). Importantly, this study provides long-term evidence from subtropical China that NPIs suppressed RSV and that the resulting seasonal displacement—with the winter peak replaced by summer–autumn predominance—has persisted for approximately three seasons and has not yet reverted. Funding This study was supported by the National Key Research and Development Program of China (2024YFC2311401); Fuzhou Municipal Clinical Specialty Construction Research Project for Strengthening Specialized Departments in 2022 (2024-YJ-ZK14); Engineering Technology Research (Development) Center of Ordinary Colleges and Universities in Guangdong Province (Grant No. Ethics declarations Ethics approval and consent to participate This retrospective study was based on de-identified clinical information obtained from existing medical records, with no additional intervention or procedure performed for research purposes. Abstract Background COVID-19 non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) altered disrupted respiratory pathogen transmission, but the medium-term epidemiological evolution of Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and any co-detection patterns after NPI relaxation remains unclear. We compared RSV positivity, seasonality, age distribution, and co-detection patterns (RSV + HRV, RSV + MP, RSV+HAdV) across four phases: pre-pandemic (Phase 1), strict...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the medium-term epidemiological evolution of Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and its co-detection patterns with other pathogens following the relaxation of non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
A multicenter study conducted across five regions of Fujian Province, China, from 2019 to 2025, enrolled 108,701 hospitalized children with acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs), each contributing one respiratory specimen for analysis.
Core methods
The study used multiplex PCR testing on respiratory specimens to assess RSV positivity, seasonality, age distribution, and co-detection patterns with Human rhinovirus (HRV), Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP), and human adenovirus (HAdV) across four pandemic phases.
Main results
RSV positivity decreased during the strict NPI phase (9.41%) compared with the pre-pandemic phase (17.12%), reached its lowest level during the policy transition phase (5.72%), and rebounded in the post-pandemic phase (13.62%). The traditional winter peak shifted to autumn and summer, and co-infections shifted toward 3–6-year-olds.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the observed changes in RSV seasonality and age distribution may reflect an immunity debt in older preschool children, although this hypothesis requires confirmation with serological and birth-cohort data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for ongoing surveillance and age-stratified prevention strategies to address the re-emerging burden of RSV and its mixed infections in the post-pandemic context.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and lack of direct measurement of immunity, which prevents definitive conclusions about the mechanisms driving the observed changes in RSV epidemiology.
