针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 融合蛋白的乳腺衍生肽的计算设计和优先级排序

Computational design and prioritization of mastoparan-derived peptides against respiratory syncytial virus fusion protein
作者： Nasrin Aliabadi, Aida Behzadi, Nahid Heydari Marandi, Marzieh Hosseini, Khadijeh Ahmadi, Mehdi Kalani, Marzieh Jamalidoust
摘要中文翻译在这项研究中,采用计算肽工程工作流程来设计针对 RSV 结构蛋白的乳腺衍生肽。这些发现为靶向预融合稳定和融合级联的潜在干扰建立了令人信服的、可测试的结构假设,为后续实验验证提供了清晰的结构路线图,证明了与其他病毒结构蛋白(G和M)相比,对RSV F的优先预测结合倾向。总体而言,本研究提出了一种迭代的计算机肽工程方法,集成了序列设计、结构预测、分子对接和分子动力学模拟,以优先考虑具有潜在 抗病毒药物 抗 RSV 活性的候选肽。资金 本研究未收到资金。伦理批准和同意参与研究设计和方案得到了伊朗设拉子医学科学大学医学伦理和健康哲学系的伦理批准,IR.SUMS.REC.1404.261。所有方法均按照相关指南和规定进行。摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是全世界下呼吸道感染的主要原因,特别是在婴儿、老年人和免疫功能低下的个体中。基于肽的 抗病毒药物 的开发代表了一种有前途的策略,可以补充现有的预防和治疗方法。在 Linux (Ubuntu) 环境中使用自定义 Python 脚本,通过系统点 突变、序列杂交和从头设计规则,在计算机上生成了 60 个乳腺衍生肽变体的库,同时保留了关键的理化特性。根据理化描述进行多参数筛选...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是全世界下呼吸道感染的主要原因,特别是在婴儿、老年人和免疫功能低下的个体中。基于肽的 抗病毒药物 的开发代表了一种有前途的策略,可以补充现有的预防和治疗方法。
设计与对象
采用计算肽工程工作流程来设计针对 RSV 结构蛋白的乳腺衍生肽。在 Linux (Ubuntu) 环境中使用自定义 Python 脚本,通过系统点 突变、序列杂交和从头设计规则,在计算机上生成了 60 个乳腺衍生肽变体的库,同时保留了关键的理化特性。根据理化描述符、结构稳定性和预测的生物活性进行多参数筛选后,选择了 39 名候选者进行进一步分析。
核心方法
使用AlphaFold预测突变肽的三维结构,并使用HADDOCK分子对接评估其与RSV融合蛋白(RSV F)的相互作用。通过 100 ns 分子动力学模拟评估 RSV F-MutantPM2 复合物。
主要结果
该复合物在大约 20 ns 后达到平衡,并在整个模拟过程中保持稳定,平均主链 RMSD 为 0.32 nm。回转半径保持稳定在 3.06 nm,表明结构紧凑。观察到持久的分子间氢键网络,平均 9.18 个氢键。RMSF 分析显示 RSV F 结合界面的灵活性较低(0.18 nm),而 MutantPM2 表现出中等灵活性(0.42 nm),表明稳定结合袋内的适应性肽动力学和持续的复合物关联,并且 MutantPM2 与 F1 子域(残基 390-496)内的融合前抗原位点 IV(残基 422-438)形成稳定的动态相互作用。
解释与创新
PM2 被确定为排名靠前的候选者,随后通过靶向点 突变 进行修饰,生成新型衍生肽。这些发现为靶向预融合稳定和融合级联的潜在干扰建立了令人信服的、可测试的结构假设,为后续实验验证提供了清晰的结构路线图,证明了与其他病毒结构蛋白(G和M)相比,对RSV F的优先预测结合倾向。
科研与公卫意义
本研究提出了一种迭代的计算机肽工程方法,集成了序列设计、结构预测、分子对接和分子动力学模拟,以优先考虑具有潜在 抗病毒药物 抗 RSV 活性的候选肽。这些发现为后续实验验证提供了清晰的结构路线图,并可能为针对 RSV 的新型 抗病毒药物 策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于计算机分析,不包括实验验证。证据基于计算模型和模拟,可能无法完全反映生物学现实。由于依赖未经实验证实的预测计算方法,置信度中等。

Computational design and prioritization of mastoparan-derived peptides against respiratory syncytial virus fusion protein

Authors: Nasrin Aliabadi, Aida Behzadi, Nahid Heydari Marandi, Marzieh Hosseini, Khadijeh Ahmadi, Mehdi Kalani, Marzieh Jamalidoust
Original abstract (English)
In this study, a computational peptide engineering workflow was employed to design mastoparan-derived peptides targeting RSV structural proteins. These findings establish a compelling, testable structural hypothesis for targeted prefusion stabilization and potential interference with the fusion cascade, providing a clear structural roadmap for subsequent experimental validation, that demonstrating a preferential predicted binding propensity toward RSV F over other viral structural proteins (G and M). Overall, this study presents an iterative in silico peptide engineering approach integrating sequence design, structure prediction, molecular docking, and molecular dynamics simulations to prioritize peptide candidates with potential antiviral activity against RSV. Funding No funding was received for this study. Ethics approval and consent to participate The study design and protocols were ethically approved by the Department of Medical Ethics and Philosophy of Health, Shiraz University of Medical Sciences, Iran under IR.SUMS.REC.1404.261. All methods were performed in accordance with the relevant guidelines and regulations. Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of lower respiratory tract infections worldwide, particularly in infants, older adults, and immunocompromised individuals. The development of peptide-based antivirals represents a promising strategy to complement existing preventive and therapeutic approaches. A library of 60 mastoparan-derived peptide variants was generated in silico using custom Python scripts in a Linux (Ubuntu) environment through systematic point mutations, sequence hybridization, and de novo design rules while preserving key physicochemical properties. Following multi-parameter screening based on physicochemical descript...

Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of lower respiratory tract infections worldwide, particularly in infants, older adults, and immunocompromised individuals. The development of peptide-based antivirals represents a promising strategy to complement existing preventive and therapeutic approaches.
Design and population
A computational peptide engineering workflow was employed to design mastoparan-derived peptides targeting RSV structural proteins. A library of 60 mastoparan-derived peptide variants was generated in silico using custom Python scripts in a Linux (Ubuntu) environment through systematic point mutations, sequence hybridization, and de novo design rules while preserving key physicochemical properties. Following multi-parameter screening based on physicochemical descriptors, structural stability, and predicted biological activity, 39 candidates were selected for further analysis.
Core methods
The three-dimensional structure of the mutated peptide was predicted using AlphaFold, and its interaction with the RSV fusion protein (RSV F) was evaluated using HADDOCK molecular docking. The RSV F-MutantPM2 complex was evaluated by a 100 ns Molecular Dynamics simulation.
Main results
The complex reached equilibrium after approximately 20 ns and remained stable throughout the simulation, with a mean backbone RMSD of 0.32 nm. The Radius of Gyration remained stable at 3.06 nm, indicating preserved structural compactness. A persistent intermolecular hydrogen-bonding network was observed, averaging 9.18 hydrogen bonds. RMSF analysis showed low flexibility at the RSV F binding interface (0.18 nm), while MutantPM2 exhibited moderate flexibility (0.42 nm), indicating adaptive peptide dynamics within a stable binding pocket and sustained complex association and MutantPM2 forms stable dynamic interactions with prefusion antigenic Site IV (residues 422–438) within the F1 subdomain (residues 390–496).
Interpretation and novelty
PM2 was identified as a top-ranked candidate and was subsequently modified through a targeted point mutation to generate a novel derivative peptide. These findings establish a compelling, testable structural hypothesis for targeted prefusion stabilization and potential interference with the fusion cascade, providing a clear structural roadmap for subsequent experimental validation, that demonstrating a preferential predicted binding propensity toward RSV F over other viral structural proteins (G and M).
Scientific and public-health significance
This study presents an iterative in silico peptide engineering approach integrating sequence design, structure prediction, molecular docking, and molecular dynamics simulations to prioritize peptide candidates with potential antiviral activity against RSV. The findings provide a clear structural roadmap for subsequent experimental validation and may inform the development of novel antiviral strategies targeting RSV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in silico analysis and does not include experimental validation. The evidence is based on computational models and simulations, which may not fully reflect biological reality. Confidence is moderate due to the reliance on predictive computational methods without experimental confirmation.

呼吸道病毒活动——美国,2025 年 7 月至 2026 年 6 月

Respiratory Virus Activity — United States, July 2025–June 2026
作者： Amber K. Winn, Onika Anglin, Erin L. Anderson, Mila M. Prill, Monica E. Patton, Heidi L. Moline, Michael Melgar, Kevin C. Ma, Clinton R. Paden, Lydia J. Atherton
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒性疾病很常见,可能导致严重后果,包括住院和死亡。
设计与对象
使用来自六个补充公共卫生 监测 系统的数据分析了 2025 年 7 月至 2026 年 6 月期间呼吸道病毒活动的趋势。
核心方法
RSV 测序未发现与婴儿对可用 RSV 单克隆抗体敏感性降低相关的变异替换。
主要结果
Annual RSV-associated hospitalization rates (number of hospitalizations per 100,000 persons) were highest among infants aged <12 months (790.2; 95% CI = 757.9-825.3), followed by children aged 12-23 months (611.9; 95% CI = 586.3-639.8) and adults aged ≥75 years (352.1; 95% CI = 309.9-407.7).
解释与创新
总体和按年龄组划分的年度 RSV 率未超过 2022-2025 年期间观察到的水平。
科研与公卫意义
及时了解推荐的 RSV 和 COVID-19 疫苗接种 可降低患严重疾病和由这些病毒引起的疾病的风险。

提供的证据未报告该研究的局限性或证据强度。

Respiratory Virus Activity — United States, July 2025–June 2026

Authors: Amber K. Winn, Onika Anglin, Erin L. Anderson, Mila M. Prill, Monica E. Patton, Heidi L. Moline, Michael Melgar, Kevin C. Ma, Clinton R. Paden, Lydia J. Atherton
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory viral illnesses are common and can lead to severe outcomes, including hospitalization and death. trends in respiratory virus activity during July 2025-June 2026 were analyzed using data from six complementary public health surveillance systems. Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) activity occurred unusually late in most U.S. SARS-CoV-2 circulated nationally with typical bimodal peaks that occurred in August 2025 and January 2026. Annual RSV-associated hospitalization rates (number of hospitalizations per 100,000 persons) were highest among infants aged <12 months (790.2; 95% CI = 757.9-825.3), followed by children aged 12-23 months (611.9; 95% CI = 586.3-639.8) and adults aged ≥75 years (352.1; 95% CI = 309.9-407.7). Annual RSV rates overall and by age group did not exceed those observed during 2022-2025. COVID-19-associated hospitalization rates were highest among adults aged ≥75 years (361.2), infants aged <6 months (173.3), and adults aged 65-74 years (111.4). Newly established 2025-2026 national pediatric mortality surveillance provisionally identified 34 RSV-associated pediatric deaths from 35 reporting jurisdictions and 31 COVID-19-associated pediatric deaths from 43 reporting jurisdictions. RSV sequencing did not identify variant substitutions associated with reduced susceptibility to the available RSV monoclonal antibodies for infants. Most SARS-CoV-2 lineages were JN.1 descendants, but detections of BA.3.2, a highly divergent SARS-CoV-2 variant, increased. Circulation of other respiratory viruses followed usual historical patterns. Staying current with recommended RSV and COVID-19 vaccinations reduces the risk for severe illness and disease caused by these viruses.

2024-2025年季节德国呼吸道疾病活动的多模型预测

Multi-model forecasting of respiratory disease activity in Germany during the 2024–2025 season
作者： Johannes Bracher, Daniel Wolffram, Rodrigo Amaral Lind, Nils Bardeck, Michael Böhm, Sebastián Contreras, Philipp Dönges, Felix Günther, Rolf Kaiser, Jan van de Kassteele
摘要中文翻译呼吸道疾病在秋季和冬季引起相当大的发病率,是公共卫生监测的重点。在德国,他们受到许多 监测 系统的约束,包括病原体特异性指标和症状指标。在本文中,我们提出了在 2024/25 季节推出的协作多目标和多模型实时预测系统,并讨论了与早期在 COVID-19 大流行期间开展的工作的差异。我们专注于急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 的全科医生咨询和严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 的住院治疗,总共运行了八个模型来生成即时预报和预测。此外,我们还考虑了季节性流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 确诊病例,其中各有一个预测模型。由于所有指标均需进行回顾性修订,因此预测模型与临近预报步骤相结合。对于SARI和ARI指标,我们将可用的模型组合成一个集合,并进行了详细的定量评估。临近预报总体表现出令人信服的表现,尽管对于某些模型来说,圣诞节假期的影响导致一月初出现向上偏差。预测总体上经过良好校准,大多数模型都优于简单的基准模型。当考虑到年龄分层目标时,大多数观察到的与持久性基线的绩效差异具有统计显着性。然而,预测峰值时间和幅度被证明是具有挑战性的,因为许多模型预测曲线过于平坦且周转时间过早(例如,SARI 是在 1 月底而不是 2 月中旬)。组合的集合预测是表现最好的方法之一,但与之前的相关项目不同,它的表现并不总是优于单个模型。最后,我们讨论了在后 COVID-19 时代组织协作预测项目的经验教训。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道疾病在秋季和冬季引起相当大的发病率,是公共卫生监测的重点。
设计与对象
在德国,他们受到许多 监测 系统的约束,包括病原体特异性指标和症状指标。
核心方法
在本文中,我们提出了在 2024/25 季节推出的协作多目标和多模型实时预测系统,并讨论了与早期在 COVID-19 大流行期间开展的工作的差异。
主要结果
临近预报总体表现出令人信服的表现,尽管对于某些模型来说,圣诞节假期的影响导致一月初出现向上偏差。
解释与创新
预测总体上经过良好校准,大多数模型都优于简单的基准模型。
科研与公卫意义
然而,预测峰值时间和幅度被证明是具有挑战性的,因为许多模型预测曲线过于平坦且周转时间过早(例如,SARI 是在 1 月底而不是 2 月中旬)。
局限与证据强度
最后,我们讨论了在后 COVID-19 时代组织协作预测项目的经验教训。

Multi-model forecasting of respiratory disease activity in Germany during the 2024–2025 season

Authors: Johannes Bracher, Daniel Wolffram, Rodrigo Amaral Lind, Nils Bardeck, Michael Böhm, Sebastián Contreras, Philipp Dönges, Felix Günther, Rolf Kaiser, Jan van de Kassteele
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory diseases cause considerable morbidity in autumn and winter and are a priority in public health monitoring. In Germany, they are subject to a number of surveillance systems, including both pathogen-specific and syndromic indicators. In this paper we present a collaborative multi-target and multi-model real-time forecasting system rolled out during the 2024/25 season, and discuss differences to earlier efforts carried out during the COVID-19 pandemic. We focus on general practitioner consultations for acute respiratory infections (ARI) and hospitalizations for severe acute respiratory infections (SARI), where a total of eight models were run to generate nowcasts and forecasts. In addition, we consider confirmed cases of seasonal influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) where one forecasting model each was available. As all indicators were subject to retrospective revisions, forecasting models were combined with a nowcasting step. For the SARI and ARI indicators, we combined the available models into an ensemble and conducted detailed quantitative evaluations. Nowcasts overall showed convincing performance, even though for some models Christmas break effects led to an upward bias in early January. Forecasts were overall well-calibrated and most models outperformed simple benchmark models. When taking into account age-stratified targets, most observed performance differences to a persistence baseline were statistically significant. Anticipating the peak timing and magnitude, however, proved to be challenging, with many models predicting too flat curves with a too early turnaround (e.g., in late January rather than mid-February for SARI). The combined ensemble forecast was among the best-performing approaches, but unlike in previous related projects did not consistently outperform individual models. We conclude by discussing learnings on the organization of collaborative forecasting projects in post-COVID-19 times.

Limitations and evidence strength

根据荷兰的财务索赔代码,为严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 的及时 监测 制定综合征病例定义

Developing a syndromic case definition for timely surveillance of severe acute respiratory infections (SARI) based on financial claim codes in the Netherlands
作者： Floor A Niessen, Irene K Veldhuijzen, Tjarda M. Boere, Mariëtte Hooiveld, Arianne B. van Gageldonk‐Lafeber, Liselotte van Asten, Anne C. Teirlinck, Arianne B van Gageldonk-Lafeber
摘要中文翻译简介:为了改善大流行的防范,针对严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 的可持续且及时的 监测 系统至关重要。我们使用财务索赔代码(DTC 代码)开发并评估了综合症 SARI-监测 的代理。方法:根据 2016-2020 年的数据,我们将 DTC 代码与按 ICD-10 代码(J0-J22、U07)分类的初次出院 诊断 进行比较,作为 SARI 的比较基准,区分儿童和成人人群以及 COVID-19 大流行之前和期间。为了建立基于 DTC 的 SARI 代理,评估了 DTC 代码的个体和组合的准确性指标以及时间趋势。我们回顾性评估了其检测 COVID-19 流行模式的能力及其与现有呼吸 监测 系统结果的关系。结果:对于成年人来说,肺炎和未明确的呼吸道和病毒感染的 DTC 代码可以作为 SARI 的代表。在儿科人群中,使用了声门下喉炎、RSV 毛细支气管炎 和呼吸道感染的代码。虽然基于 DTC 的 SARI 代理的趋势与 ICD-10 编码的 SARI 的趋势相关,但相对于该基准的准确性适中。基于成人 DTC 的 SARI 指标反映了大流行期间 COVID-19 住院治疗的高峰,并与大流行前时期的流感和 RSV 实验室检测结果一致。基于儿科 DTC 的 SARI 代理与 RSV 实验室检测一致。结论:我们为综合症 SARI-监测 建立了基于 DTC 的代理,该代理似乎适合监测 SARI 趋势。然而,在患者层面,ICD-10 编码的 SARI 的准确性似乎中等。应使用实时数据前瞻性评估基于 DTC 的 SARI 代理的准确性及其检测相关流行病信号的能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为了提高大流行的防范能力,针对严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 的可持续且及时的 监测 系统至关重要。
设计与对象
我们使用财务索赔代码(DTC 代码)开发并评估了综合症 SARI-监测 的代理。
核心方法
根据 2016-2020 年的数据,我们将 DTC 代码与按 ICD-10 代码(J0-J22、U07)分类的初次出院 诊断 进行比较,作为 SARI 的比较基准,区分儿童和成人人群以及 COVID-19 大流行之前和期间。
主要结果
在儿科人群中,使用了声门下喉炎、RSV 毛细支气管炎 和呼吸道感染的代码。
解释与创新
基于成人 DTC 的 SARI 指标反映了大流行期间 COVID-19 住院治疗的高峰,并与大流行前时期的流感和 RSV 实验室检测结果一致。
科研与公卫意义
我们为综合症 SARI-监测 建立了基于 DTC 的代理,该代理似乎适合监测 SARI 趋势。
局限与证据强度
应使用实时数据前瞻性评估基于 DTC 的 SARI 代理的准确性及其检测相关流行病信号的能力。

Developing a syndromic case definition for timely surveillance of severe acute respiratory infections (SARI) based on financial claim codes in the Netherlands

Authors: Floor A Niessen, Irene K Veldhuijzen, Tjarda M. Boere, Mariëtte Hooiveld, Arianne B. van Gageldonk‐Lafeber, Liselotte van Asten, Anne C. Teirlinck, Arianne B van Gageldonk-Lafeber
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: To improve pandemic preparedness, a sustainable and timely surveillance system for severe acute respiratory infections (SARI) is crucial. We developed and evaluated a proxy for syndromic SARI-surveillance using financial claim codes (DTC-codes). METHODS: With data from 2016-2020 we compared DTC-codes with primary discharge diagnosis classified by ICD-10 codes (J0-J22, U07) as comparative benchmark for SARI, distinguishing between paediatric and adult populations, and pre- and during COVID-19-pandemic periods. To establish DTC-based proxies for SARI, accuracy metrics of individual and combinations of DTC-codes, and temporal trends were assessed. We retrospectively assessed its ability to detect COVID-19 epidemic patterns and its relationship with outcomes of existing respiratory surveillance systems. RESULTS: For adults, DTC-codes for pneumonia and unspecified respiratory and viral infections served as proxy for SARI. In the paediatric population, codes for laryngitis subglottica, RSV bronchiolitis and respiratory tract infection were used. While trends in DTC-based SARI proxies correlate with trends in ICD-10-coded SARI, the accuracy relative to this benchmark was moderate. The adult DTC-based SARI proxy reflected peaks in COVID-19 hospitalisations during the pandemic and aligned with influenza and RSV laboratory detections in the pre-pandemic period. The paediatric DTC-based SARI proxy aligned with RSV laboratory detections. CONCLUSION: We established DTC-based proxies for syndromic SARI-surveillance that appeared suitable to monitor SARI-trends. However, on the patient level the accuracy for ICD-10-coded SARI appeared moderate. Accuracy of the DTC-based SARI proxy and its ability to detect relevant epidemic signals, should be assessed prospectively with real-time data.

公共 监测 指标对老年人 RSV 和流感住院的增量预测价值

Incremental predictive value of public surveillance indicators for RSV and influenza hospitalizations in older adults
作者： Hong-Wei Zheng, Tinge Zhang, Shao-Mei Lin
摘要中文翻译目的:评估公共 监测 指标是否可以改善年龄 ≥ 65 岁且超出住院史和季节性的成人中流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 相关住院的预测。方法:我们回顾性分析了 2018 年 10 月至 2026 年 7 月期间的美国 FluSurv-NET 和 RSV-NET 住院率。我们将基于近期住院情况和季节性的预测与使用老年人急诊科 (ED) 百分比、全年龄实验室阳性率或国家加权流感样疾病 (wILI) 的预测进行了比较。使用平均绝对误差 (MAE) 差异和 95% 置信区间 (CI) 在相同的预测周上评估准确性。其他比较使用相同的历史观察结果和替代季节性曲线。结果:在 120 个 RSV 预测周中,实验室阳性使 MAE 改善,每周每 100,000 人住院 0.096 例(95% CI 0.039-0.159)。单独的 ED 并没有明显改善预测(-0.059;-0.119 至 0.005),ED 也没有增加实验室阳性率。在 74 流感周,wILI 在主要季节性模型下将 MAE 提高了 0.266 (0.064-0.567)。使用相同的历史观察保留了这些主要发现。RSV 实验室收益在替代季节曲线下也持续存在,而使用更灵活的季节曲线的 wILI 估计值为 0.099(-0.062 至 0.299)。结论:在所检查的季节规格中,所有年龄段的实验室阳性率均改善了老年人 RSV 预测。一周流感 wILI 增量取决于季节性建模方式。ED 百分比没有提供一致的一周增量收益。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估公共 呼吸道合胞病毒 指标是否可以增强对 65 岁以上成人因 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和流感而住院的预测,超出使用住院史和季节性的预测范围。
设计与对象
该研究回顾性分析了 2018 年 10 月至 2026 年 7 月的美国住院率,将基于近期住院率和季节性的预测与纳入急诊科百分比、全年龄实验室阳性率和国家加权流感样疾病 (wILI) 等其他指标的预测进行比较。
核心方法
使用平均绝对误差 (MAE) 差异和 95% 置信区间 (CI) 评估预测的准确性,并使用相同的历史观测值和替代季节性曲线进行额外比较。
主要结果
On 120 RSV forecast weeks, laboratory positivity improved MAE by 0.096 hospitalizations per 100,000 per week (95% CI 0.039-0.159). ED percentages alone did not clearly improve forecasts (-0.059; -0.119 to 0.005), nor did ED added to laboratory positivity. On 74 influenza weeks, wILI improved MAE by 0.266 (0.064-0.567) under the primary seasonal model.
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,所有年龄段的实验室阳性率都改善了不同季节规格下老年人 RSV 的预测,而一周流感 wILI 的增量取决于季节性的建模方式。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,纳入所有年龄段的实验室阳性数据可以提高老年人 RSV 住院预测的准确性,从而有可能改善公共卫生准备和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对现有 监测 数据的依赖可能会限制普遍性,并且由于缺乏前瞻性验证和历史数据解释中的潜在偏差,证据并不充分。

Incremental predictive value of public surveillance indicators for RSV and influenza hospitalizations in older adults

Authors: Hong-Wei Zheng, Tinge Zhang, Shao-Mei Lin
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: To assess whether public surveillance indicators improve forecasts of influenza- and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-associated hospitalizations among adults aged ≥ 65 years beyond hospitalization history and seasonality. METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed U.S. FluSurv-NET and RSV-NET hospitalization rates from October 2018 through July 2026. We compared forecasts based on recent hospitalizations and seasonality with forecasts that also used older-adult emergency department (ED) percentages, all-age laboratory positivity, or national weighted influenza-like illness (wILI). Accuracy was assessed on identical forecast weeks using mean absolute error (MAE) differences and 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Additional comparisons used the same historical observations and alternative seasonal curves. RESULTS: On 120 RSV forecast weeks, laboratory positivity improved MAE by 0.096 hospitalizations per 100,000 per week (95% CI 0.039-0.159). ED alone did not clearly improve forecasts (-0.059; -0.119 to 0.005), nor did ED added to laboratory positivity. On 74 influenza weeks, wILI improved MAE by 0.266 (0.064-0.567) under the primary seasonal model. Using the same historical observations preserved these principal findings. RSV laboratory gains also persisted with alternative seasonal curves, whereas the wILI estimate with a more flexible seasonal curve was 0.099 (-0.062 to 0.299). CONCLUSIONS: All-age laboratory positivity improved older-adult RSV forecasts across the examined seasonal specifications. The one-week influenza wILI increment depended on how seasonality was modeled. ED percentages provided no consistent one-week incremental benefit.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate whether public surveillance indicators could enhance forecasts of hospitalizations due to respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and influenza in adults aged ≥ 65 years, beyond what could be predicted using hospitalization history and seasonality.
Design and population
The study retrospectively analyzed U.S. hospitalization rates from October 2018 through July 2026, comparing forecasts based on recent hospitalizations and seasonality with those incorporating additional indicators such as emergency department percentages, all-age laboratory positivity, and national weighted influenza-like illness (wILI).
Core methods
Accuracy of forecasts was evaluated using mean absolute error (MAE) differences and 95% confidence intervals (CIs), with additional comparisons made using the same historical observations and alternative seasonal curves.
Main results
On 120 RSV forecast weeks, laboratory positivity improved MAE by 0.096 hospitalizations per 100,000 per week (95% CI 0.039-0.159). ED percentages alone did not clearly improve forecasts (-0.059; -0.119 to 0.005), nor did ED added to laboratory positivity. On 74 influenza weeks, wILI improved MAE by 0.266 (0.064-0.567) under the primary seasonal model.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that all-age laboratory positivity improved older-adult RSV forecasts across different seasonal specifications, while the one-week influenza wILI increment depended on how seasonality was modeled.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that incorporating all-age laboratory positivity data could enhance the accuracy of RSV hospitalization forecasts for older adults, potentially improving public health preparedness and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective design and reliance on existing surveillance data may limit generalizability, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of prospective validation and potential biases in historical data interpretation.

居住在不列颠哥伦比亚省偏远地区的婴儿的 RSV 和全因下呼吸道感染负担:基于人群的出生回顾性 队列研究 研究

RSV and all-cause lower respiratory infection burden among infants residing in remote British Columbia: Retrospective population-based birth cohort study
作者： Allison W. Watts, Marina Viñeta Paramo, Taylor R. Jamieson, Jeffrey N. Bone, Leana Lavoie, Laura T. Arbour, Alfonso J. Solimano, Manish Sadarangani, Hind Sbihi, David A. Goldfarb
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估婴儿 RSV 感染相关住院治疗的基于人群的 发病率,重点关注与社区偏远有关的差异。
设计与对象
队列研究 回顾性研究于 2013 年 4 月至 2024 年 3 月在加拿大不列颠哥伦比亚省进行,随访时间为一年。该研究纳入了 472,623 名婴儿,其中一部分 (n = 3636) 居住在偏远社区。
核心方法
该研究使用常规收集的健康管理数据来评估全因和 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 的住院情况,以及三级 PICU 入院、住院时间和航空运输等次要结果。发病率 率比 (IRR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI) 使用泊松广义估计方程进行估计,考虑按社区进行聚类并根据性别、早产和慢性病进行调整。
主要结果
Infants from remote communities had higher hospitalization risks for all-cause LRTIs (IRR 2.91, 95% CI 2.24-3.79) and RSV-LRTIs (IRR 1.60, 95% CI 1.17-2.19) compared to those in metropolitan areas. After adjusting for covariates, the risk remained elevated for all-cause LRTIs (aIRR 2.84, 95% CI 2.22-3.63) and RSV-LRTIs (aIRR 1.56, 95% CI 1.15-2.12).
解释与创新
作者解释说,偏远社区因 RSV 和全因 LRTI 住院率较高,并不能完全用测量的临床或社会健康决定因素来解释,突显了疾病负担地理差异的新发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要制定针对特定地区的预防策略并公平实施 RSV 免疫计划,以解决偏远地区 RSV 和全因 LRTI 住院率过高的负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于常规收集的行政数据,这些数据可能存在缺失或不准确的信息。然而,证据确凿,调整后的 发病率 比率清楚地表明偏远社区的风险增加。

RSV and all-cause lower respiratory infection burden among infants residing in remote British Columbia: Retrospective population-based birth cohort study

Authors: Allison W. Watts, Marina Viñeta Paramo, Taylor R. Jamieson, Jeffrey N. Bone, Leana Lavoie, Laura T. Arbour, Alfonso J. Solimano, Manish Sadarangani, Hind Sbihi, David A. Goldfarb
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To determine the population-based incidence of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection-related hospitalization in infancy by community remoteness. METHODS: Retrospective cohort study of live births within an integrated health jurisdiction in British Columbia (BC, Canada) from April 2013 to March 2024, followed for 1 year, using routinely collected health administrative data. Main outcomes were hospitalizations for all-cause and RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI). Secondary outcomes were tertiary pediatric intensive care unit (PICU) admissions, length of stay, and air transport. Main exposures were community remoteness and social determinants of health. Incidence rate ratios (IRRs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were estimated with Poisson generalized estimating equations accounting for clustering by community, with and without additional adjustment for sex, prematurity, and chronic conditions. RESULTS: Among 472,623 infants, those living in remote communities (n = 3636) had higher hospitalization risk for all-cause (IRR 2.91, 95% CI 2.24-3.79) and RSV-LRTIs (IRR 1.60, 95% CI 1.17-2.19) compared to metropolitan areas. Length of stay and PICU admission rates were similar by region. Almost half (48.8%) of children from remote areas hospitalized for RSV-LRTIs required air evacuation. Infants from remote communities remained at higher risk for all-cause (aIRR 2.84, 95% CI 2.22-3.63) and RSV-LRTI (aIRR 1.56, 95% CI 1.15-2.12) hospitalizations after adjusting for covariates. CONCLUSION: Infants in remote communities experienced a disproportionately high burden of RSV and all-cause LRTI hospitalization that is not fully explained by measured clinical or social determinants of health. These findings underscore the need for region-specific approaches and equitable implementation of prevention programs as jurisdictions move toward universal RSV immunization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the population-based incidence of RSV infection-related hospitalizations in infants, focusing on disparities linked to community remoteness.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on live births in British Columbia, Canada, from April 2013 to March 2024, with follow-up for one year. The study included 472,623 infants, with a subset (n = 3636) residing in remote communities.
Core methods
The study used routinely collected health administrative data to evaluate hospitalizations for all-cause and RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs), along with secondary outcomes such as tertiary PICU admissions, length of stay, and air transport. Incidence rate ratios (IRRs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were estimated using Poisson generalized estimating equations, accounting for clustering by community and adjusting for sex, prematurity, and chronic conditions.
Main results
Infants from remote communities had higher hospitalization risks for all-cause LRTIs (IRR 2.91, 95% CI 2.24-3.79) and RSV-LRTIs (IRR 1.60, 95% CI 1.17-2.19) compared to those in metropolitan areas. After adjusting for covariates, the risk remained elevated for all-cause LRTIs (aIRR 2.84, 95% CI 2.22-3.63) and RSV-LRTIs (aIRR 1.56, 95% CI 1.15-2.12).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the higher hospitalization rates in remote communities for RSV and all-cause LRTIs are not fully explained by the measured clinical or social determinants of health, highlighting a novel finding of geographic disparities in disease burden.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the need for region-specific prevention strategies and equitable implementation of RSV immunization programs to address the disproportionately high burden of RSV and all-cause LRTI hospitalizations in remote areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on routinely collected administrative data, which may have missing or inaccurate information. However, the evidence is strong, with adjusted incidence rate ratios providing a clear indication of the increased risk in remote communities.

vartracker:一种用于病原体纵向变异分析和可视化的端到端工具。

vartracker: an end-to-end tool for pathogen longitudinal variant analysis and visualization.
作者： Charles S P Foster, William D Rawlinson
摘要中文翻译纵向测序可以揭示急性和慢性感染期间的细粒度病原体 进化,并为公共卫生应对措施提供信息。然而,将有序的病原体基因组数据整合到连贯的进化和临床框架中可能是乏味且容易出错的。我们推出了 vartracker,一种用于纵向病原体变异分析和可视化的开源工具。给定有序的示例清单,vartracker 支持三个入口点:原始序列读取、参考对齐的 BAM 文件或用户提供的变体调用格式 (VCF) 文件和覆盖输入。原始读取和 BAM 输入通过集成的 Snakemake 工作流程进行处理,而 VCF 模式则从预先计算的文件开始。变体相对于参考 基因组 进行标准化和注释,跨时间点进行跟踪,并分类为原始或新出现的以及瞬态或持久的。报告推断的氨基酸变化,对于 SARS-CoV-2 分析,可以通过功能数据库自动链接关键 突变 的相关已发表文献。vartracker 输出模式记录的结果表、用于再现性的来源元数据、出版物质量的静态图以及用于数据探索的交互式热图。尽管 vartracker 与 SARS-CoV-2 参考资产打包在一起,并且最初是为 SARS-CoV-2 数据集开发的,但在提供适当的参考数据时,vartracker 与病原体无关。我们使用 SARS-CoV-2 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 A (RSV-A) 数据集展示了其实用性。vartracker 可通过 GitHub、PyPI 和 Bioconda 免费获取。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决将有序病原体基因组数据整合到连贯的进化和临床框架中的挑战,这是乏味且容易出错的。
设计与对象
该研究引入了 vartracker,这是一种用于纵向病原体变异分析和可视化的开源工具,并使用 SARS-CoV-2 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 A (RSV-A) 数据集进行了演示。
核心方法
vartracker 通过集成的 Snakemake 工作流程处理原始读取和 BAM 输入,VCF 模式从预先计算的文件开始。变体被标准化,相对于参考基因组进行注释,并被分类为原始的或新出现的以及瞬态的或持久的。
主要结果
报告推断的氨基酸变化,对于 SARS-CoV-2 分析,可以通过功能数据库自动链接关键 突变 的相关已发表文献。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了 vartracker 在分析病原体 进化 并将 突变 与功能数据联系起来方面的实用性,这是一种纵向变异分析的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过详细跟踪病原体 进化 和 突变 的影响,特别是 呼吸道合胞病毒 的影响,为 监测 和公共卫生应对措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于证明该工具在 SARS-CoV-2 和 RSV-A 数据集上的实用性,并且证据强度中等,因为它依赖于仅抽象信息。

vartracker: an end-to-end tool for pathogen longitudinal variant analysis and visualization.

Authors: Charles S P Foster, William D Rawlinson
Original abstract (English)
Longitudinal sequencing can reveal fine-grained pathogen evolution during acute and chronic infections and inform public health responses. However, integrating ordered pathogen genomic data into a coherent evolutionary and clinical framework can be tedious and error-prone. We present vartracker, an open-source tool for longitudinal pathogen variant analysis and visualization. Given an ordered sample manifest, vartracker supports three entry points: raw sequence reads, reference-aligned BAM files, or user-supplied variant-call format (VCF) files and coverage inputs. Raw-read and BAM inputs are processed through an integrated Snakemake workflow, whereas VCF mode starts from precomputed files. Variants are normalized and annotated relative to a reference genome, tracked across timepoints, and classified as original or newly emerging and as transient or persistent. Inferred amino acid changes are reported, and for SARS-CoV-2 analyses, relevant published literature for key mutations can be automatically linked through a functional database. vartracker outputs a schema-documented results table, provenance metadata for reproducibility, publication-quality static figures, and an interactive heatmap for data exploration. Although packaged with SARS-CoV-2 reference assets and initially developed for SARS-CoV-2 datasets, vartracker is pathogen-agnostic when appropriate reference data are supplied. We demonstrate its utility using SARS-CoV-2 and respiratory syncytial virus A (RSV-A) datasets. vartracker is freely available through GitHub, PyPI, and Bioconda.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of integrating ordered pathogen genomic data into a coherent evolutionary and clinical framework, which is tedious and error-prone.
Design and population
The study introduced vartracker, an open-source tool for longitudinal pathogen variant analysis and visualization, which was demonstrated using SARS-CoV-2 and respiratory syncytial virus A (RSV-A) datasets.
Core methods
vartracker processes raw-read and BAM inputs through an integrated Snakemake workflow, and VCF mode starts from precomputed files. Variants are normalized, annotated relative to a reference genome, and classified as original or newly emerging and as transient or persistent.
Main results
Inferred amino acid changes are reported, and for SARS-CoV-2 analyses, relevant published literature for key mutations can be automatically linked through a functional database.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating the utility of vartracker in analyzing pathogen evolution and linking mutations to functional data, which is a novel approach for longitudinal variant analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and public health responses by enabling detailed tracking of pathogen evolution and mutation impacts, particularly for respiratory syncytial virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to demonstrating the tool's utility with SARS-CoV-2 and RSV-A datasets, and the evidence strength is moderate as it relies on abstract-only information.

多样化的肺部挑战引发了保守的单核细胞到巨噬细胞的分化蓝图

Diverse lung challenges elicit a conserved monocyte-to-macrophage differentiation blueprint
作者： Chrysante S. Iliakis, Wouter T’Jonck, Isobel C. Mouat, Jack McCowan, Zoe Burden, Gareth-Rhys Jones, Sam Bankole, Jiawei Liang, Justina Kulikauskaite, Stefania Crotta
摘要中文翻译肺泡巨噬细胞 (AM) 是抵御肺部损伤的第一道防线。这些损伤通常导致胎儿来源的组织驻留 AM (fAM) 被具有不同功能的单核细胞来源的 AM (mono-AM) 取代,这会在未来的肺部挑战中影响肺部免疫力。然而,这些改变是否是保守的或侮辱特异性的尚不清楚。在这里,我们表明 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染会长期重塑 AM 隔室。我们对 RSV 感染、甲型流感病毒感染或氯膦酸盐脂质体给药后的 fAM 和 mono-AM 进行比较分析,并确定 CD11b + mono-AM 的关键组织整合检查点,该检查点涉及增殖能力的获得并需要 EGR2 介导的转录重连。尽管分化过程中的炎症环境截然不同,但 RSV、甲型流感病毒或氯膦酸盐脂质体引发的 Mono-AM 具有固有的个体发育依赖性转录、增殖、代谢和增强的免疫反应性刻板特征;因此,由无菌或感染性损伤引起的单 AM 可以防止随后的肺炎链球菌感染。相比之下,fAM 显示上下文相关的转录改变。我们的研究结果强调,AM 区室的长期变化显示出依赖于起源的表型分歧,并且单 AM 整合遵循由环境因素微调的硬连线轨迹。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解不同肺部损伤后肺泡巨噬细胞(AM)区室的长期变化,解决这些变化是保守的还是损伤特异性的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对小鼠 RSV 感染、甲型流感病毒感染或氯膦酸盐脂质体给药后胎儿来源的组织驻留 AM (fAM) 和单核细胞来源的 AM (mono-AM) 进行比较分析。
核心方法
研究人员使用包括测序在内的各种检测方法对 fAM 和 mono-AM 进行了比较分析,以确定转录重连和增殖能力。
主要结果
RSV感染长期重塑AM区室,由RSV、甲型流感病毒或氯膦酸盐脂质体引发的单AMs表现出固有的个体发育依赖性转录、增殖、代谢和增强的免疫反应性的刻板特征,从而防止随后的肺炎链球菌感染。
解释与创新
作者解释说,单 AM 具有保守的分化蓝图,与特定的损伤无关,并且这种固有的轨迹可以通过环境因素进行微调。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,不同损伤引起的单 AM 对后续感染具有保护作用,这可以为未来的肺免疫和 疫苗接种 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到动物模型的使用以及对单一类型肺部损伤的关注的限制,这可能无法完全推广到人类的情况。

Diverse lung challenges elicit a conserved monocyte-to-macrophage differentiation blueprint

Authors: Chrysante S. Iliakis, Wouter T’Jonck, Isobel C. Mouat, Jack McCowan, Zoe Burden, Gareth-Rhys Jones, Sam Bankole, Jiawei Liang, Justina Kulikauskaite, Stefania Crotta
Original abstract (English)
Alveolar macrophages (AMs) form a first line of defense to lung insults. These insults often lead to replacement of fetal-derived tissue-resident AMs (fAMs) by monocyte-derived AMs (mono-AMs) with different functionality, which impacts lung immunity during future lung challenges. However, whether these alterations are conserved or insult specific is not well understood. Here we show that respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection remodels the AM compartment long term. We perform comparative analyses of fAMs and mono-AMs after RSV infection, influenza A virus infection or clodronate liposome administration and identify a crucial tissue integration checkpoint of CD11b + mono-AMs that involves acquisition of proliferative capacity and requires EGR2-mediated transcriptional rewiring. Mono-AMs elicited by RSV, influenza A virus or clodronate liposomes have a hardwired ontogeny-dependent stereotypical profile of transcription, proliferation, metabolism and enhanced immunoreactivity despite a substantially different inflammatory environment during differentiation; accordingly, mono-AMs elicited by sterile or infectious insult protect from subsequent Streptococcus pneumoniae infection. By contrast, fAMs display context-dependent transcriptional alterations. Our findings highlight that long-term changes in the AM compartment show origin-dependent phenotypic divergence and that mono-AM integration follows a hardwired trajectory fine-tuned by environmental factors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the long-term changes in the alveolar macrophage (AM) compartment after different lung insults, addressing the knowledge gap regarding whether these alterations are conserved or insult-specific.
Design and population
The study involved comparative analyses of fetal-derived tissue-resident AMs (fAMs) and monocyte-derived AMs (mono-AMs) after RSV infection, influenza A virus infection, or clodronate liposome administration in mice.
Core methods
The researchers performed comparative analyses of fAMs and mono-AMs using various assays, including sequencing, to identify transcriptional rewiring and proliferation capacity.
Main results
RSV infection remodels the AM compartment long term, with mono-AMs elicited by RSV, influenza A virus, or clodronate liposomes showing a hardwired ontogeny-dependent stereotypical profile of transcription, proliferation, metabolism, and enhanced immunoreactivity, which protects from subsequent Streptococcus pneumoniae infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that mono-AMs have a conserved differentiation blueprint, independent of the specific insult, and that this hardwired trajectory is fine-tuned by environmental factors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that mono-AMs elicited by different insults have a protective role against subsequent infections, which could inform future strategies for lung immunity and vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of animal models and the focus on a single type of lung insult, which may not fully generalize to human conditions.

疾病预防控制中心国家废水 监测 系统废水中呼吸道病毒病毒活性水平的验证,2025 – 2026 年呼吸道季节

Validation of Centers for Disease Control and Prevention National Wastewater Surveillance System’s Wastewater Viral Activity Level for Respiratory Viruses, 2025 –2026 Respiratory Season
作者： Jenna Webb, Megan E. Gerdes, Heather E. Reese, Megan Bias, Dan Cornforth, Igor Volkov, Dennis Nichols, Alexandra Busbee, Erin Anderson, Elisabeth Burnor
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 于 2023 年开发了 SARS-CoV-2 废水病毒活动水平指标 (WVAL),以满足公共指标的需求,该指标汇总废水病毒 RNA 浓度数据,为美国提供辖区、区域和国家 监测 系统估计值。WVAL 于 2024 年 8 月进行了扩展,纳入了甲型流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的病毒特定指标。本文介绍了更新后的 WVAL 方法在 2025 年验证的结果。方法 在 2025 年 3 月至 7 月进行的验证过程中,使用截至 2025 年 3 月 22 日从 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感和 RSV 采样点收集的废水 监测 数据对候选 WVAL 方法进行了评估。评估的组成部分包括基线计算和稳定期、基线和标准差更新的频率、用于计算基线的样本回顾期以及用于分配 WVAL 类别的切点。结果 数据包括 4,447 个 SARS-CoV-2、1,592 个甲型流感和 1,454 个 RSV 数据提交者级采样点。选择 z 分数阈值 4 进行异常值去除,并选择具有 24 个月回顾期的非标准化废水浓度进行跨病原体的基线和标准差计算。SARS-CoV-2 的基线和标准差更新计划于 4 月 1 日和 10 月 1 日更新,甲型流感和 RSV 的基线和标准差更新计划于 8 月 1 日更新。选择了八周的稳定期。选择中位数聚合进行区域和国家估计,并选择病原体特异性切点以使 WVAL 类别与临床 监测 数据保持一致。国家 WVAL 与急诊科 监测 的 SARS-CoV-2(Kendall tau = 0.78)、甲型流感(0.81)和 RSV(0.74)数据存在中度至强相关性。结论 年度验证支持 WVAL 方法的更新,减少了极值,提高了病原体和地理水平之间的可比性,并与临床 监测 保持一致。更新的方法为 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感和 RSV 的公共废水 监测 提供了更加一致的框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在验证更新后的呼吸道病毒废水病毒活性水平 (WVAL) 方法,包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),以改善美国的公共卫生 呼吸道合胞病毒。这一需求源自 CDC 于 2023 年开发的 SARS-CoV-2 WVAL 指标,该指标强调了病毒 RNA 浓度数据聚合标准化方法的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究于 2025 年 3 月至 7 月期间进行,使用截至 2025 年 3 月 22 日从 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感和 RSV 采样点收集的废水 监测 数据评估了 WVAL 方法。总体数据包括来自美国多个采样点的废水数据。
核心方法
验证过程涉及评估基线计算和稳定期、基线和标准差更新频率、基线计算的样本回顾期以及分配 WVAL 类别的切点等组件。选择 z 分数阈值 4 来去除异常值,并使用具有 24 个月回顾期的非标准化废水浓度来计算病原体的基线和标准偏差。
主要结果
该研究包括来自 4,447 个 SARS-CoV-2、1,592 个甲型流感和 1,454 个 RSV 数据提交者级别采样点的数据。该方法已更新,包括甲型流感和 RSV 的病毒特异性指标,从而将 WVAL 系统扩展到最初关注的 SARS-CoV-2 之外。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持更新的 WVAL 方法,该方法减少了极值,提高了病原体和地理水平之间的可比性,并与临床 监测 数据保持一致。新颖之处在于 WVAL 系统的扩展,包括 RSV 和甲型流感。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感和 RSV 的公共废水 监测 提供了更加一致的框架,可以为公共卫生决策提供信息并提高病毒活动监测的准确性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于摘要证据,并且使用截至 2025 年 3 月 22 日的数据验证了该方法。这些证据支持更新的呼吸道病毒 WVAL 方法具有中等到高可信度。

Validation of Centers for Disease Control and Prevention National Wastewater Surveillance System’s Wastewater Viral Activity Level for Respiratory Viruses, 2025 –2026 Respiratory Season

Authors: Jenna Webb, Megan E. Gerdes, Heather E. Reese, Megan Bias, Dan Cornforth, Igor Volkov, Dennis Nichols, Alexandra Busbee, Erin Anderson, Elisabeth Burnor
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) developed the Wastewater Viral Activity Level metric (WVAL) for SARS-CoV-2 in 2023 to meet the need for a public metric that aggregates wastewater viral RNA concentration data to provide jurisdictional, regional, and national surveillance system estimates for the United States. The WVAL was expanded in August 2024 to include virus-specific metrics for influenza A and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). This paper describes results the 2025 validation of the updated WVAL methodology. Methods During validation conducted from March through July 2025, candidate WVAL methods were evaluated using wastewater surveillance data collected through March 22, 2025, from SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, and RSV sampling sites. Evaluated components included the baseline calculation and stabilization period, the frequency of baseline and standard deviation updates, the sample look-back period used to calculate the baseline, and the cut points used to assign WVAL categories. Results Data included 4,447 SARS-CoV-2, 1,592 influenza A, and 1,454 RSV data submitter level sampling sites. A z-score threshold of 4 was selected for outlier removal, and non-normalized wastewater concentrations with a 24-month look-back period were selected for baseline and standard deviation calculations across pathogens. Baseline and standard deviation updates were scheduled for April 1 and October 1 for SARS-CoV-2 and August 1 for influenza A and RSV. An eight-week stabilization period was selected. Median-of-medians aggregation was selected for regional and national estimates, and pathogen-specific cut-points were selected to align WVAL categories with clinical surveillance data. National WVALs were moderately to strongly correlated with emergency department surveillance data for SARS-CoV-2 (Kendall’s tau = 0.78), influenza A (0.81), and RSV (0.74). Conclusions Annual validation supported updates to the WVAL methodology that reduced extreme values, improved comparability across pathogens and geographic levels, and maintained alignment with clinical surveillance. The updated methods provide a more consistent framework for public wastewater surveillance of SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, and RSV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to validate the updated Wastewater Viral Activity Level (WVAL) methodology for respiratory viruses, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), to improve public health surveillance in the United States. The need arose from the CDC's development of the WVAL metric for SARS-CoV-2 in 2023, which highlighted the importance of a standardized approach for viral RNA concentration data aggregation.
Design and population
The study was conducted from March through July 2025, evaluating the WVAL methodology using wastewater surveillance data collected through March 22, 2025, from SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, and RSV sampling sites. The population included wastewater data from multiple sampling sites across the United States.
Core methods
The validation process involved assessing components such as baseline calculation and stabilization period, frequency of baseline and standard deviation updates, sample look-back period for baseline calculations, and cut points for assigning WVAL categories. A z-score threshold of 4 was selected for outlier removal, and non-normalized wastewater concentrations with a 24-month look-back period were used for baseline and standard deviation calculations across pathogens.
Main results
The study included data from 4,447 SARS-CoV-2, 1,592 influenza A, and 1,454 RSV data submitter level sampling sites. The methodology was updated to include virus-specific metrics for influenza A and RSV, expanding the WVAL system beyond its initial focus on SARS-CoV-2.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as supporting the updated WVAL methodology, which reduced extreme values, improved comparability across pathogens and geographic levels, and maintained alignment with clinical surveillance data. The novelty lies in the expansion of the WVAL system to include RSV and influenza A.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a more consistent framework for public wastewater surveillance of SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, and RSV, which can inform public health decisions and enhance the accuracy of viral activity monitoring.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the methodology was validated using data up to March 22, 2025. The evidence supports a moderate to high confidence in the updated WVAL methodology for respiratory viruses.

教育干预对泰国孕妇 RSV 疫苗 知识和接受度的影响:一项随机对照试验

Impact of an educational intervention on the knowledge and acceptance of RSV vaccine among Thai pregnant women: a randomized controlled trial
作者： Zainab Aliyu Attahir, Surasith Chaithongwongwatthana, Nasamon Wanlapakorn
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是全球婴儿急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因。母体RSV 疫苗接种是一种有效的预防策略;然而,在许多情况下,接受度仍然不确定。本研究旨在评估基于视频的教育干预对泰国孕妇接受母体 RSV 疫苗接种 意愿的影响。方法 在朱拉隆功国王纪念医院(2025 年 12 月至 2026 年 2 月)进行的一项随机对照试验中,符合条件的孕妇被随机分配接受 5 分钟关于母亲 RSV 疫苗 的教育视频加上常规产前护理(干预),或仅接受常规产前护理(对照)。问卷评估了基线、干预后和随访时的 疫苗 接受度、知识、态度和障碍。主要结局是愿意接受母体 RSV 疫苗接种。次要结果包括 RSV 相关知识和观念的变化。比较各组之间的结果。使用参数和非参数统计分析这两种结果,同时使用多变量逻辑回归评估与 疫苗 接受度相关的因素。结果 410 名随机参与者中,383 名参与者完成了随访(干预组 n = 195;对照组 n = 188)。接受 疫苗接种 的基线意愿相似(干预:67.2% 与对照:71.8%;p = 0.33)。在干预组中,观看视频后的意愿立即从 67.2% 上升至 82.6%(增加 15.4%,95% CI:8.6%-23.2%)。然而,随访时未观察到显着的组间差异(78.5% vs. 干预组)干预后知识得分显着较高(平均值±SD:5.1±1.3 vs. 4.7±1.5;p = 0.02)。基线接受度、高知识、职业和积极态度是意愿的独立预测因素。疫苗 接受的常见障碍包括成本、缺乏政府支持和安全问题。结论干预措施可以提高知识并立即提高意愿,但不会显着提高随访时的接受度。因此,教育干预措施应与解决安全问题、提供者建议和经济可及性的策略相结合,以最大限度地提高孕产妇 RSV 疫苗 的吸收率。试验注册 该试验在泰国临床试验注册中心进行了回顾性注册(注册号:TCTR20251226003 https://www.thaiclinicaltrials.org/show/TCTR20251226003)。该试验于2025年12月16日首次注册,并于2025年12月26日获得批准。它是按照报告试验综合标准(CONSORT)2025声明进行和报告的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是全球婴儿急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因。母体RSV 疫苗接种是一种有效的预防策略;然而,在许多情况下,接受度仍然不确定。
设计与对象
这项研究是一项随机对照试验,于 2025 年 12 月至 2026 年 2 月在朱拉隆功国王纪念医院进行,涉及 410 名孕妇,随机接受关于母亲 RSV 疫苗 的 5 分钟教育视频加常规产前护理或仅接受常规产前护理。
核心方法
问卷评估了基线、干预后和随访时的 疫苗 接受度、知识、态度和障碍。使用参数和非参数统计分析这两种结果,同时使用多变量逻辑回归评估与 疫苗 接受度相关的因素。
主要结果
在 410 名随机参与者中,383 名参与者完成了随访(干预组 n = 195;对照组 n = 188)。在干预组中,观看视频后的意愿立即从 67.2% 上升至 82.6%(增加 15.4%,95% CI:8.6%-23.2%)。干预组的干预后知识得分显着较高(平均值±标准差:5.1±1.3 vs. 4.7±1.5;p = 0.02)。
解释与创新
简短的视频干预可以提高知识并立即提高意愿,但并不能显着提高后续的接受度。基线接受度、高知识、职业和积极态度是意愿的独立预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,教育干预措施可以增强接受孕产妇 RSV 疫苗接种 的知识和意愿,但需要解决安全问题、提供者建议和财务可及性的其他策略来提高 疫苗 的吸收。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。由于该研究是一项随机对照试验,随访率相对较高,但随访时组间无显着差异,因此证据强度中等。

Impact of an educational intervention on the knowledge and acceptance of RSV vaccine among Thai pregnant women: a randomized controlled trial

Authors: Zainab Aliyu Attahir, Surasith Chaithongwongwatthana, Nasamon Wanlapakorn
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory tract infections in infants globally. Maternal RSV vaccination is an effective preventive strategy; however, acceptance remains uncertain in many settings. This study aimed to evaluate a video-based educational intervention on willingness to accept maternal RSV vaccination among pregnant women in Thailand. Methods In a randomized controlled trial at King Chulalongkorn Memorial Hospital (December 2025 to February 2026), eligible pregnant women were randomized to receive either a 5-minute educational video on the maternal RSV vaccine plus routine antenatal care (intervention), or routine antenatal care alone (control). Questionnaires assessed vaccine acceptance, knowledge, attitudes, and barriers at baseline, immediately post-intervention, and follow-up. The primary outcome was willingness to accept maternal RSV vaccination. Secondary outcomes included changes in RSV-related knowledge and perceptions. Outcomes were compared between groups. Both outcomes were analyzed using parametric and non-parametric statistics, while the factors associated with vaccine acceptance were evaluated using multivariable logistic regression. Results Of 410 randomized participants, 383 participants completed follow-up (intervention n = 195; control n = 188). Baseline willingness to accept vaccination was similar (intervention: 67.2% vs. control: 71.8%; p = 0.33). In the intervention group, willingness increased immediately after viewing the video from 67.2% to 82.6% (increase 15.4%, 95% CI: 8.6%-23.2%). However, no significant between-group difference was observed at follow-up (78.5% vs. The intervention group had significantly higher post-intervention knowledge scores (mean ± SD: 5.1 ± 1.3 vs. 4.7 ± 1.5; p = 0.02). Baseline acceptance, high knowledge, occupation and positive attitudes were independent predictors of willingness. Common barriers to vaccine acceptance included cost, lack of government support, and safety concerns. Conclusion A brief video intervention can improve knowledge and produce an immediate increase in willingness but did not significantly increase acceptance at follow-up. Therefore, educational interventions should be combined with strategies addressing safety concerns, provider recommendations, and financial accessibility to maximize maternal RSV vaccine uptake. Trial registration The trial was retrospectively registered with the Thai Clinical Trials Registry (Registration Number: TCTR20251226003 https://www.thaiclinicaltrials.org/show/TCTR20251226003 ). The trial was first registered on December 16, 2025, and approved on December 26, 2025. It was conducted and reported in accordance with the Consolidated Standards of Reporting Trials (CONSORT) 2025 Statement.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory tract infections in infants globally. Maternal RSV vaccination is an effective preventive strategy; however, acceptance remains uncertain in many settings.
Design and population
This study was a randomized controlled trial conducted at King Chulalongkorn Memorial Hospital from December 2025 to February 2026, involving 410 pregnant women randomized to receive either a 5-minute educational video on the maternal RSV vaccine plus routine antenatal care or routine antenatal care alone.
Core methods
Questionnaires assessed vaccine acceptance, knowledge, attitudes, and barriers at baseline, immediately post-intervention, and follow-up. Both outcomes were analyzed using parametric and non-parametric statistics, while the factors associated with vaccine acceptance were evaluated using multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
Of 410 randomized participants, 383 participants completed follow-up (intervention n = 195; control n = 188). In the intervention group, willingness increased immediately after viewing the video from 67.2% to 82.6% (increase 15.4%, 95% CI: 8.6%-23.2%). The intervention group had significantly higher post-intervention knowledge scores (mean ± SD: 5.1 ± 1.3 vs. 4.7 ± 1.5; p = 0.02).
Interpretation and novelty
A brief video intervention can improve knowledge and produce an immediate increase in willingness but did not significantly increase acceptance at follow-up. Baseline acceptance, high knowledge, occupation and positive attitudes were independent predictors of willingness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that educational interventions can enhance knowledge and willingness to accept maternal RSV vaccination, but additional strategies addressing safety concerns, provider recommendations, and financial accessibility are needed to improve vaccine uptake.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design. The evidence strength is moderate due to the study being a randomized controlled trial with a relatively high follow-up rate but no significant between-group difference at follow-up.

两岁以上 RSV 阳性儿童的抗生素处方:单中心回顾性审核

ANTIBIOTIC PRESCRIBING IN RSV POSITIVE CHILDREN AGED OVER TWO YEARS: A SINGLE-CENTRE RETROSPECTIVE AUDIT
作者： Jhyni Ravishankar, Manu Peruvelil Moses, Emily L. D. Chesshyre
摘要中文翻译背景:毛细支气管炎是一种常见的病毒性下呼吸道感染,主要影响2岁以下儿童,在英国由呼吸道合胞病毒(呼吸道合胞病毒,RSV)引起的病例高达80%。诊断主要表现为临床症状,儿童通常出现流涕症状,随后发展为咳嗽、进食困难和发热。NICE建议将支持性治疗作为治疗的主要治疗方式,并不推荐常规使用抗生素、类固醇、吸入性支气管扩张剂和胸部X光检查,但临床实践中仍存在对这些建议的不遵守现象。在较大儿童中,诊断的确定性进一步降低,RSV阳性本身并不能确认毛细支气管炎,这在缺乏年龄特异性指导的群体中,造成了临床和抗菌药物使用管理的挑战。NICE建议在这一年龄组中考虑其他诊断,包括病毒性诱发喘息或早发性哮喘,特别是在存在反复喘息、无湿啰音或个人或家族过敏史的情况下。本次审计旨在评估年龄超过2岁的RSV阳性儿童是否接受了支持性治疗,并在开具抗生素的情况下,确定与抗生素处方相关的临床因素。方法:在英国皇家德文郡和埃克塞特医院进行了一项单中心回顾性审计。通过EPIC电子病历系统,共识别出83名年龄超过2岁且RSV阳性的儿科患者,这些患者于2024年10月至2025年2月期间就诊于儿科部门。提取了有关人口统计学、危险因素、临床表现、检查、治疗和结果的数据。结果:44.6%的年龄超过2岁的RSV阳性儿童接受了抗生素治疗,尽管NICE建议将支持性治疗作为治疗的主要方法。45.8%的患者需要住院治疗。发热(64.9%)、呼吸窘迫(59.5%)和胸部X光检查结果是与抗生素处方最显著相关的临床发现。值得注意的是,51.4%的接受抗生素治疗的患者进行了胸部X光检查,尽管NICE并未推荐常规进行此类检查,其中94.7%的检查结果异常。这些发现已被广泛认为是模拟肺炎而非确诊肺炎,不应用于指导疑似毛细支气管炎的抗生素处方。关键的是,70.3%的接受抗生素治疗的患者未进行血液检查,这意味着大多数处方是在没有任何支持性炎症标志物的情况下开具的。62.1%的抗生素处方是由Tier 1临床医生开具的。此外,还存在更广泛的指南不遵守现象,42.2%的患者接受了吸入器或雾化器治疗,31.3%的患者接受了类固醇治疗,而NICE并未推荐在毛细支气管炎中常规使用这些药物。结论:本次审计揭示了年龄超过2岁的RSV阳性儿童管理中存在显著的指南不遵守现象,近一半的患者接受了抗生素治疗,其中许多患者缺乏细菌感染的客观证据。在这一年龄组中,由于毛细支气管炎、病毒性诱发喘息和早发性哮喘的重叠,诊断的不确定性是主要驱动因素,但无法单独解释观察到的广泛不遵守现象。本次审计得出的关键建议如下。建议对Tier 1临床医生进行针对性教育,以提高对差异性诊断、抗菌药物管理以及在这一年龄组中适当使用检查的认识。抗生素处方应有明确的临床依据记录,并且未来审计周期中需要改善对过敏史、吸烟暴露和体检结果的记录。这些发现突显了儿科抗菌药物管理中的更广泛挑战:在缺乏针对性教育和明确本地指导的情况下,诊断的不确定性可能导致抗生素的过度使用,而这一人群的证据基础仍然有限。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估两年内 RSV 阳性儿童的管理,解决诊断 毛细支气管炎 时临床不确定性的挑战,以及持续不遵守 NICE 指南建议支持性护理而非常规抗生素使用的问题。
设计与对象
英国皇家德文郡和埃克塞特医院进行了一项单中心回顾性审核,涉及 83 名年龄超过 2 岁、RSV 检测呈阳性并于 2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 2 月期间到儿科就诊的患者。
核心方法
提取了有关人口统计、风险因素、临床表现、调查、管理和结果的数据,重点关注抗生素处方实践和相关临床因素。
主要结果
尽管 NICE 指南建议支持性护理,但 44.6% 的两岁以上 RSV 阳性儿童仍服用了抗生素。此外,45.8%的患者需要住院,51.4%的患者进行了胸部X光检查,其中94.7%的患者显示异常。
解释与创新
审计强调,在两年多的时间里,在管理 RSV 阳性儿童方面,严重不遵守 NICE 指南,许多抗生素处方缺乏细菌感染的客观证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了临床医生需要进行有针对性的抗菌药物管理教育,以及对 RSV 阳性儿童进行准确的 诊断 以减少不必要的抗生素使用的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单中心回顾性审核,可能会影响普适性。证据是中等的,因为它是基于观察数据而不是实验或前瞻性研究。

ANTIBIOTIC PRESCRIBING IN RSV POSITIVE CHILDREN AGED OVER TWO YEARS: A SINGLE-CENTRE RETROSPECTIVE AUDIT

Authors: Jhyni Ravishankar, Manu Peruvelil Moses, Emily L. D. Chesshyre
Original abstract (English)
Background: Bronchiolitis is a common viral lower respiratory tract infection predominantly affecting children under two years of age, with Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) responsible for up to 80% of cases in the United Kingdom. Diagnosis is primarily clinical, with children presenting with coryzal symptoms progressing to cough, feeding difficulty and fever. NICE recommends supportive care as the primary treatment and advises against the routine use of antibiotics, steroids, inhaled bronchodilators, and chest X-rays, yet non-adherence to these recommendations persists in clinical practice. In older children, diagnostic certainty is further reduced and RSV positivity alone does not confirm bronchiolitis, creating both a clinical and antimicrobial stewardship challenge in an age group where limited age-specific guidance creates further clinical uncertainty. NICE recommends considering other diagnoses, including viral-induced wheeze or early-onset asthma in this age group, particularly where there is recurrent wheeze, absence of crackles, or a personal or family history of atopy. This audit aimed to evaluate whether RSV-positive children aged over two years were managed supportively, and if prescribed antibiotics, to identify clinical factors associated with antibiotic prescribing in this cohort. Methods: A single-center retrospective audit was conducted at the Royal Devon and Exeter Hospital, United Kingdom. In total, 83 patients aged over two years who tested RSV-positive and presented to the pediatric department between October 2024 and February 2025 were identified via the EPIC electronic patient record system. Data were extracted on demographics, risk factors, clinical presentation, investigations, management, and outcomes. Results: 44.6% of RSV-positive children aged over two years were prescribed antibiotics, despite NICE recommending supportive care as the primary approach to treatment. 45.8% of patients required inpatient admission. Fever (64.9%), respiratory distress (59.5%), and chest X-ray findings were the clinical findings most strongly associated with antibiotic prescribing. Notably, 51.4% of antibiotic-prescribed patients had a chest X-ray performed despite this not being routinely recommended by NICE, with 94.7% showing abnormalities. Such findings are well recognized to mimic pneumonia rather than confirm it, and should not be used to guide antibiotic prescribing in suspected bronchiolitis. Critically, 70.3% of patients prescribed antibiotics had no blood tests performed, meaning the majority of prescriptions were made without any supporting inflammatory markers. 62.1% of antibiotic prescriptions were initiated by Tier 1 clinicians. Broader guideline non-adherence was also evident, with 42.2% of patients receiving inhalers or nebulizers and 31.3% receiving steroids, neither of which is routinely recommended by NICE in bronchiolitis. Conclusions: This audit reveals substantial guideline non-adherence in the management of RSV-positive children aged over two years, with nearly half receiving antibiotics, many without objective evidence of bacterial infection. Diagnostic uncertainty in this age group, where bronchiolitis, viral-induced wheeze, and early-onset asthma overlap, is a key driver, but cannot alone account for the breadth of non-adherence observed. Key recommendations arising from this audit include the following. Targeted education for Tier 1 clinicians on differential diagnosis, antimicrobial stewardship, and the appropriate use of investigations in this age group is recommended. Antibiotic prescribing should be supported by documented clinical justification, and improved recording of atopy history, smoking exposure, and examination findings will be essential for future audit cycles. These findings highlight a broader challenge in paediatric antimicrobial stewardship: diagnostic uncertainty, without targeted education and clear local guidance, risks driving antibiotic overuse in a population where the evidence base remains limited.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the management of RSV-positive children over two years, addressing the challenge of clinical uncertainty in diagnosing bronchiolitis and the persistent non-adherence to NICE guidelines recommending supportive care over routine antibiotic use.
Design and population
A single-center retrospective audit was conducted at the Royal Devon and Exeter Hospital, United Kingdom, involving 83 patients aged over two years who tested RSV-positive and presented to the pediatric department between October 2024 and February 2025.
Core methods
Data were extracted on demographics, risk factors, clinical presentation, investigations, management, and outcomes, focusing on antibiotic prescribing practices and associated clinical factors.
Main results
44.6% of RSV-positive children over two years were prescribed antibiotics, despite NICE guidelines recommending supportive care. Additionally, 45.8% of patients required inpatient admission, and 51.4% had chest X-rays performed, with 94.7% showing abnormalities.
Interpretation and novelty
The audit highlights substantial non-adherence to NICE guidelines in managing RSV-positive children over two years, with many antibiotic prescriptions lacking objective evidence of bacterial infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the need for targeted education for clinicians on antimicrobial stewardship and the importance of accurate diagnosis in RSV-positive children to reduce unnecessary antibiotic use.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single-center retrospective audit, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on observational data rather than experimental or prospective studies.

儿科病房儿童中母体 RSV 毛细支气管炎 和 RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 的临床严重程度:一项回顾性观察研究

MATERNAL RSV VACCINATION AND CLINICAL SEVERITY OF RSV-POSITIVE BRONCHIOLITIS IN CHILDREN ADMITTED TO THE PAEDIATRIC WARD: A RETROSPECTIVE OBSERVATIONAL STUDY
作者： Dinakaran Jayachandran, Muhammad Shoaib, Anna Bennett
摘要中文翻译背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿 毛细支气管炎 需要住院的主要原因。母体 RSV 疫苗接种 于 2024 年 9 月在英国引入,以减少婴儿早期严重的 RSV 疾病。这项回顾性研究评估了 RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 入院儿童的临床特征与母亲 RSV 免疫状态的关系,以及母亲 RSV 毛细支气管炎 与呼吸支持和住院时间之间的关系。方法 该研究纳入了 2025 年 10 月至 2026 年 2 月期间在信托基金内两家医院收治的经实验室确诊为 RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 的儿童。母体 RSV 疫苗接种 状态是从 BadgerNet 电子记录中获得的。儿童数据是从电子病历中收集的。收集的数据包括就诊时的年龄、呼吸支持和住院时间。呼吸支持分为无呼吸支持、低流量吸氧 (LFO2) 或高流量经鼻吸氧 (AIRVO)。结果 共有 97 名 RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 儿童入院。90 名儿童的母亲 RSV 疫苗接种 状态可用;37 例 (41%) 是接种疫苗的母亲所生,53 例 (59%) 是未接种疫苗的母亲所生。在疫苗接种组中,24 名(64.9%)儿童年龄为 0-6 个月,12 名(32.4%)儿童年龄为 6-12 个月,只有 1 名(2.7%)年龄大于 12 个月。相比之下,在未接种疫苗的母亲所生的孩子中,19 名 (36%) 年龄为 0-6 个月,8 名 (15%) 年龄为 6-12 个月,26 名 (49%) 年龄超过 12 个月。然而,接种组中感染 RSV 的 2 个月以下婴儿数量低于未接种组(4 例 vs 11 例)。因此,在母亲接受过 RSV 疫苗 的婴儿和儿童中,几乎所有入院 (97.3%) 都发生在 12 个月大之前。相比之下,未接种疫苗组中近一半 (49%) 的入院儿童年龄超过 12 个月。母亲接受过 RSV 疫苗 的孩子总体上需要的呼吸支持似乎较少。接种组中不需要呼吸支持的比例高于未接种组(62.2% vs 49%)。接种疫苗组中 27.0% 的儿童需要低流量氧气,而未接种疫苗组中这一比例为 36%。同样,接种疫苗组中有 10.8% 的儿童需要 AIRVO(高流量鼻氧),而未接种疫苗组的这一比例为 15%。疫苗接种 状态与住院时间之间存在统计学显着相关性 (χ2 = 12.83,p = 0.0016)。母亲接受过 疫苗 治疗的孩子更有可能住院 3 天(50.9% vs 18.9%)。局限性 这是一项观察性分析,未包括潜在的混杂因素,包括早产、潜在合并症和母体 RSV 疫苗接种 的时间。两组之间年龄分布的差异可能影响了结果。结论 接受 RSV 疫苗 的母亲所生的孩子似乎需要较少的呼吸支持,并且住院时间显着缩短。接种疫苗组中需要升级至 AIRVO 的儿童似乎较少,这表明 毛细支气管炎 的严重程度可能会降低。这些发现表明母体 RSV 疫苗接种 与疾病严重程度和医疗保健利用率降低之间存在关联。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿 毛细支气管炎 需要入院的主要原因。本研究旨在评估 RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 入院儿童的临床特征与母亲 RSV 免疫状态的关系,以及母亲 RSV 毛细支气管炎 与呼吸支持和住院时间之间的关系。
设计与对象
这项回顾性观察研究是在 2025 年 10 月至 2026 年 2 月期间在信托基金内的两家医院进行的。共有 97 名患有 RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 的儿童入院,其中 90 名儿童的母亲 RSV 毛细支气管炎 状态可用。
核心方法
儿童数据从电子病历中收集,母亲 RSV 疫苗接种 状态从 BadgerNet 电子记录中获取。收集的数据包括就诊时的年龄、呼吸支持和住院时间。
主要结果
90 名儿童的母亲 RSV 疫苗接种 状态可用;37 例 (41%) 是接种疫苗的母亲所生,53 例 (59%) 是未接种疫苗的母亲所生。接种组中感染 RSV 的 2 个月以下婴儿数量低于未接种组(4 例 vs 11 例)。
解释与创新
这些发现表明母体 RSV 疫苗接种 与疾病严重程度和医疗保健利用率降低之间存在关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能为婴儿 RSV 预防和管理的未来策略提供参考,特别是关于母亲 疫苗接种 在减少呼吸支持需求和缩短住院时间方面的潜在益处。
局限与证据强度
这是一项观察性分析,不包括潜在的混杂因素,包括早产、潜在的合并症和母体 RSV 疫苗接种 的时间。由于观察性质和缺乏对混杂变量的控制,证据是中等的。

MATERNAL RSV VACCINATION AND CLINICAL SEVERITY OF RSV-POSITIVE BRONCHIOLITIS IN CHILDREN ADMITTED TO THE PAEDIATRIC WARD: A RETROSPECTIVE OBSERVATIONAL STUDY

Authors: Dinakaran Jayachandran, Muhammad Shoaib, Anna Bennett
Original abstract (English)
Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of bronchiolitis requiring hospital admission in infants. Maternal RSV vaccination was introduced in the UK in September 2024 to reduce severe RSV disease during early infancy. This retrospective study evaluated the clinical characteristics of children admitted with RSV-positive bronchiolitis in relation to maternal RSV immunisation status and the association between maternal RSV vaccination and respiratory support and length of hospital stay. Methods Children admitted with laboratory-confirmed RSV-positive bronchiolitis between October 2025 and February 2026 across two hospitals within the Trust were included in the study. Maternal RSV vaccination status was obtained from BadgerNet electronic records. Children’s data were collected from electronic patient records. Data collected included age at presentation, respiratory support, and duration of hospital stay. Respiratory support was categorised as no respiratory support, low-flow oxygen (LFO2), or high-flow nasal oxygen (AIRVO). Results A total of 97 children were admitted with RSV-positive bronchiolitis. Maternal RSV vaccination status was available for 90 children; 37 (41%) were born to vaccinated mothers and 53 (59%) to unvaccinated mothers. In the vaccinated group, 24 (64.9%) children were aged 0–6 months, 12 (32.4%) were aged 6–12 months, and only one (2.7%) was older than 12 months. In contrast, among children born to unvaccinated mothers, 19 (36%) were aged 0–6 months, 8 (15%) were aged 6–12 months, and 26 (49%) were older than 12 months. However, the number of infants younger than 2 months with RSV infection was lower in the vaccinated group than in the unvaccinated group (4 vs 11 children). Thus, among infants and children whose mothers had received the RSV vaccine, almost all admissions (97.3%) occurred before 12 months of age. In contrast, almost half of admissions (49%) in the unvaccinated group occurred in children older than 12 months. Children whose mothers had received the RSV vaccine appeared to require less respiratory support overall. The proportion requiring no respiratory support was higher in the vaccinated group than in the unvaccinated group (62.2% vs 49%). Low-flow oxygen was required in 27.0% of children in the vaccinated group compared with 36% in the unvaccinated group. Similarly, AIRVO (high-flow nasal oxygen) was required in 10.8% of children in the vaccinated group compared with 15% in the unvaccinated group. There was a statistically significant association between vaccination status and length of hospital stay (χ2 = 12.83, p = 0.0016). Children whose mother had received the vaccine were more likely to have a hospital stay of 3 days (50.9% vs 18.9%). Limitations This was an observational analysis, and potential confounding factors were not included, including prematurity, underlying comorbidities and the timing of maternal RSV vaccination. The difference in age distribution between the two groups may have influenced the results. Conclusions Children born to mothers who received the RSV vaccine appeared to require less respiratory support and had statistically significant shorter hospital stays. Fewer children in the vaccinated group appeared to require escalation to AIRVO, suggesting a potential reduction in the severity of bronchiolitis. These findings suggest an association between maternal RSV vaccination and reduced disease severity and healthcare utilisation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of bronchiolitis requiring hospital admission in infants. This study aimed to evaluate the clinical characteristics of children admitted with RSV-positive bronchiolitis in relation to maternal RSV immunisation status and the association between maternal RSV vaccination and respiratory support and length of hospital stay.
Design and population
This retrospective observational study was conducted across two hospitals within the Trust between October 2025 and February 2026. A total of 97 children were admitted with RSV-positive bronchiolitis, with maternal RSV vaccination status available for 90 of them.
Core methods
Children’s data were collected from electronic patient records, and maternal RSV vaccination status was obtained from BadgerNet electronic records. Data collected included age at presentation, respiratory support, and duration of hospital stay.
Main results
Maternal RSV vaccination status was available for 90 children; 37 (41%) were born to vaccinated mothers and 53 (59%) to unvaccinated mothers. The number of infants younger than 2 months with RSV infection was lower in the vaccinated group than in the unvaccinated group (4 vs 11 children).
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest an association between maternal RSV vaccination and reduced disease severity and healthcare utilisation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform future strategies for RSV prevention and management in infants, particularly regarding the potential benefits of maternal vaccination in reducing the need for respiratory support and shortening hospital stays.
Limitations and evidence strength
This was an observational analysis, and potential confounding factors were not included, including prematurity, underlying comorbidities and the timing of maternal RSV vaccination. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature and lack of control for confounding variables.

两岁以下住院儿童 RSV 阳性和 RSV 阴性 毛细支气管炎 的临床和实验室特征:比较横断面研究

Clinical and Laboratory Characteristics of RSV-Positive and RSV-Negative Bronchiolitis in Hospitalized Children Younger Than Two Years: A Comparative Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Shehreen Iqbal, Amina Mobeen, Hina Naveed, Sidra Nisar, Sidra Farooq, Shahzad Munir, Dr. Sidra Farooq
摘要中文翻译目的:比较 RSV 阳性和 RSV 阴性 毛细支气管炎 住院儿童的人口统计学、临床和实验室特征。方法:这项比较横断面研究纳入了 2025 年 11 月至 2026 年 4 月期间因 毛细支气管炎 入住伊斯兰堡一家三级医院的 60 名两岁以下儿童。RSV 状态通过病毒聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 测定,每组 30 名儿童。使用 Wood-Downes-Ferrés (WDF) 评分评估临床严重程度。根据可用的组摘要重新计算双边比较。结果:RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 儿童比 RSV 阴性儿童年轻(3.9 ± 3.2 个月 vs 8.9 ± 9.8 个月;p=0.012)。咳嗽和发烧在两组中都很常见。RSV 阴性组更常报告两种以上的症状(73.3% vs 43.3%;p=0.035)。RSV 阳性组的平均 WDF 评分较高(5.9 ± 2.2 vs 4.4 ± 2.4;p=0.014)。白细胞总数在数值上较高(12.2 ± 3.7 vs 10.5 ± 3.1 ×109/L),但重新计算时这种差异并不具有统计学意义(p=0.059)。两组的 C 反应蛋白阳性率相同 (50.0%)。结论:RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 住院儿童年龄较小,未经调整的床边呼吸严重程度评分较高。出现的症状和血常规检查结果有很大重叠。没有定量 PCR 病毒载量信息,也无法重建年龄或性别分层评估。应谨慎解释这些发现,因为无法获得年龄调整分析和个人严重程度类别。该研究描述了住院组之间的差异,而不是证明在单中心比较中 RSV 独立决定入院时的严重程度。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在比较 RSV 阳性和 RSV 阴性 毛细支气管炎 住院儿童的人口统计学、临床和实验室特征。
设计与对象
在伊斯兰堡的一家三级医院进行了一项横断面比较研究,纳入了 2025 年 11 月至 2026 年 4 月期间因 毛细支气管炎 入院的 60 名两岁以下儿童,其中 RSV 阳性组和 RSV 阴性组各 30 名儿童。
核心方法
RSV 状态通过病毒聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 确定,并使用 Wood-Downes-Ferrés (WDF) 评分评估临床严重程度。根据可用的组摘要重新计算双边比较。没有定量 PCR 病毒载量信息,也无法重建年龄或性别分层评估。
主要结果
RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 儿童比 RSV 阴性儿童年轻(3.9 ± 3.2 个月 vs 8.9 ± 9.8 个月;p=0.012)。RSV 阴性组更常报告两种以上的症状(73.3% vs 43.3%;p=0.035)。RSV 阳性组的平均 WDF 评分较高(5.9 ± 2.2 vs 4.4 ± 2.4;p=0.014)。白细胞总数在数值上较高(12.2 ± 3.7 vs 10.5 ± 3.1 ×109/L),但重新计算时这种差异并不具有统计学意义(p=0.059)。两组的 C 反应蛋白阳性率相同 (50.0%)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,RSV 阳性 毛细支气管炎 住院儿童年龄较小,未经调整的床边呼吸道严重程度评分较高,同时出现的症状和常规血液检查结果有很大重叠。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于了解住院儿童的 RSV 相关临床特征,可能有助于制定针对性的 监测 和 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的管理策略。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏年龄调整分析和个体严重程度类别,并且证据强度中等,因此应谨慎解释该研究的结果。

Clinical and Laboratory Characteristics of RSV-Positive and RSV-Negative Bronchiolitis in Hospitalized Children Younger Than Two Years: A Comparative Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Shehreen Iqbal, Amina Mobeen, Hina Naveed, Sidra Nisar, Sidra Farooq, Shahzad Munir, Dr. Sidra Farooq
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To compare the demographic, clinical and laboratory characteristics of hospitalized children with RSV-positive and RSV-negative bronchiolitis. Methodology: This comparative cross-sectional study included 60 children younger than two years admitted with bronchiolitis to a tertiary-care hospital in Islamabad from November 2025 to April 2026. RSV status was determined by viral polymerase chain reaction (PCR), with 30 children in each group. Clinical severity was assessed using the Wood-Downes-Ferrés (WDF) score. Two-sided comparisons were recalculated from available group summaries. Results: Children with RSV-positive bronchiolitis were younger than RSV-negative children (3.9 ± 3.2 vs 8.9 ± 9.8 months; p=0.012). Cough and fever were common in both groups. More than two presenting symptoms were reported more often in the RSV-negative group (73.3% vs 43.3%; p=0.035). Mean WDF score was higher in the RSV-positive group (5.9 ± 2.2 vs 4.4 ± 2.4; p=0.014). Total leukocyte count was numerically higher (12.2 ± 3.7 vs 10.5 ± 3.1 ×109/L), although this difference was not statistically significant on recalculation (p=0.059). C-reactive protein positivity was identical in both groups (50.0%). Conclusions: Hospitalized children with RSV-positive bronchiolitis were younger and had higher unadjusted bedside respiratory severity scores. Presenting symptoms and routine blood findings overlapped substantially. No quantitative PCR viral-load information was available, and no age- or sex-stratified assessment could be reconstructed. These findings should be interpreted cautiously because age-adjusted analyses and individual severity categories were unavailable. The study describes differences between hospitalized groups rather than proving that RSV independently determines severity at the time of admission in this single-center comparison.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to compare the demographic, clinical and laboratory characteristics of hospitalized children with RSV-positive and RSV-negative bronchiolitis.
Design and population
A comparative cross-sectional study was conducted at a tertiary-care hospital in Islamabad, involving 60 children younger than two years admitted with bronchiolitis from November 2025 to April 2026, with 30 children in each RSV-positive and RSV-negative group.
Core methods
RSV status was determined by viral polymerase chain reaction (PCR), and clinical severity was assessed using the Wood-Downes-Ferrés (WDF) score. Two-sided comparisons were recalculated from available group summaries. No quantitative PCR viral-load information was available, and no age- or sex-stratified assessment could be reconstructed.
Main results
Children with RSV-positive bronchiolitis were younger than RSV-negative children (3.9 ± 3.2 vs 8.9 ± 9.8 months; p=0.012). More than two presenting symptoms were reported more often in the RSV-negative group (73.3% vs 43.3%; p=0.035). Mean WDF score was higher in the RSV-positive group (5.9 ± 2.2 vs 4.4 ± 2.4; p=0.014). Total leukocyte count was numerically higher (12.2 ± 3.7 vs 10.5 ± 3.1 ×109/L), although this difference was not statistically significant on recalculation (p=0.059). C-reactive protein positivity was identical in both groups (50.0%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that hospitalized children with RSV-positive bronchiolitis were younger and had higher unadjusted bedside respiratory severity scores, while presenting symptoms and routine blood findings overlapped substantially.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the understanding of RSV-related clinical features in hospitalized children, potentially aiding in the development of targeted surveillance and management strategies for RSV bronchiolitis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings should be interpreted cautiously due to the lack of age-adjusted analyses and individual severity categories, and the evidence is moderate in strength.

儿童呼吸道病原体阳性:2024-2025 年中国杭州一家儿童医院的模式转变

Respiratory Pathogen Positivity in Children: Shifting Patterns at a Single Children’s Hospital in Hangzhou, China, 2024–2025
作者： Bi-Wen Yao, Xue-Bin Zhou, 张晓燕, Qin‐rui Lai, Wei Li, Jia-Bin Li, Xiao-Yan Zhang
摘要中文翻译呼吸道感染是儿童常见的疾病,对儿科健康构成重要威胁。因此,监测循环病原体谱的时间变化对于提供有效的预防和 治疗 策略非常重要。为了评估 2024 年至 2025 年受测儿童呼吸道病原体检测阳性的变化,我们检查了从中国东部一家大型儿童医院就诊的患者获得的呼吸道病原体检测数据,结果根据病原体、年龄组和发病月份进行分层。人鼻病毒(HRV)仍然是 2024 年(10,268/47,650;21.55%)和 2025 年(10,634/50,627;21.00%)最常见的病原体。2024年,肺炎支原体(MP)排名第二(9663/47,650;20.28%),而2025年其检出率大幅下降(1647/50,627;3.25%)。相比之下,呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV;8058/50,627;15.92%)和甲型流感病毒(InfA;4424/50,627;8.74%)在2025年变得更加突出,分别占据HRV之后的第二和第三位。1-3 岁儿童在呼吸道感染中所占比例始终较高。然而,超过 3 岁后,病原体谱发生了变化,最明显的变化是 InfA 感染与年龄相关的增加。与 2024 年相比,2025 年冬季记录的病例数也大幅增加。气候变化、疫苗接种 覆盖范围和其他因素在这些流行病学变化中的潜在作用需要进一步调查。这些单中心测试阳性结果不应被解释为受测儿童的区域阳性率。因此,儿童呼吸道感染的预防和控制措施应考虑病原体谱的变化,特别是在高危年龄组和季节性高峰期间。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道感染是儿童常见的疾病,对儿科健康构成重要威胁。
设计与对象
为了评估 2024 年至 2025 年受测儿童呼吸道病原体检测阳性的变化,我们检查了从中国东部一家大型儿童医院就诊的患者获得的呼吸道病原体检测数据,结果根据病原体、年龄组和发病月份进行分层。
核心方法
该研究分析了中国杭州一家儿童医院 2024 年至 2025 年期间的呼吸道病原体检测数据,按病原体、年龄组和发病月份对结果进行分层。
主要结果
人鼻病毒(HRV)仍然是 2024 年(10,268/47,650;21.55%)和 2025 年(10,634/50,627;21.00%)最常见的病原体。2024年,肺炎支原体(MP)排名第二(9663/47,650;20.28%),而2025年其检出率大幅下降(1647/50,627;3.25%)。
解释与创新
相比之下,呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV;8058/50,627;15.92%)和甲型流感病毒(InfA;4424/50,627;8.74%)在2025年变得更加突出,分别占据HRV之后的第二和第三位。
科研与公卫意义
因此,儿童呼吸道感染的预防和控制措施应考虑病原体谱的变化,特别是在高危年龄组和季节性高峰期间。
局限与证据强度
这些单中心测试阳性结果不应被解释为受测儿童的区域阳性率。

Respiratory Pathogen Positivity in Children: Shifting Patterns at a Single Children’s Hospital in Hangzhou, China, 2024–2025

Authors: Bi-Wen Yao, Xue-Bin Zhou, 张晓燕, Qin‐rui Lai, Wei Li, Jia-Bin Li, Xiao-Yan Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory infections are frequently encountered in children and constitute an important threat to pediatric health. Monitoring temporal shifts in the circulating pathogen spectrum is consequently important for informing effective prevention and treatment strategies. To assess changes in respiratory pathogen test positivity among tested children during 2024–2025, we examined respiratory pathogen testing data obtained from patients attending a large children’s hospital in eastern China, with results stratified according to pathogen, age group, and month of onset. Human rhinovirus (HRV) remained the most commonly detected pathogen in both 2024 (10,268/47,650; 21.55%) and 2025 (10,634/50,627; 21.00%). In 2024, Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP) ranked second (9663/47,650; 20.28%), whereas its detection rate decreased substantially in 2025 (1647/50,627; 3.25%). In contrast, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV; 8058/50,627; 15.92%) and influenza A virus (InfA; 4424/50,627; 8.74%) became more prominent in 2025, occupying the second and third positions, respectively, after HRV. Children aged 1–3 years represented a consistently large proportion of respiratory infections. Beyond 3 years of age, however, the pathogen profile shifted, with the most evident change being an age-related rise in InfA infections. The number of cases recorded during winter also increased substantially in 2025 relative to 2024. The potential roles of climate variability, vaccination coverage, and other factors in these epidemiological changes require further investigation. These single-center test-positive findings should not be interpreted as regional positivity rate among tested children. Prevention and control measures for pediatric respiratory infections should therefore account for changes in the pathogen spectrum, particularly among high-risk age groups and during seasonal peaks.

Question and background
Respiratory infections are frequently encountered in children and constitute an important threat to pediatric health.
Design and population
To assess changes in respiratory pathogen test positivity among tested children during 2024–2025, we examined respiratory pathogen testing data obtained from patients attending a large children’s hospital in eastern China, with results stratified according to pathogen, age group, and month of onset.
Core methods
The study analyzed respiratory pathogen testing data from a single children’s hospital in Hangzhou, China, during 2024–2025, stratifying results by pathogen, age group, and month of onset.
Main results
Human rhinovirus (HRV) remained the most commonly detected pathogen in both 2024 (10,268/47,650; 21.55%) and 2025 (10,634/50,627; 21.00%). In 2024, Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP) ranked second (9663/47,650; 20.28%), whereas its detection rate decreased substantially in 2025 (1647/50,627; 3.25%).
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
Prevention and control measures for pediatric respiratory infections should therefore account for changes in the pathogen spectrum, particularly among high-risk age groups and during seasonal peaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
These single-center test-positive findings should not be interpreted as regional positivity rate among tested children.
