大流行后 ALRTI 住院儿童呼吸道病原体和外周免疫相关性的变化:2023-2024 年中国北方地区回顾性分子 监测 研究

Post-pandemic shifts in respiratory pathogens and peripheral immune correlates among hospitalized children with ALRTI: a retrospective molecular surveillance study in northern China, 2023–2024
作者： Jie Yuan, Wei Sun, Sheng-Wang Ge, 安淑华, Bo-Juan Zheng, Bo Niu, Shu-Hua An
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
大流行后呼吸道病原体循环发生了变化,但同时发生的分子和免疫数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
我们研究了 2023 年至 2024 年中国北方因急性下呼吸道感染 (ALRTI) 住院儿童的病原体趋势和外周免疫相关性。
核心方法
这项单中心回顾性研究包括 604 例住院病例。13 靶标多重核酸组定义了病原体组。对单一感染、混合感染(≥2种病原体类别)和阴性组的免疫球蛋白、淋巴细胞亚群和炎症标志物进行了比较。使用具有聚类稳健标准误差的回归来调整年龄、性别和年份的免疫比较,以进行重复入院和错误发现率校正。使用年龄标准化率和针对年龄、性别和日历月进行调整的逻辑回归来评估年度趋势。
主要结果
Among 601 complete-panel episodes, 528 (87.9%) had a detected pathogen. Mycoplasma pneumoniae predominated (43.4%), followed by rhinovirus (23.6%) and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV; 14.3%). pneumoniae detection increased from 32.4% in 2023 to 54.3% in 2024, while RSV decreased from 21.4% to 7.3% (both q < 0.001). Annual shifts persisted after age standardisation and covariate adjustment: adjusted odds ratios for 2024 vs. 2023 were 3.09 (95% CI, 1.94–4.94) for M. pneumoniae, 0.29 (0.16–0.53) for RSV and 0.13 (0.03–0.53) for influenza A.
解释与创新
2023 年至 2024 年间,病原体谱发生了显着变化,支持了持续的分子 监测 和对免疫措施的年龄感知解释。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果谨慎地告知需要持续进行分子 监测 和呼吸道感染免疫相关性的年龄特异性解释。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单中心设计和重复入院的潜在偏差而受到限制。由于依赖回顾性数据并使用调整后的比值比,证据强度中等。

Post-pandemic shifts in respiratory pathogens and peripheral immune correlates among hospitalized children with ALRTI: a retrospective molecular surveillance study in northern China, 2023–2024

Authors: Jie Yuan, Wei Sun, Sheng-Wang Ge, 安淑华, Bo-Juan Zheng, Bo Niu, Shu-Hua An
Original abstract (English)
Background Post-pandemic respiratory pathogen circulation has changed, but concurrent molecular and immune data remain limited. We examined pathogen trends and peripheral immune correlates in children hospitalized with acute lower respiratory tract infection (ALRTI) in northern China during 2023–2024. Methods This single-centre retrospective study included 604 hospitalization episodes. A 13-target multiplex nucleic-acid panel defined pathogen groups. Immunoglobulins, lymphocyte subsets and inflammatory markers were compared across monoinfection, mixed infection (≥2 pathogen categories) and panel-negative groups. Immune comparisons adjusted for age, sex and year using regression with cluster-robust standard errors for repeated admissions and false-discovery-rate correction. Annual trends were assessed using age-standardised rates and logistic regression adjusted for age, sex and calendar month. Results Among 601 complete-panel episodes, 528 (87.9%) had a detected pathogen. Mycoplasma pneumoniae predominated (43.4%), followed by rhinovirus (23.6%) and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV; 14.3%). pneumoniae detection increased from 32.4% in 2023 to 54.3% in 2024, while RSV decreased from 21.4% to 7.3% (both q < 0.001). Annual shifts persisted after age standardisation and covariate adjustment: adjusted odds ratios for 2024 vs. 2023 were 3.09 (95% CI, 1.94–4.94) for M. pneumoniae, 0.29 (0.16–0.53) for RSV and 0.13 (0.03–0.53) for influenza A. Mixed infection (21.5%) decreased with age (adjusted odds ratio per year, 0.92; 95% CI, 0.86–0.98). After adjustment and multiplicity correction, M. pneumoniae and mixed infections were associated with higher immunoglobulin M. Rhinovirus monoinfection was associated with lower total T-cell and CD8+ T-cell percentages and higher B-cell percentage; mixed infection was associated with lower total T-cell percentage. Cytokine analyses were exploratory because approximately 35% of episodes were tested. Conclusion Pathogen profiles shifted substantially between 2023 and 2024, supporting continued molecular surveillance and age-aware interpretation of immune measures. Because the panel did not cover typical bacterial pathogens, panel-negative episodes should not be equated with absence of infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Post-pandemic respiratory pathogen circulation has changed, but concurrent molecular and immune data remain limited.
Design and population
We examined pathogen trends and peripheral immune correlates in children hospitalized with acute lower respiratory tract infection (ALRTI) in northern China during 2023–2024.
Core methods
This single-centre retrospective study included 604 hospitalization episodes. A 13-target multiplex nucleic-acid panel defined pathogen groups. Immunoglobulins, lymphocyte subsets and inflammatory markers were compared across monoinfection, mixed infection (≥2 pathogen categories) and panel-negative groups. Immune comparisons adjusted for age, sex and year using regression with cluster-robust standard errors for repeated admissions and false-discovery-rate correction. Annual trends were assessed using age-standardised rates and logistic regression adjusted for age, sex and calendar month.
Main results
Among 601 complete-panel episodes, 528 (87.9%) had a detected pathogen. Mycoplasma pneumoniae predominated (43.4%), followed by rhinovirus (23.6%) and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV; 14.3%). pneumoniae detection increased from 32.4% in 2023 to 54.3% in 2024, while RSV decreased from 21.4% to 7.3% (both q < 0.001). Annual shifts persisted after age standardisation and covariate adjustment: adjusted odds ratios for 2024 vs. 2023 were 3.09 (95% CI, 1.94–4.94) for M. pneumoniae, 0.29 (0.16–0.53) for RSV and 0.13 (0.03–0.53) for influenza A.
Interpretation and novelty
Pathogen profiles shifted substantially between 2023 and 2024, supporting continued molecular surveillance and age-aware interpretation of immune measures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings cautiously inform the need for ongoing molecular surveillance and age-specific interpretation of immune correlates in respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-centre design and potential biases from repeated admissions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on retrospective data and the use of adjusted odds ratios.

鼻咽拭子冷冻保存对常见呼吸道病毒分子靶标检测的影响

Impact of Nasopharyngeal Swab Freezing Storage on the Detection of Common Respiratory Viral Molecular Targets
作者： Andrea Matucci, Fabio Formenti, Katrine Barbero, Giulia Gambini, Lavinia Nicolini, Francesca Perandin, Chiara Piubelli
摘要中文翻译存档的呼吸道标本是用于回顾性诊断研究和测定验证的宝贵生物样本。然而,长期储存可能会影响病毒核酸完整性和分子检测,这对于 RNA 病毒来说尤其重要,其 RNA 基因组 可能特别容易受到储存相关降解的影响。本研究评估了鼻咽拭子 (NPS) 等分试样的质量和稳定性,该等分试样在我们的生物库中于 -80 °C 下保存至少一年,随后重新测试相同的病毒 RNA 靶标。使用全自动样本到结果分子平台重新测试之前针对 SARS-CoV-2、流感病毒或 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 进行的 NPS,该平台以不同的多重检测格式集成了核酸提取、扩增和病毒靶点检测。由于使用与相应新鲜样品所用的检测方法不同的检测方法对储存的样品进行测试,因此使用第三种独立的分子检测方法进一步评估了不一致的结果。将定性结果与之前的测试进行比较以评估诊断一致性,并分析循环阈值 (Ct) 值的变化。目标 SARS-CoV-2 显示出最高的稳定性,各种检测格式的阳性一致性百分比 (PPA) 为 97.1-98.6%,Ct 增加 2.08-3.03 个循环。RSV 显示出中等稳定性,PPA 为 88.4-97.1%,Ct 增加了 1.75-5.20 个循环。流感显示出最大的信号损失,PPA 为 80.0-92.9%,平均 Ct 增加 5.47-7.92 个周期。对于所有病毒靶点来说,储存后的 Ct 增加都很显着。测定配置之间的差异表明测定配方和目标特性可能会影响存储后的性能。尽管与存储相关的信号损失根据病毒靶点和检测配置而变化,但储存在-80°C下的呼吸道拭子等分试样可以支持回顾性分子检测。在解释存档样本的结果时应考虑这些影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估长期冷冻储存对病毒分子靶点检测的影响,特别是呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV),因为储存可能会影响核酸完整性和分子检测。
设计与对象
该研究涉及重新测试在生物库中在 -80°C 下保存至少一年的鼻咽拭子 (NPS) 等分试样,重点关注 RSV 和其他常见呼吸道病毒。
核心方法
使用全自动样本到结果分子平台对存储的 NPS 样本进行重新测试,并使用第三个独立分子测定进一步评估不一致的结果,以确保准确性。
主要结果
RSV 显示出中等稳定性,正一致性百分比 (PPA) 为 88.4-97.1%,Ct 增加为 1.75-5.20 个循环。流感显示出最大的信号损失,PPA 为 80.0-92.9%,平均 Ct 增加为 5.47-7.92 个周期。
解释与创新
该研究强调,与存储相关的信号丢失因病毒靶标和检测配置而异,强调需要仔细解释存档的样本。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,应谨慎使用存档的呼吸道标本进行分子检测,尤其是 RSV 检测,并表明在诊断准确性中考虑特定检测因素的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于存档的标本,没有考虑实时病毒载量或环境因素,但由于受控设置和多重测定验证,证据是温和的。

Impact of Nasopharyngeal Swab Freezing Storage on the Detection of Common Respiratory Viral Molecular Targets

Authors: Andrea Matucci, Fabio Formenti, Katrine Barbero, Giulia Gambini, Lavinia Nicolini, Francesca Perandin, Chiara Piubelli
Original abstract (English)
Archived respiratory specimens are valuable biological samples for retrospective diagnostic studies and assay validation. However, long-term storage may affect viral nucleic acid integrity and molecular detection, which is an issue of particular relevance for RNA viruses, whose RNA genomes may be particularly susceptible to storage-related degradation. The present study evaluates the quality and stability of nasopharyngeal swab (NPS) aliquots stored at −80 °C for at least one year in our biobank and subsequently retested for the same viral RNA targets. NPS previously tested for SARS-CoV-2, influenza virus, or respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) were retested using a fully automated sample-to-result molecular platform integrating nucleic acid extraction, amplification, and viral target detection in different multiplex assay formats. As the stored samples were tested using an assay different from the one used for the corresponding fresh samples, discordant results were further assessed using a third independent molecular assay. Qualitative results were compared with previous testing to assess diagnostic agreement, and changes in cycle threshold (Ct) values were analyzed. Target SARS-CoV-2 showed the highest stability, with positive percent agreement (PPA) of 97.1–98.6% and Ct increases of 2.08–3.03 cycles across assay formats. RSV showed intermediate stability, with a PPA of 88.4–97.1% and Ct increases of 1.75–5.20 cycles. Influenza showed the greatest signal loss, with PPA of 80.0–92.9% and mean Ct increases of 5.47–7.92 cycles. The Ct increases after storage were significant for all viral targets. Differences between assay configurations indicated that assay formulation and target characteristics may influence post-storage performance. Respiratory swab aliquots stored at −80° C can support retrospective molecular testing, although storage-related signal loss varies according to the viral target and assay configuration. These effects should be considered when interpreting results from archived specimens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of long-term freezing storage on the detection of viral molecular targets, particularly for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), as storage may affect nucleic acid integrity and molecular detection.
Design and population
The study involved retesting nasopharyngeal swab (NPS) aliquots stored at −80 °C for at least one year in a biobank, focusing on RSV and other common respiratory viruses.
Core methods
Stored NPS samples were retested using a fully automated sample-to-result molecular platform, and discordant results were further assessed with a third independent molecular assay to ensure accuracy.
Main results
RSV showed intermediate stability with a positive percent agreement (PPA) of 88.4–97.1% and Ct increases of 1.75–5.20 cycles. Influenza showed the greatest signal loss with a PPA of 80.0–92.9% and mean Ct increases of 5.47–7.92 cycles.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that storage-related signal loss varies depending on the viral target and assay configuration, emphasizing the need for careful interpretation of archived specimens.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the cautious use of archived respiratory specimens for molecular testing, particularly for RSV, and suggest the importance of considering assay-specific factors in diagnostic accuracy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to archived specimens and does not account for real-time viral load or environmental factors, but the evidence is moderate due to the controlled setting and multiple assay validations.

影响母亲的真空和乳汁对支气管炎的影响,并影响呼吸系统。佩雷拉·罗塞尔中心医院。乌拉圭。因维耶诺,2025

Impacto de la vacunación materna en lactantes con bronquiolitis ingresados con soporte respiratorio. Centro Hospitalario Pereira Rossell. Uruguay. Invierno, 2025
作者： ANA PAULA FUMEAUX, NOELIA GORDIENKO, Ana Paula Hernández, Alfonsina Rígoli, SOFÍA POSE, MARÍA VICTORIA SELLANES, Lorena Pardo, HECTOR TELECHEA
摘要中文翻译旨在分析 2025 年冬季在佩雷拉·罗塞尔医院中心,母体 疫苗接种 对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 灌注蛋白 F 对需要呼吸支持的 1 岁以下婴儿 毛细支气管炎 的影响。进行了一项分析、观察、纵向、双向 队列研究 研究,包括 12 个月以下需要呼吸支持的 毛细支气管炎 婴儿支持,根据产妇疫苗接种状态分为两组。分析了与更严重的疾病进展和严重程度相关的临床特征。定量变量以中位数和四分位数范围或平均值和标准差的形式呈现,定性变量以绝对和相对频率的形式呈现。根据变量类型,应用曼-惠特尼 U 检验、t 检验、费舍尔精确检验和卡方检验来比较两组(母亲接种疫苗的患者与母亲未接种疫苗的患者)。p 值<0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。共纳入 226 名患者,其中大部分为男性(136 名,60%),其中 176 名(79%)患有 RSV。接种疫苗的母亲比例为 44 人(19%)。母亲接种疫苗的患者的中位年龄(3 个月,范围 2-5)低于 5 个月(范围 3-9),p < 0.001。入院时的临床变量或与较差结果相关的临床变量在各组之间没有观察到显着差异。结论:母亲未接种疫苗的婴儿比例较低,可能表明该 疫苗 对住院风险的影响更大。为了更好地理解这一策略,需要包括其他变量在内的进一步研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估母亲 疫苗接种 与 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 对需要呼吸支持的 1 岁以下婴儿的影响。这是因为小婴儿因 RSV 相关住院的负担很高。
设计与对象
2025 年冬季,乌拉圭佩雷拉·罗塞尔医院中心进行了一项观察性、纵向、双重性 队列研究 研究,研究对象包括 12 个月以下、需要呼吸支持的 毛细支气管炎 婴儿,并按母亲 疫苗接种 状态进行分层。
核心方法
该研究使用以中位数和四分位数范围或平均值和标准差表示的定量变量,以及以绝对和相对频率表示的定性变量,分析了与疾病进展和严重程度相关的临床特征。采用Mann-Whitney U检验、t检验、Fisher精确检验和卡方检验等统计检验来比较各组。
主要结果
与母亲未接种疫苗的婴儿(5 个月,范围 3-9)相比,母亲接种疫苗的婴儿(3 个月,范围 2-5)的婴儿中位年龄显着较低,p < 0.001。入院时的临床变量或与较差结果相关的临床变量在各组之间没有观察到显着差异。
解释与创新
作者认为,母亲未接种疫苗的婴儿比例较低可能表明母亲 疫苗接种 对 RSV 具有潜在的保护作用。然而,他们强调需要进一步研究以更好地理解这一策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能为有关孕产妇 疫苗接种 的公共卫生策略提供信息,以降低婴儿因 RSV 相关住院的风险。他们强调了在 RSV 毛细支气管炎 管理中考虑母亲 疫苗接种 状态的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,并未建立因果关系。由于缺乏对混杂变量的控制,证据受到限制,由于可能存在偏差,结果应谨慎解释。

Impacto de la vacunación materna en lactantes con bronquiolitis ingresados con soporte respiratorio. Centro Hospitalario Pereira Rossell. Uruguay. Invierno, 2025

Authors: ANA PAULA FUMEAUX, NOELIA GORDIENKO, Ana Paula Hernández, Alfonsina Rígoli, SOFÍA POSE, MARÍA VICTORIA SELLANES, Lorena Pardo, HECTOR TELECHEA
Original abstract (English)
To analyze the impact of maternal vaccination against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) perfusion protein F in infants under one year of age with bronchiolitis requiring respiratory support at the Pereira Rossell Hospital Center during the winter of 2025. An analytical, observational, longitudinal, ambispective cohort study was conducted, including infants under 12 months of age with bronchiolitis requiring respiratory support, divided into two groups according to maternal vaccination status. Clinical characteristics associated with worse disease progression and severity were analyzed. Quantitative variables were presented as medians and interquartile ranges or means and standard deviations, and qualitative variables as absolute and relative frequencies. Mann-Whitney U test, t-test, Fisher’s exact test, and chi-square test were applied, depending on the type of variable, to compare both groups (patients whose mothers were vaccinated vs. those whose mothers were not). A p-value <0.05 was considered statistically significant. Two hundred and twenty-six patients were included, mostly male (136, 60%), and 176 (79%) had RSV. The proportion of vaccinated mothers was 44 (19%). The median age was lower in patients whose mothers were vaccinated (3 months, range 2–5) vs. 5 months (range 3–9), p < 0.001. No significant differences were observed between the groups for clinical variables at admission or those associated with a worse outcome. Conclusions: The lower proportion of infants with unvaccinated mothers may suggest a greater impact of this vaccine on the risk of hospitalization. Further studies including other variables are needed for a better understanding of this strategy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of maternal vaccination against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) on infants under one year of age with bronchiolitis requiring respiratory support. This is relevant due to the high burden of RSV-related hospitalizations in young infants.
Design and population
An observational, longitudinal, ambispective cohort study was conducted at the Pereira Rossell Hospital Center in Uruguay during the winter of 2025, including infants under 12 months of age with bronchiolitis requiring respiratory support, stratified by maternal vaccination status.
Core methods
The study analyzed clinical characteristics associated with disease progression and severity, using quantitative variables presented as medians and interquartile ranges or means and standard deviations, and qualitative variables as absolute and relative frequencies. Statistical tests including Mann-Whitney U test, t-test, Fisher’s exact test, and chi-square test were applied to compare the groups.
Main results
The median age was significantly lower in infants whose mothers were vaccinated (3 months, range 2–5) compared to those whose mothers were not (5 months, range 3–9), p < 0.001. No significant differences were observed between the groups for clinical variables at admission or those associated with a worse outcome.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that the lower proportion of infants with unvaccinated mothers may indicate a potential protective effect of maternal vaccination against RSV. However, they emphasize the need for further studies to better understand this strategy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform public health strategies regarding maternal vaccination to reduce the risk of RSV-related hospitalizations in infants. They highlight the importance of considering maternal vaccination status in the management of RSV bronchiolitis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and does not establish causality. The evidence is limited by the lack of control for confounding variables, and the results should be interpreted with caution due to the potential for bias.

毛细支气管炎 婴儿和儿童无创通气 (NIV) 失败的预测因素

PREDICTORS OF NON-INVASIVE VENTILATION (NIV) FAILURE IN INFANTS AND CHILDREN WITH BRONCHIOLITIS
作者： Iman Al Habsi, Saif Awlad Thani, Aziza Al Hashmi, Balqis Al Ghelani, Asma Al Naabi, Maryam Al Hakmani, Asma Al Mayahi, A. Alhashmi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
毛细支气管炎 是婴幼儿住院和重症监护的主要原因。无创通气 (NIV) 越来越多地用于呼吸支持,但 毛细支气管炎 中 NIV 失败的预测因素仍未完全确定。
设计与对象
一项双向研究在阿曼皇家医院进行,历时 2023 年 10 月至 2026 年 2 月的三个 毛细支气管炎 季节。纳入了因 毛细支气管炎 需要 NIV 而入住儿科重症监护病房或高度依赖病房的 2 岁以下儿童。
核心方法
收集人口统计学、临床、放射学、病毒学和生理学变量。NIV 失败被定义为需要气管插管。进行单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析以确定 NIV 失败的独立预测因素。
主要结果
Of 330 children, 46 (13.9%) required intubation, while 284 (86.1%) were successfully managed with NIV. Independent predictors of NIV failure were RSV infection (aOR 2.75, 95% CI 1.25–6.06; P=0.012), radiographic lobar collapse (aOR 3.81, 95% CI 1.70–8.52; P=0.001), apnea (aOR 4.71, 95% CI 1.39–15.99; P=0.013), and higher pCO2 one hour after NIV initiation (aOR 1.05 per mmHg, 95% CI 1.01–1.10; P=0.010).
解释与创新
RSV 感染、肺叶塌陷、呼吸暂停和 NIV 开始后 1 小时较高的 pCO2 与 NIV 失败独立相关。
科研与公卫意义
及早识别这些预测因素可能有助于识别需要更密切监测并及时升级呼吸支持的儿童。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

PREDICTORS OF NON-INVASIVE VENTILATION (NIV) FAILURE IN INFANTS AND CHILDREN WITH BRONCHIOLITIS

Authors: Iman Al Habsi, Saif Awlad Thani, Aziza Al Hashmi, Balqis Al Ghelani, Asma Al Naabi, Maryam Al Hakmani, Asma Al Mayahi, A. Alhashmi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Bronchiolitis is a major cause of hospitalization and critical care admission in infants and young children. Non-invasive ventilation (NIV) is increasingly used for respiratory support, but predictors of NIV failure in bronchiolitis remain incompletely defined. This study aimed to identify predictors of NIV failure in children younger than 2 years hospitalized with bronchiolitis. Methods: A bidirectional study was conducted at the Royal Hospital, Oman, over three bronchiolitis seasons from October 2023 to February 2026. Children younger than 2 years admitted to the pediatric intensive care unit or high-dependency unit with bronchiolitis requiring NIV were included. Demographic, clinical, radiological, virological, and physiological variables were collected. NIV failure was defined as the need for endotracheal intubation. Univariable and multivariable logistic regression analyses were performed to identify independent predictors of NIV failure. Results: Of 330 children, 46 (13.9%) required intubation, while 284 (86.1%) were successfully managed with NIV. Independent predictors of NIV failure were RSV infection (aOR 2.75, 95% CI 1.25–6.06; P=0.012), radiographic lobar collapse (aOR 3.81, 95% CI 1.70–8.52; P=0.001), apnea (aOR 4.71, 95% CI 1.39–15.99; P=0.013), and higher pCO2 one hour after NIV initiation (aOR 1.05 per mmHg, 95% CI 1.01–1.10; P=0.010). Median time to intubation was 15 hours. Patients who failed NIV had a longer median critical care stay than those who succeeded (9.5 vs 5 days; P<0.001). Conclusion: NIV was successful in most children with bronchiolitis. RSV infection, lobar collapse, apnea, and higher pCO2 one hour after NIV initiation were independently associated with NIV failure. Early recognition of these predictors may help identify children requiring closer monitoring and timely escalation of respiratory support.

Bronchiolitis is a major cause of hospitalization and critical care admission in infants and young children. Non-invasive ventilation (NIV) is increasingly used for respiratory support, but predictors of NIV failure in bronchiolitis remain incompletely defined.

Independent predictors of NIV failure were RSV infection (aOR 2.75, 95% CI 1.25–6.06; P=0.012), radiographic lobar collapse (aOR 3.81, 95% CI 1.70–8.52; P=0.001), apnea (aOR 4.71, 95% CI 1.39–15.99; P=0.013), and higher pCO2 one hour after NIV initiation (aOR 1.05 per mmHg, 95% CI 1.01–1.10; P=0.010).

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

推进 疫苗 佐剂:监管框架和发展策略

Advancing Vaccine Adjuvants: Regulatory Frameworks and Developmental Strategies
作者： Yousuf Naqvi, Wolfgang W. Leitner, Nathalie Garçon, Ramin Sabet-Azad, Antonio Guzzi, Daniel J. Fullen, Dennis Christensen, Syed Hassan Askary, Victoria Graham, Adam Hacker
摘要中文翻译佐剂是大多数 疫苗 制剂的组成部分,主要用作抗原的递送系统和免疫刺激剂。铝盐是第一个被批准用于人类 疫苗 的佐剂,目前仍然是 EMA 和 FDA 许可的 疫苗 中使用最广泛的佐剂。佐剂增强疫苗诱导的免疫应答;提高疫苗的疗效,特别是在老年人和婴儿等易感染人群中;提供抗原剂量节约;并塑造 疫苗 诱导反应的免疫特征。疫苗 的有效性主要归因于作为活性药物成分(API)的抗原和作为 疫苗 制剂中的组成材料的佐剂。佐剂通过激活先天免疫系统并增强 免疫应答 对共递送抗原的适应性来增强 疫苗 的有效性。佐剂充当免疫刺激剂或递送系统,通常通过促进抗原呈递细胞 (APC) 摄取抗原并触发细胞因子和趋化因子释放来改善 B 和 T 细胞活化。佐剂可以是内源性的,如在活的、减毒的、基于病原体的疫苗中发现的,也可以是外源的,添加到例如蛋白质亚基或基于mRNA的疫苗中以增强免疫原性。在过去的几十年里,新型外源佐剂已成为新 疫苗 开发的关键,既可作为递送系统,又可作为免疫刺激剂。值得注意的例子包括用于季节性和大流行性流感 疫苗 的 MF59,以及用于带状疱疹、疟疾和 RSV 疫苗 的 AS01。COVID-19大流行不仅加速了脂质纳米粒子(LNP)佐剂的mRNA 疫苗的开发和使用,而且加速了已授权疫苗中已存在的新型佐剂(例如Matrix M)和模拟物(例如Advax-CpG)的开发。虽然人们对新型佐剂的兴趣日益浓厚,为 疫苗 和递送系统的开发创造了新的机遇,但也给它们的开发、监管和临床应用带来了挑战。COVID-19 大流行还暴露了佐剂供应和供应链的脆弱性,强调需要能够提供更容易获得、可扩展和有弹性的替代品的佐剂模拟物。本文探讨了 疫苗 中佐剂使用的监管环境,重点介绍了新型佐剂开发的关键方面、机遇和潜在策略。它还讨论了减轻可能的安全问题的方法,并讨论了流行病防范创新联盟 (CEPI) 建立的佐剂库的使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
佐剂是大多数 疫苗 制剂的组成部分,主要用作抗原的递送系统和免疫刺激剂。该研究解决了对先进 疫苗 佐剂的需求,特别是在 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 及其相关挑战的背景下。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了 疫苗 佐剂的监管框架和开发策略。它重点关注佐剂在增强 疫苗 诱导的 呼吸道合胞病毒 中的作用,特别是对于 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和相关病原体。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对佐剂现有文献和监管指南的全面审查,包括佐剂作为免疫刺激剂或递送系统的功能。该研究还讨论了流行病防范创新联盟(CEPI)建立的佐剂库的使用。
主要结果
疫苗 的有效性主要归因于作为活性药物成分(API)的抗原和作为 疫苗 制剂中的组成材料的佐剂。佐剂通过激活先天免疫系统并增强 免疫应答 对共递送抗原的适应性来增强 疫苗 的有效性。
解释与创新
人们对新型佐剂日益增长的兴趣为疫苗和递送系统的开发创造了新的机遇,但也给它们的开发、监管和临床应用带来了挑战。本文探讨了 疫苗 中佐剂使用的监管环境,重点介绍了新型佐剂开发的关键方面、机遇和潜在策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更容易获得、可扩展和有弹性的佐剂提供信息,特别是针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和其他相关病原体。该研究还提出了减轻与佐剂相关的可能安全问题的方法。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告与研究相关的具体局限性或证据强度。基于对佐剂及其在 疫苗 开发中的作用的一般讨论,信心是中等的。

Advancing Vaccine Adjuvants: Regulatory Frameworks and Developmental Strategies

Authors: Yousuf Naqvi, Wolfgang W. Leitner, Nathalie Garçon, Ramin Sabet-Azad, Antonio Guzzi, Daniel J. Fullen, Dennis Christensen, Syed Hassan Askary, Victoria Graham, Adam Hacker
Original abstract (English)
Adjuvants have been an integral part of most vaccine formulations, mainly functioning as delivery systems and immunostimulants for antigens. Aluminum salts, the first adjuvants approved for human vaccine use, remain the most widely used adjuvants in EMA- and FDA-licensed vaccines. Adjuvants enhance vaccine-induced immune responses; improve vaccine efficacy, especially in populations vulnerable to infections such as the elderly and infants; provide antigen dose-sparing; and shape the immune profile of the vaccine-induced response. The effectiveness of vaccines is attributable primarily to the antigens as active pharmaceutical ingredients (APIs) and the adjuvants as constituent material in the vaccine formulation. Adjuvants enhance vaccine effectiveness by activating the innate immune system and boosting adaptive immune responses to co-delivered antigens. Adjuvants function as immunostimulants or delivery systems, often acting as both by promoting antigen uptake by Antigen Presenting Cells (APCs) and triggering cytokine and chemokine release to improve B and T cell activation. Adjuvants are either endogenous, as found in live, attenuated, pathogen-based vaccines, or exogenous, added to, e.g., protein subunit or mRNA-based vaccines to boost immunogenicity. Over the past few decades, novel exogenous adjuvants have become critical to new vaccine development, serving both as delivery systems and immune stimulants. Notable examples include MF59, used in seasonal and pandemic influenza vaccines, and AS01, incorporated in shingles, malaria, and RSV vaccines. The COVID-19 pandemic has accelerated not only the development and use of mRNA vaccines adjuvanted by lipid nanoparticles (LNPs) but also the development of novel adjuvants (e.g., Matrix M) and mimics (e.g., Advax-CpG) of those already present in licensed vaccines. While the growing interest in novel adjuvants has created new opportunities for development of vaccines and delivery systems, it has also introduced challenges in their development, regulation, and clinical application. The COVID-19 pandemic also exposed vulnerabilities in adjuvant availability and supply chains, underscoring the need for adjuvant mimics that can provide more accessible, scalable, and resilient alternatives. This paper explores the regulatory landscape surrounding the use of adjuvants in vaccines, highlighting critical aspects, opportunities and potential strategies for the development of novel adjuvants. It also addresses approaches to mitigate possible safety concerns and discusses the use of an adjuvant library established by the Coalition for Epidemic Preparedness Innovations (CEPI).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Adjuvants have been an integral part of most vaccine formulations, mainly functioning as delivery systems and immunostimulants for antigens. The study addresses the need for advancing vaccine adjuvants, particularly in the context of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and its associated challenges.
Design and population
The study is a review of regulatory frameworks and developmental strategies for vaccine adjuvants. It focuses on the role of adjuvants in enhancing vaccine-induced immune responses, especially for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and related pathogens.
Core methods
The methods involve a comprehensive review of existing literature and regulatory guidelines on adjuvants, including their function as immunostimulants or delivery systems. The study also discusses the use of an adjuvant library established by the Coalition for Epidemic Preparedness Innovations (CEPI).
Main results
The effectiveness of vaccines is attributable primarily to the antigens as active pharmaceutical ingredients (APIs) and the adjuvants as constituent material in the vaccine formulation. Adjuvants enhance vaccine effectiveness by activating the innate immune system and boosting adaptive immune responses to co-delivered antigens.
Interpretation and novelty
The growing interest in novel adjuvants has created new opportunities for development of vaccines and delivery systems, but it has also introduced challenges in their development, regulation, and clinical application. This paper explores the regulatory landscape surrounding the use of adjuvants in vaccines, highlighting critical aspects, opportunities and potential strategies for the development of novel adjuvants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accessible, scalable, and resilient adjuvants, particularly for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and other related pathogens. The study also addresses approaches to mitigate possible safety concerns associated with adjuvants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength related to the study. The confidence is moderate based on the general discussion of adjuvants and their role in vaccine development.

眼睛保护对减少医护人员呼吸道疾病 传播 的功效:基于机制的叙事综合

Efficacy of eye protection in reducing respiratory disease transmission in healthcare workers: a mechanism-informed narrative synthesis
作者： Trisha Greenhalgh, Matthew Oliver, Denize Atan, Rebecca K. Helm, Loay Kanou, Rebecca Helm
摘要中文翻译考虑到现有研究的设计、偏见和功效,它们能展示什么?它们的研究结果在以后的评论和指导中如何解释?需要进行一项新的审查,根据这些机制解释每项研究,以确定证据可以和不能显示什么。IPLASY ￼1 注册系统审查和荟萃分析协议国际平台 IPLASY 眼睛保护对减少医护人员呼吸道疾病的功效 传播:基于机制的叙述综合 Greenhalgh,T;奥利弗,M;阿坦,D;赫尔姆,R;Kanou,L. 资助者在制定方案或进行研究方面没有任何作用。初始注册时的审查阶段 - 数据分析。IPLASY 注册号:INPLASY2026110068 修正案 - 该方案于 2026 年 11 月 19 日在国际注册系统审查和荟萃分析方案平台 (INPLASY) 注册,最后更新于 2026 年 11 月 19 日。方法 搜索策略 医护人员在工作过程中传播的呼吸道感染,包括 SARS-CoV-1、SARS-CoV-2、 MERS-CoV、流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 和任何其他呼吸道病原体。简介 评论问题/目的 目的:为医护人员提供基于风险的眼睛保护方法(护目镜、面罩和面罩),以预防呼吸道疾病 传播,同时考虑因果机制。护目镜是否可以减少医护人员的呼吸道感染?在什么情况下可以?对于不同的病原体和暴露情况,疾病和干预的机制如何预测任何影响的大小?基本原理 建议医护人员采取眼睛保护措施,预防多种呼吸道疾病...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在为医护人员提供一种基于风险的眼睛保护方法,以预防呼吸道疾病 传播,同时考虑因果机制。背景强调了针对几种呼吸道病原体的眼睛保护建议及其在 COVID-19 大流行期间的广泛使用。
设计与对象
该研究包括比较使用不同护目镜的医护人员之间感染率的所有定量设计,例如随机试验、队列研究 研究和 暴发 调查。它重点关注医疗机构中传播的呼吸道感染,包括 SARS-CoV-1、SARS-CoV-2、MERS-CoV、流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 和其他呼吸道病原体。
核心方法
该综述使用了跨 PubMed、CINAHL 和 Web of Science 等数据库的系统搜索策略,并纳入了专家建议、早期综述和用于评估的人工智能工具。每项研究均使用定制人工智能工具 (EAGLE2.0) 进行评估,并检查偏差、混杂因素和效应量计算。
主要结果
该综合将每项研究的估计与其可以检测到的最小影响进行比较,确定研究设计中的偏差,并根据预测机制检查结果。它还研究了混杂因素的内部测试,并按病原体和等级比较了结果。
解释与创新
作者通过将这些发现与疾病 传播 和眼睛保护的预测机制相结合来解释这些发现,强调了在评估证据时考虑这些机制的重要性。这种方法很新颖,因为它侧重于因果路径而不是粗略估计。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果可以通过强调眼睛保护在减少 传播 中的作用,为医疗保健环境中制定有针对性的感染控制策略(特别是 呼吸道合胞病毒)提供信息。它们还提供了对现有研究的偏见和局限性的见解。
局限与证据强度
由于其他防护设备和细心工人效应的潜在混淆以及对观察性研究的依赖,证据受到限制。由于这些局限性以及解释因果机制的复杂性,研究结果的可信度较低。

Efficacy of eye protection in reducing respiratory disease transmission in healthcare workers: a mechanism-informed narrative synthesis

Authors: Trisha Greenhalgh, Matthew Oliver, Denize Atan, Rebecca K. Helm, Loay Kanou, Rebecca Helm
Original abstract (English)
What can the existing studies show, given their designs, biases and power, and how have their findings been interpreted in later reviews and guidance? A new review, which interprets each study against these mechanisms, is needed to establish what the evidence can and cannot show. INPLASY ￼1 International Platform of Registered Systematic Review and Meta-analysis Protocols INPLASY Efficacy of eye protection in reducing respiratory disease transmission in healthcare workers: a mechanism-informed narrative synthesis Greenhalgh, T; Oliver, M; Atan, D; Helm, R; Kanou, L. The funder had no role in developing the protocol or the conduct of the study. Review stage at the time of initial registration - Data analysis. INPLASY registration number: INPLASY2026110068 Amendments - This protocol was registered with the International Platform of Registered Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis Protocols (INPLASY) on 19 November 2026 and was last updated on 19 November 2026. METHODS Search strategy Respiratory infections transmitted to healthcare workers in the course of their work, including SARS-CoV-1, SARS-CoV-2, MERS-CoV, influenza, respiratory syncytial virus and any other respiratory pathogen. INTRODUCTION eview question / Objective Objective: to inform a risk-based approach to eye protection (goggles, face shields and visors) against respiratory disease transmission in healthcare workers, taking account of causal mechanisms. Does eye protection reduce respiratory infection i n h e a l t h c a r e w o r k e r s , a n d i n w h i c h circumstances? What do the mechanisms of disease and of the intervention predict about the size of any effect, for different pathogens and exposure scenarios? Rationale Eye protection is recommended for healthcare workers against several respiratory pat...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to inform a risk-based approach to eye protection against respiratory disease transmission in healthcare workers, considering causal mechanisms. The background highlights the recommendation of eye protection for several respiratory pathogens and its widespread use during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
The study included all quantitative designs comparing infection rates between healthcare workers with differing use of eye protection, such as randomised trials, cohort studies, and outbreak investigations. It focused on respiratory infections transmitted in healthcare settings, including SARS-CoV-1, SARS-CoV-2, MERS-CoV, influenza, respiratory syncytial virus, and other respiratory pathogens.
Core methods
The review used a systematic search strategy across databases like PubMed, CINAHL, and Web of Science, and incorporated expert recommendations, earlier reviews, and AI tools for appraisal. Each study was evaluated with a bespoke AI tool (EAGLE2.0) and checked for bias, confounding, and effect size calculations.
Main results
The synthesis compared each study’s estimate with the smallest effect it could detect, identified biases in study designs, and examined findings against predicted mechanisms. It also looked at internal tests for confounding and compared results by pathogen and tier.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings by aligning them with the predicted mechanisms of disease transmission and eye protection, highlighting the importance of considering these mechanisms in evaluating the evidence. This approach is novel as it focuses on the causal pathways rather than crude estimates.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted infection control strategies in healthcare settings, particularly for respiratory syncytial virus, by highlighting the role of eye protection in reducing transmission. They also provide insights into the biases and limitations of existing studies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by potential confounding from other protective equipment and the careful-worker effect, as well as the reliance on observational studies. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to these limitations and the complexity of interpreting causal mechanisms.

封闭社区呼吸道感染风险:疗养院和监狱的多维度风险评估和综合监测(Stell-ARI项目)

Risk of respiratory infections in closed communities: multi-dimensional risk assessment and integrated surveillance in a nursing home and a prison (Stell-ARI project)
作者： Annalaura Carducci, Ileana Federigi, Alessandra Pagani, Nebiyu Tariku Atomsa, Lara Tavoschi, Guglielmo Arzilli, Caterina Rizzo, Francesco Pistelli, Claudia Meschi, Laura Carrozzi
摘要中文翻译病毒性急性呼吸道感染(ARI)与生活在封闭和脆弱社区的人们尤其相关。Stell-ARI项目旨在设计和应用一个多维框架,将环境风险评估(基于建筑结构和管理、居民人口特征、室内空气质量(IAQ)以及空气和表面的微生物污染)与基于废水和临床的监测结合起来,用于SARS-CoV-2、人类腺病毒(HAdV)、流感病毒A(IVA)和B(IVB)、呼吸道合胞病毒 A(RSVA)和B (RSVB)。该系统在两个试点机构社区得到应用:疗养院和监狱。研究设计包括对设施管理人员、居民和工人进行问卷调查,以及对化学和微生物污染物的环境监测。计算了特定领域和全局风险评分。疗养院的建筑结构和管理(3 vs. 13)和居民人口特征(3.4 vs. 3.4 vs.)的得分均低于监狱。化学空气风险评分显示出相似的差异(1.3 vs. 3.0),两种环境的室内污染物特征也存在差异。疗养院的微生物空气和表面风险评分也更好(4.0 vs 5.0)。在这两种环境中,均检测到了 COVID-19 簇以及 SARS-CoV-2 和 HAdV在空气和地表样本以及废水中都发现了这种病毒,不仅与临床病例相符,而且在整个监测期间,在疗养院中出现的频率更高。总之,这种新颖的多维结构化方法虽然需要进一步验证,但却是支持 ARIs 监测、封闭和脆弱机构社区预防和控制的潜在操作工具。

问题与背景
病毒性急性呼吸道感染(ARI)与生活在封闭和脆弱社区的人们尤其相关。

研究设计包括对设施管理人员、居民和工人进行问卷调查,以及对化学和微生物污染物的环境监测。

主要结果
疗养院的微生物空气和表面风险评分也更好(4.0 比 5.0)。

化学空气风险评分显示出相似的差异(1.3 与 1.3)。

Risk of respiratory infections in closed communities: multi-dimensional risk assessment and integrated surveillance in a nursing home and a prison (Stell-ARI project)

Authors: Annalaura Carducci, Ileana Federigi, Alessandra Pagani, Nebiyu Tariku Atomsa, Lara Tavoschi, Guglielmo Arzilli, Caterina Rizzo, Francesco Pistelli, Claudia Meschi, Laura Carrozzi
Original abstract (English)
Viral Acute Respiratory Infections (ARIs) are particularly relevant for people living in closed and vulnerable communities. The Stell-ARI Project aimed to design and apply a multi-dimensional framework integrating environmental risk assessment (based on building structure and management, resident population characteristics, indoor air quality (IAQ), and microbiological contamination of air and surfaces) with wastewater-based and clinical surveillance for SARS-CoV-2, Human Adenovirus (HAdV), Influenza Virus A (IVA) and B (IVB), Respiratory Syncytial Virus A (RSVA) and B (RSVB). This system was applied in two pilot institutional communities: a nursing home and a prison. The study design included questionnaire surveys of facility managers, residents and workers alongside environmental monitoring of chemical and microbial contaminants. Domain-specific and global risk scores were calculated. Scores were lower in the nursing home than in the prison for building structure and management (3 vs. 13) and resident population characteristics (3.4 vs. The chemical air risk score showed similar differences (1.3 vs. 3.0) and the two settings also differed in their indoor pollutant profiles. The microbiological air and surface risk score was also better in nursing home (4.0 vs 5.0). In both settings, a COVID-19 cluster was detected and SARS-CoV-2 and HAdV were found in air and surface samples, as well as in wastewater, not only in coincidence with clinical cases, but throughout the monitoring period, with a higher frequency in nursing home. In conclusion, this novel multi-dimensional structured approach, although needing further validation, represents a potential operational tool to support ARIs surveillance, prevention and control in closed and vulnerable institutional communities.

年龄 ≥ 60 岁且单独接受 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗 或与其他 疫苗 联合治疗的美国成年人报告的健康事件

Health events reported by US adults aged ≥ 60 years who received a respiratory syncytial virus vaccine alone or with other vaccines
作者： Anne M. Hause, Bi-Cheng Zhang, Paige Marquez, Michael Melgar, Amadea Britton, Tanya R. Myers, Sarah Myer, David K. Shay, Sarah A. Meyer
摘要中文翻译有关 RSV 疫苗 与其他 疫苗 一起施用时的反应原性的数据有限。2023年10月20日至2024年12月29日期间,18,252名年龄≥60岁的V-safe参与者在RSV 疫苗接种后一周内完成了自愿调查。其中,11,333 名患者 (62%) 单独接受了辉瑞、葛兰素史克或 Moderna RSV 疫苗,6919 名患者 (38%) 在同一周内与至少一种其他 疫苗 一起接受了 RSV 疫苗。与单独接受辉瑞 疫苗 治疗的参与者相比,接受辉瑞与另一种 疫苗 治疗的参与者更有可能报告注射部位症状。单独接受 GSK 疫苗 治疗或与另一种 疫苗 联合治疗的患者更有可能报告症状,但风险并不因联合用药状态而异。Moderna 疫苗接种 后出现症状的风险与辉瑞后出现症状的风险相似。这些数据可用于向患者提供有关 疫苗接种 单独使用 RSV 或与其他 疫苗 联合使用后的预期反应的建议。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该评价重点关注 60 岁以上美国成年人报告的健康事件,这些成年人单独接受了 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗 或与其他 疫苗 一起接受治疗。核心问题是评估 RSV 疫苗 单独给药或与其他 疫苗 联合给药时在该人群中的反应原性和安全性。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括对 18,252 名年龄≥60 岁的 V-safe 参与者进行的自愿调查的数据,这些参与者在 RSV 疫苗接种 后一周内(2023 年 10 月 20 日至 2024 年 12 月 29 日)完成了调查。研究设计是观察性的,审查方法包括分析 疫苗接种 后自我报告的症状。
共识与结论
Moderna 疫苗接种 后出现症状的风险与辉瑞后相似。然而,对于 GSK 疫苗 来说,无论是单独服用还是与另一种 疫苗 一起服用,出现症状的风险都较高。没有明确的证据表明葛兰素史克的联合用药状态会带来不同的风险。
争议与缺口
审查指出,有关 RSV 疫苗 与其他 疫苗 一起给药时的反应原性的数据有限。在某些情况下,数据的缺乏造成了联合用药安全性的不确定性。
科研与实践启示
这些数据可用于向患者提供有关 疫苗接种 单独使用 RSV 或与其他 疫苗 联合使用后的预期反应的建议。研究结果表明,需要进一步研究来阐明 RSV 疫苗 与其他 疫苗 共同给药时的反应原性。

Health events reported by US adults aged ≥ 60 years who received a respiratory syncytial virus vaccine alone or with other vaccines

Authors: Anne M. Hause, Bi-Cheng Zhang, Paige Marquez, Michael Melgar, Amadea Britton, Tanya R. Myers, Sarah Myer, David K. Shay, Sarah A. Meyer
Original abstract (English)
Data concerning reactogenicity of RSV vaccines when administered with other vaccines are limited. During October 20, 2023-December 29, 2024, 18,252 V-safe participants aged ≥60 years completed a voluntary survey during the week after RSV vaccination. Of these, 11,333 (62%) received a Pfizer, GSK, or Moderna RSV vaccine alone and 6919 (38%) received an RSV vaccine with at least one other vaccine in the same week. Compared with participants who received Pfizer vaccine alone, those who received Pfizer with another vaccine were more likely to report injection site symptoms. Those who received GSK vaccine, either alone or with another vaccine, were more likely to report symptoms, but risk did not vary by coadministration status. Risk of symptoms after Moderna vaccination was similar to risk after Pfizer. These data can be used to advise patients about expected reactions after vaccination with RSV alone or with other vaccines.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on health events reported by US adults aged ≥ 60 years who received a respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccine alone or with other vaccines. The central question is to evaluate the reactogenicity and safety of RSV vaccines in this population when administered alone or in combination with other vaccines.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base includes data from a voluntary survey of 18,252 V-safe participants aged ≥60 years who completed the survey during the week after RSV vaccination, between October 20, 2023, and December 29, 2024. The study design is observational, and the review method involves analyzing self-reported symptoms following vaccination.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the risk of symptoms after Moderna vaccination is similar to that after Pfizer. However, for the GSK vaccine, the risk of symptoms was higher regardless of whether it was administered alone or with another vaccine. There is no clear evidence of varying risk based on coadministration status for GSK.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that data concerning the reactogenicity of RSV vaccines when administered with other vaccines are limited. This lack of data creates uncertainty about the safety of coadministration in some cases.
Research and practice implications
These data can be used to advise patients about expected reactions after vaccination with RSV alone or with other vaccines. The findings suggest the need for further research to clarify the reactogenicity of RSV vaccines when coadministered with other vaccines.

加拿大司法管辖区有和没有公共资助的通用 呼吸道合胞病毒 计划时患者对 呼吸道合胞病毒 免疫的态度差异

Differences in patient attitudes on respiratory syncytial virus immunization among Canadian jurisdictions with and without publicly funded universal nirsevimab programs
作者： Jeffrey Man Hay Wong, Elisabeth McClymont, Jenna Langlois, Sandra Blitz, Tali Bogler, Isabelle Boucoiran, Eliana Castillo, Rohan D'Souza, Darine El‐Chaâr, Chelsea Elwood
摘要中文翻译背景:2024年,加拿大推出了两种预防婴儿严重RSV感染的策略:母体疫苗(RSVpreF)和婴儿单克隆抗体(尼塞韦单抗)。尽管加拿大指南建议通用季节性 尼塞韦单抗,但各省/地区的实施情况各不相同。我们比较了有和没有公共资助的通用 尼塞韦单抗 计划的司法管辖区对 RSV 免疫的态度和偏好。方法:2024 年 11 月至 2025 年 3 月期间,我们开展了一项全国前瞻性 队列研究 研究,通过社交媒体招募怀孕或近期怀孕的参与者。参与者完成了基线调查,并在产后 3 个月和 6 个月进行了随访。调查收集了人口数据、产科结果、报告的免疫产品接收情况以及对 RSVpreF 和 尼塞韦单抗 的态度,包括感知到的障碍。根据居住在有和没有通用 尼塞韦单抗 计划的省/地区对参与者进行了分析。结果:在分析中纳入的 1008 名参与者中,40.6% 居住在拥有资助的通用 尼塞韦单抗 计划的司法管辖区。如果两种免疫产品都是免费且可用的,在具有通用 尼塞韦单抗 的司法管辖区,78.5% 的人会接受 尼塞韦单抗,而 72.4% 的人会接受 RSVpreF。在没有资助 尼塞韦单抗 项目的省份,73.9% 的参与者会接受 尼塞韦单抗,其中 RSVpreF 的接受率为 89.8% (p < 0.001)。在具有通用 尼塞韦单抗 计划的辖区,尼塞韦单抗 被认为在预防 RSV 疾病方面比 RSVpreF 更有益(p < 0.001)且更有效(p = 0.02)。大约 96% 的参与者同意获得 RSVpreF 的决定取决于他们,但对 尼塞韦单抗 持相同看法的人较少 (84.6%)。在对怀孕期间的 疫苗 进行排名时,43.9% 的参与者将 Tdap 列为首要任务,而 21% 的参与者将 RSV 疫苗接种 列为第一。60.5% 的参与者对 Tdap 的排名高于 RSVpreF,不同司法管辖区之间没有显着差异。结论:在 2024/2025 RSV 季节,RSVpreF 和 尼塞韦单抗 之间的偏好与省/地区对婴儿单克隆抗体的公共资助有关。随着 RSV 免疫预防计划的发展,政策制定者应该认识到,资助决策会影响公众对现有免疫产品的态度和偏好。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该评价调查了加拿大司法管辖区有和没有公共资助的通用 呼吸道合胞病毒 计划的患者对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫的态度差异。目标是 RSV 免疫,具体比较对两种产品的态度:母体 疫苗 (RSVpreF) 和婴儿单克隆抗体 (尼塞韦单抗)。该研究的重点是怀孕或最近怀孕的参与者以及他们接受这些基于司法管辖区资助政策的免疫策略的意愿。
证据基础与方法
该审查基于 2024 年 11 月至 2025 年 3 月期间进行的一项全国前瞻性 队列研究 研究,通过社交媒体招募怀孕或最近怀孕的参与者。参与者在产后 3 个月和 6 个月完成基线调查和随访。该研究按居住在有和没有通用 尼塞韦单抗 计划的省份/地区分析了数据。证据包括人口统计数据、产科结果、报告的免疫产品接收情况以及对 RSVpreF 和 尼塞韦单抗 的态度,包括感知到的障碍。
共识与结论
研究发现,在具有通用 尼塞韦单抗 计划的司法管辖区,78.5% 的参与者会接受 尼塞韦单抗,而 72.4% 的参与者会接受 RSVpreF。在没有资助 尼塞韦单抗 项目的省份,73.9% 的人会接受 尼塞韦单抗,89.8% 的人会接受 RSVpreF (p < 0.001)。拥有通用 尼塞韦单抗 计划的辖区的参与者认为 尼塞韦单抗 在预防 RSV 疾病方面比 RSVpreF 更有益(p < 0.001)且更有效(p = 0.02)。此外,96% 的参与者同意获得 RSVpreF 的决定取决于他们,但对 尼塞韦单抗 持相同看法的人较少 (84.6%)。
争议与缺口
该研究没有报告有和没有通用 尼塞韦单抗 计划的司法管辖区之间接受率差异背后的原因。它还没有探讨其他因素(例如医疗保健获取、社会经济地位或健康素养)对免疫偏好的影响。此外,该综述没有提供有关这些免疫策略的长期影响或其​​在降低 RSV 相关发病率和 死亡率 方面的有效性的信息。
科研与实践启示
研究结果表明,对 尼塞韦单抗 等婴儿单克隆抗体的公共资助可以影响患者对 RSV 免疫的态度和偏好。政策制定者在设计免疫规划时应考虑这些偏好,并应优先研究影响接受率的因素,例如医疗保健、健康素养和社会经济地位。此外,还需要进一步的研究来评估这些策略在减少 RSV 相关疾病负担方面的长期有效性。

Differences in patient attitudes on respiratory syncytial virus immunization among Canadian jurisdictions with and without publicly funded universal nirsevimab programs

Authors: Jeffrey Man Hay Wong, Elisabeth McClymont, Jenna Langlois, Sandra Blitz, Tali Bogler, Isabelle Boucoiran, Eliana Castillo, Rohan D'Souza, Darine El‐Chaâr, Chelsea Elwood
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In 2024, Canada introduced two strategies to prevent severe infant RSV infection: maternal vaccine (RSVpreF) and infant monoclonal antibody (nirsevimab). Although Canadian guidelines recommended universal seasonal nirsevimab, implementation varied across provinces/territories. We compared attitudes and preferences towards RSV immunization in jurisdictions with and without publicly funded universal nirsevimab programs. METHODS: Between November 2024 and March 2025, we conducted a national prospective cohort study recruiting pregnant or recently pregnant participants through social media. Participants completed a baseline survey, with follow-up at 3 and 6 months postpartum. Surveys collected demographic data, obstetrical outcomes, reported receipt of immunization products, and attitudes towards RSVpreF and nirsevimab, including perceived barriers. Participants were analyzed by residence in provinces/territories with versus without universal nirsevimab programs. RESULTS: Among 1008 participants included in the analysis, 40.6% resided in jurisdictions with funded universal nirsevimab programs. If both immunization products were free and available, in jurisdictions with universal nirsevimab, 78.5% would accept nirsevimab, while 72.4% would accept RSVpreF. In provinces without funded nirsevimab programs, 73.9% of participants would accept nirsevimab with an 89.8% acceptance rate for RSVpreF (p < 0.001). In jurisdictions with universal nirsevimab programs, nirsevimab was perceived as more beneficial (p < 0.001) and more effective (p = 0.02) at preventing RSV disease than RSVpreF. Approximately 96% of participants agreed that the decision to obtain RSVpreF was up to them, however, fewer felt the same about nirsevimab (84.6%). When ranking vaccines in pregnancy, 43.9% of participants ranked Tdap as their top priority, while 21% ranked RSV vaccination first. Tdap was ranked higher than RSVpreF by 60.5% of participants, with no significant differences between jurisdictions. CONCLUSIONS: During the 2024/2025 RSV season, preferences between RSVpreF and nirsevimab were associated with provincial/territorial public funding for infant monoclonal antibodies. As RSV immunoprophylaxis programs evolve, policymakers should recognize that funding decisions can influence public attitudes and preferences towards available immunization products.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines differences in patient attitudes toward respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunization in Canadian jurisdictions with and without publicly funded universal nirsevimab programs. The target is RSV immunization, specifically comparing attitudes toward two products: maternal vaccine (RSVpreF) and infant monoclonal antibody (nirsevimab). The study focuses on pregnant or recently pregnant participants and their willingness to accept these immunization strategies based on jurisdictional funding policies.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a national prospective cohort study conducted between November 2024 and March 2025, recruiting pregnant or recently pregnant participants through social media. Participants completed a baseline survey and follow-up at 3 and 6 months postpartum. The study analyzed data by residence in provinces/territories with versus without universal nirsevimab programs. The evidence includes demographic data, obstetrical outcomes, reported receipt of immunization products, and attitudes toward RSVpreF and nirsevimab, including perceived barriers.
Consensus and conclusions
The study found that in jurisdictions with universal nirsevimab programs, 78.5% of participants would accept nirsevimab, while 72.4% would accept RSVpreF. In provinces without funded nirsevimab programs, 73.9% would accept nirsevimab, and 89.8% would accept RSVpreF (p < 0.001). Participants in jurisdictions with universal nirsevimab programs perceived nirsevimab as more beneficial (p < 0.001) and more effective (p = 0.02) at preventing RSV disease than RSVpreF. Additionally, 96% of participants agreed that the decision to obtain RSVpreF was up to them, but fewer felt the same about nirsevimab (84.6%).
Controversies and gaps
The study does not report on the reasons behind the differences in acceptance rates between jurisdictions with and without universal nirsevimab programs. It also does not explore the impact of other factors, such as healthcare access, socioeconomic status, or health literacy, on immunization preferences. Furthermore, the review does not provide information on the long-term effects of these immunization strategies or their effectiveness in reducing RSV-related morbidity and mortality.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest that public funding for infant monoclonal antibodies like nirsevimab can influence patient attitudes and preferences toward RSV immunization. Policymakers should consider these preferences when designing immunization programs and should prioritize research into the factors that affect acceptance rates, such as healthcare access, health literacy, and socioeconomic status. Additionally, further studies are needed to evaluate the long-term effectiveness of these strategies in reducing RSV-related disease burden.

母亲 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗接种:对 疫苗 可接受性和摄取决定因素的系统回顾和荟萃分析

Maternal respiratory syncytial virus vaccination: A systematic review and meta-analysis of determinants of vaccine acceptability and uptake
作者： Lorenna Mendonça de Albuquerque, Nazim Sadou, Ugochinyere Vivian Ukah, Gabriel Côté‐Corriveau, Jessica Healy‐Profitós, Nathalie Auger, Gabriel Côté-Corriveau, Jessica Healy-Profitós
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
母体 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗接种 具有预防婴儿呼吸道疾病的潜力。
证据基础与方法
我们使用具有逆方差加权的随机效应模型进行了荟萃分析,以估计社会人口因素与 RSV 疫苗接种 意图和吸收之间的关联的合并比值比 (OR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI)。
共识与结论
Low educational attainment (OR 0.59, 95% CI 0.45-0.76; 8 studies) and unemployment (OR 0.73, 95% CI 0.56-0.96; 7 studies) were associated with >20% less intention to be vaccinated compared with higher education or employment.
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
母亲年龄和婚姻状况与 RSV 疫苗接种 意图无关。

Maternal respiratory syncytial virus vaccination: A systematic review and meta-analysis of determinants of vaccine acceptability and uptake

Authors: Lorenna Mendonça de Albuquerque, Nazim Sadou, Ugochinyere Vivian Ukah, Gabriel Côté‐Corriveau, Jessica Healy‐Profitós, Nathalie Auger, Gabriel Côté-Corriveau, Jessica Healy-Profitós
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Maternal respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination has potential to prevent infant respiratory morbidity. We identified sociodemographic factors influencing the acceptability and uptake of RSV vaccination among pregnant individuals. METHODS: We carried out a systematic review and meta-analysis of sociodemographic factors associated with maternal RSV vaccination behavior. We searched MEDLINE, EMBASE, and Web of Science for observational studies of RSV vaccination in pregnant women published before September 10, 2025. We included articles that examined how maternal race/ethnicity, education, employment, age, and marital status were related to RSV vaccine intention and uptake. We performed a meta-analysis using random effects models with inverse-variance weighting to estimate pooled odds ratios (OR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI) for the association between sociodemographic factors and RSV vaccination intention and uptake. RESULTS: Seventeen studies comprising 15,275 individuals met inclusion criteria. Black race was the strongest determinant of vaccination behavior during pregnancy, followed by socioeconomic factors. In meta-analysis, Black race was associated with more than 50% lower odds of RSV vaccine uptake compared with White race (OR 0.43, 95% CI 0.32-0.57; 6 studies). Low educational attainment (OR 0.59, 95% CI 0.45-0.76; 8 studies) and unemployment (OR 0.73, 95% CI 0.56-0.96; 7 studies) were associated with >20% less intention to be vaccinated compared with higher education or employment. Maternal age and marital status were not associated with RSV vaccination intention. CONCLUSION: Black patients and patients from low socioeconomic backgrounds should be a primary focus of obstetric RSV vaccination efforts.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Maternal respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination has potential to prevent infant respiratory morbidity.
Evidence base and method
We performed a meta-analysis using random effects models with inverse-variance weighting to estimate pooled odds ratios (OR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI) for the association between sociodemographic factors and RSV vaccination intention and uptake.
Consensus and conclusions
Low educational attainment (OR 0.59, 95% CI 0.45-0.76; 8 studies) and unemployment (OR 0.73, 95% CI 0.56-0.96; 7 studies) were associated with >20% less intention to be vaccinated compared with higher education or employment.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Maternal age and marital status were not associated with RSV vaccination intention.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

向 V-safe 报告的接种 RSV 疫苗的美国孕妇的授权后安全性 监测

Post-authorization safety surveillance of RSV vaccinated U.S. pregnant women reporting to V-safe
作者： Lauren Head Zauche, Bi-Cheng Zhang, David K. Shay, Paige L. Marquez, Tanya R. Myers, Anne M. Hause
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是美国婴儿住院的主要原因。2023 年,美国食品和药物管理局批准了 RSV 疫苗(辉瑞、Abrysvo)用于孕妇给药,以预防婴儿患严重 RSV 疾病。V-safe 收集有关 疫苗接种 后不久发生的事件的信息。参加 V-safe 的疫苗接种女性在 7 天内每天完成一次关于注射部位和全身反应、其严重程度和健康影响(即干扰正常日常活动、缺勤或缺课或接受医疗保健)的网络调查,然后在 疫苗接种 后的 2-6 周内每周完成一次。2023年10月12日至2025年3月21日期间,1200名孕妇报告了RSV 疫苗接种,并完成了至少一项健康调查。中位年龄为 34 岁。疫苗接种 调查后 0-7 天报告的常见症状包括注射部位疼痛 (32.5%)、疲劳 (27.7%) 和肌肉疼痛 (20.5%)。疫苗接种 后第一天最常报告反应,大多数自我归类为轻度或中度。12.5% 的参与者报告 疫苗接种 后第一周内对健康产生了影响。971 名在妊娠 32-36 周接种疫苗并提供分娩日期的参与者中有 59 名 (6.1%) 报告早产。在临床试验中,5.7% 的接种疫苗的妇女出现早产。然而,更多 V-safe 参与者有早产的危险因素。根据接种疫苗的妇女的报告,母体 RSV 疫苗接种 后出现轻度或中度严重程度的短期反应原性很常见。我们的研究结果与对孕妇施用 RSV 疫苗 的许可前随机临床试验的安全性数据基本一致。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是美国婴儿住院的主要原因。此次审查的重点是通过 V-safe 系统向美国孕妇施用 RSV 疫苗 的安全性 监测。
证据基础与方法
2023 年,美国食品和药物管理局批准了 RSV 疫苗(辉瑞、Abrysvo)用于孕妇给药,以预防婴儿患严重 RSV 疾病。参加 V-safe 的疫苗接种女性在 7 天内每天完成一次关于注射部位和全身反应、其严重程度和健康影响(即干扰正常日常活动、缺勤或缺课或接受医疗保健)的网络调查,然后在 疫苗接种 后的 2-6 周内每周完成一次。
共识与结论
2023年10月12日至2025年3月21日期间,1200名孕妇报告了RSV 疫苗接种,并完成了至少一项健康调查。疫苗接种 调查后 0-7 天报告的常见症状包括注射部位疼痛 (32.5%)、疲劳 (27.7%) 和肌肉疼痛 (20.5%)。疫苗接种 后第一天最常报告反应,大多数自我归类为轻度或中度。12.5% 的参与者报告 疫苗接种 后第一周内对健康产生了影响。
争议与缺口
971 名在妊娠 32-36 周接种疫苗并提供分娩日期的参与者中有 59 名 (6.1%) 报告早产。在临床试验中,5.7% 的接种疫苗的妇女出现早产。然而,更多 V-safe 参与者有早产的危险因素。我们的研究结果与对孕妇施用 RSV 疫苗 的许可前随机临床试验的安全性数据基本一致。
科研与实践启示
根据接种疫苗的妇女的报告,母体 RSV 疫苗接种 后出现轻度或中度严重程度的短期反应原性很常见。

Post-authorization safety surveillance of RSV vaccinated U.S. pregnant women reporting to V-safe

Authors: Lauren Head Zauche, Bi-Cheng Zhang, David K. Shay, Paige L. Marquez, Tanya R. Myers, Anne M. Hause
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of US infant hospitalizations. In 2023, the Food and Drug Administration approved an RSV vaccine (Pfizer, Abrysvo) for administration to pregnant women to prevent severe RSV disease in their infants. V-safe collects information about events experienced shortly after vaccination. Vaccinated women who enrolled in V-safe completed web-based surveys about injection site and systemic reactions, their severity, and health impacts (i.e., interference with normal daily activities, missing work or school, or receipt of healthcare) daily for 7 days and then weekly during weeks 2-6 post-vaccination. During October 12, 2023-March 21, 2025, 1200 pregnant women reported RSV vaccination and completed at least one health survey. Median age was 34 years. Common symptoms reported on 0-7 days post-vaccination surveys included injection site pain (32.5%), fatigue (27.7%), and muscle aches (20.5%). Reactions were most frequently reported the first day post-vaccination, and most were self-categorized as mild or moderate. A health impact was reported by 12.5% of participants during the first week after vaccination. Preterm delivery was reported by 59 (6.1%) of 971 participants vaccinated 32-36 weeks of gestation who provided a delivery date. In clinical trials, 5.7% of vaccinated women had preterm delivery. However, more V-safe participants had risk factors for preterm delivery. Based on reports from vaccinated women, short term reactogenicity of mild or moderate severity was common after maternal RSV vaccination. Our findings were generally consistent with safety data from pre-licensure randomized clinical trials of RSV vaccines administered to pregnant women.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question

Evidence base and method

However, more V-safe participants had risk factors for preterm delivery. Our findings were generally consistent with safety data from pre-licensure randomized clinical trials of RSV vaccines administered to pregnant women.

Based on reports from vaccinated women, short term reactogenicity of mild or moderate severity was common after maternal RSV vaccination.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

尼塞韦单抗 后的 RSV 预防:欧洲学到了什么,也没有学到什么。

RSV prevention after nirsevimab: what Europe has learned-and what it has not.
作者： Narmeen Mallah, Josefina L. Razzini, Iago Giné-Vázquez, Ana Isabel Dacosta-Urbieta, Cintia Álvarez-Smith, Irene Rivero-Calle, Andres Muy, Federico Martinón-Torres
摘要中文翻译未标记:欧洲在两个季节内从针对一小部分高危人群的选择性 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防转向能够保护大多数婴儿的基于人群的策略。我们的目的是研究 尼塞韦单抗 引入后欧洲在 RSV 预防方面的经验,并确定有关实施、计划绩效、比较评估和剩余证据差距的关键经验教训。检索了 尼塞韦单抗 在欧洲引入后评估 RSV 预防的研究。根据实施策略、有效性结果、医疗保健影响、安全性、阻力和新出现的研究问题绘制证据。来自欧洲的真实世界研究一致报告,实施 尼塞韦单抗 后,RSV 住院和重症监护入院人数大幅减少。计划设计和交付的异质性表明,产品有效性只是影响的决定因素之一:管理时间、追赶策略、覆盖范围、孕产与社区交付以及基线负担的年龄分布等计划特征影响观察到的结果。第二季的证据将有限的剩余个人保护与持续的累积两季效益区分开来。尽管跨策略比较仍然容易受到时间和选择偏差的影响,但在分娩前充分施用时,母体 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 非常有效。安全调查结果仍然令人放心;与耐药性相关的替代是可检测到的,但并未损害人群水平的有效性。最近推出的 clesrovimab 进一步扩大了预防选择。结论:欧洲已经回答了普遍的婴儿 RSV 预防是否可以减轻严重疾病负担,但尚未对方案的比较方式进行标准化。下一阶段应从产品宣传转向项目科学:针对大多数婴儿的主要策略、明确的救援适应症、关联的免疫结果-基因组监测、共同估计值以及公平和机会成本的透明衡量。已知信息: • 长效单克隆抗体和母体 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 可预防婴儿早期严重的 RSV 疾病。新内容: • 欧洲的教训并不是通用的产品排名:分娩架构、追赶以及孕产妇 疫苗接种 和出生之间的间隔决定了人口影响。• 第二季政策需要估算和纪律:有限的剩余效益可能与大量累积的两季效益共存且无反弹。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
欧洲在两个季节内从针对一小部分高危人群的选择性 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防转向能够保护大多数婴儿的基于人群的策略。
证据基础与方法
我们的目的是研究 尼塞韦单抗 引入后欧洲在 RSV 预防方面的经验,并确定有关实施、计划绩效、比较评估和剩余证据差距的关键经验教训。检索了 尼塞韦单抗 在欧洲引入后评估 RSV 预防的研究。根据实施策略、有效性结果、医疗保健影响、安全性、阻力和新出现的研究问题绘制证据。
共识与结论
来自欧洲的真实世界研究一致报告,实施 尼塞韦单抗 后,RSV 住院和重症监护入院人数大幅减少。安全调查结果仍然令人放心;与耐药性相关的替代是可检测到的,但并未损害人群水平的有效性。欧洲的教训并不是一个通用的产品排名:分娩架构、追赶以及产妇 疫苗接种 和出生之间的间隔决定了人口影响。
争议与缺口
计划设计和交付的异质性表明,产品有效性只是影响的决定因素之一:管理时间、追赶策略、覆盖范围、孕产与社区交付以及基线负担的年龄分布等计划特征影响观察到的结果。尽管跨策略比较仍然容易受到时间和选择偏差的影响,但在分娩前充分施用时,母体 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 非常有效。第二季政策需要估算和纪律:有限的剩余效益可以与大量累积的两季效益并存,并且不会反弹。
科研与实践启示
下一阶段应从产品宣传转向项目科学:针对大多数婴儿的主要策略、明确的救援适应症、关联的免疫结果-基因组监测、共同估计值以及公平和机会成本的透明衡量。最近推出的 clesrovimab 进一步扩大了预防选择。

RSV prevention after nirsevimab: what Europe has learned-and what it has not.

Authors: Narmeen Mallah, Josefina L. Razzini, Iago Giné-Vázquez, Ana Isabel Dacosta-Urbieta, Cintia Álvarez-Smith, Irene Rivero-Calle, Andres Muy, Federico Martinón-Torres
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Europe moved within two seasons from selective respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prophylaxis for a small high-risk group to population-based strategies capable of protecting most infants. We aimed to examine the European experience with RSV prevention after nirsevimab introduction, and identify key lessons regarding implementation, programme performance, comparative evaluation, and remaining evidence gaps. Studies evaluating RSV prevention after nirsevimab introduction in Europe were searched. Evidence was mapped according to implementation strategies, effectiveness outcomes, healthcare impact, safety, resistance, and emerging research questions. Real-world studies from Europe consistently reported considerable reductions in RSV hospitalisation and intensive-care admission following nirsevimab implementation. Heterogeneity in programme design and delivery suggests that product effectiveness is only one determinant of impact: programme characteristics like administration timing, catch-up strategies, coverage, maternity-versus-community delivery, and the age distribution of baseline burden influence observed outcomes. Second-season evidence distinguishes limited residual individual protection from a persisting cumulative two-season benefit. Maternal RSVpreF vaccination is highly effective when administered sufficiently before delivery, although cross-strategy comparisons remain vulnerable to timing and selection bias. Safety findings remain reassuring; resistance-associated substitutions are detectable but have not undermined population-level effectiveness. The recent introduction of clesrovimab further expands preventive options. CONCLUSION: Europe has answered whether universal infant RSV prevention can reduce severe disease burden but not yet standardised how programmes are compared. The next phase should move from product advocacy to programme science: one primary strategy for most infants, explicit rescue indications, linked immunisation-outcome-genomic surveillance, common estimands and transparent measurement of equity and opportunity cost. WHAT IS KNOWN: • Long-acting monoclonal antibodies and maternal RSVpreF vaccination prevent severe RSV disease in early infancy. WHAT IS NEW: • The European lesson is not a universal product ranking: delivery architecture, catch-up and the interval between maternal vaccination and birth determine population impact. • Second-season policy requires estimand discipline: limited residual effectiveness can coexist with substantial cumulative two-season benefit and no rebound.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Europe moved within two seasons from selective respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prophylaxis for a small high-risk group to population-based strategies capable of protecting most infants.
Evidence base and method
We aimed to examine the European experience with RSV prevention after nirsevimab introduction, and identify key lessons regarding implementation, programme performance, comparative evaluation, and remaining evidence gaps. Studies evaluating RSV prevention after nirsevimab introduction in Europe were searched. Evidence was mapped according to implementation strategies, effectiveness outcomes, healthcare impact, safety, resistance, and emerging research questions.
Consensus and conclusions
Real-world studies from Europe consistently reported considerable reductions in RSV hospitalisation and intensive-care admission following nirsevimab implementation. Safety findings remain reassuring; resistance-associated substitutions are detectable but have not undermined population-level effectiveness. The European lesson is not a universal product ranking: delivery architecture, catch-up and the interval between maternal vaccination and birth determine population impact.
Controversies and gaps
Heterogeneity in programme design and delivery suggests that product effectiveness is only one determinant of impact: programme characteristics like administration timing, catch-up strategies, coverage, maternity-versus-community delivery, and the age distribution of baseline burden influence observed outcomes. Maternal RSVpreF vaccination is highly effective when administered sufficiently before delivery, although cross-strategy comparisons remain vulnerable to timing and selection bias. Second-season policy requires estimand discipline: limited residual effectiveness can coexist with substantial cumulative two-season benefit and no rebound.
Research and practice implications
The next phase should move from product advocacy to programme science: one primary strategy for most infants, explicit rescue indications, linked immunisation-outcome-genomic surveillance, common estimands and transparent measurement of equity and opportunity cost. The recent introduction of clesrovimab further expands preventive options.

利用粘膜和全身免疫力对抗呼吸道病毒:疫苗 平台、递送策略和佐剂

Harnessing mucosal and systemic immunity against respiratory viruses: vaccine platforms, delivery strategies, and adjuvants
作者： Wan-Di Zhu, Duna Heo, Bao‐Zhong Wang, Sang‐Moo Kang
摘要中文翻译简介:针对呼吸道病毒(包括流感、COVID-19 和 呼吸道合胞病毒)的持久免疫力仍然是一个挑战。有效的 疫苗 和 疫苗接种 策略可以提供针对这些病毒的持久保护,需要诱导全身免疫和粘膜免疫。涵盖领域:本综述涵盖了呼吸道病毒疫苗平台的最新趋势,包括修饰的病毒样颗粒和基于mRNA的疫苗,以及佐剂,以及诱导全身和粘膜免疫并产生肺驻留记忆细胞的新疫苗接种策略。更好地理解先天 免疫应答 到 疫苗接种 对于提高 疫苗 功效和安全性以及识别早期预测相关性至关重要。专家意见:本文概述了开发下一代呼吸道病毒 疫苗 的最新进展和方法以及诱导全身和粘膜免疫的新 疫苗接种 策略。它还讨论了粘膜 疫苗接种 的临床前和临床结果之间的差距。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),专门解决实现针对 RSV(包括 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 亚型)的持久免疫的挑战,并探索可诱导全身免疫和粘膜免疫的 疫苗 平台、递送策略和佐剂。
证据基础与方法
该综述对 RSV 疫苗 开发的最新进展进行了叙述性综合,涵盖 疫苗 平台,例如改良的病毒样颗粒和基于 mRNA 的 疫苗,以及诱导系统和粘膜免疫和生成肺驻留记忆细胞的新策略。它不报告特定的数据库、搜索日期或荟萃分析方法。
共识与结论
该综述强调,有效的 疫苗 对抗 RSV 需要诱导系统免疫和粘膜免疫,并概述了 疫苗 平台和旨在实现这一目标的策略的最新趋势。它强调了了解免疫应答对于提高疫苗疗效和安全性的重要性。
争议与缺口
该综述指出了粘膜 疫苗接种 的临床前和临床结果之间的差距,表明需要进一步的研究来弥合这种差异。它还要求更好地理解先天的 免疫应答 到 疫苗接种,以识别早期的预测相关性。
科研与实践启示
该审查建议优先研究可诱导针对 RSV 的持久免疫力的 疫苗 平台和策略,重点是粘膜免疫和肺驻留记忆细胞。它还表明需要改进转化研究,以使临床前发现与临床结果保持一致。

Harnessing mucosal and systemic immunity against respiratory viruses: vaccine platforms, delivery strategies, and adjuvants

Authors: Wan-Di Zhu, Duna Heo, Bao‐Zhong Wang, Sang‐Moo Kang
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Long-lasting immunity against respiratory viruses, including influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus, remains a challenge. Effective vaccines and vaccination strategies that confer durable protection against these viruses require the induction of both systemic and mucosal immunity. AREA COVERED: This review covers the latest trends in respiratory viral vaccine platforms, including modified virus-like particles and mRNA-based vaccines, as well as adjuvants, and new vaccination strategies that induce systemic and mucosal immunity and generate lung-resident memory cells. A better understanding of innate immune responses to vaccination is critical for improving vaccine efficacy and safety and for identifying early predictive correlates. EXPERT OPINION: This article outlines recent advances and approaches to developing next-generation respiratory viral vaccines and new vaccination strategies to induce systemic and mucosal immunity. It also discusses the gap between preclinical and clinical outcomes of mucosal vaccination.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), specifically addressing the challenge of achieving long-lasting immunity against RSV, including RSV-A and RSV-B subtypes, and explores vaccine platforms, delivery strategies, and adjuvants that can induce both systemic and mucosal immunity.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of recent advances in RSV vaccine development, covering vaccine platforms such as modified virus-like particles and mRNA-based vaccines, as well as new strategies to induce systemic and mucosal immunity and generate lung-resident memory cells. It does not report specific databases, search dates, or meta-analysis methods.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that effective vaccines against RSV require the induction of both systemic and mucosal immunity, and it outlines recent trends in vaccine platforms and strategies aimed at achieving this. It emphasizes the importance of understanding immune responses to improve vaccine efficacy and safety.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes a gap between preclinical and clinical outcomes of mucosal vaccination, suggesting that further research is needed to bridge this discrepancy. It also calls for a better understanding of innate immune responses to vaccination to identify early predictive correlates.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends prioritizing research into vaccine platforms and strategies that can induce durable immunity against RSV, with a focus on mucosal immunity and lung-resident memory cells. It also suggests the need for improved translational research to align preclinical findings with clinical outcomes.

Vaccination in Adult Patients with Inflammatory Bowel Disease: An Updated Practical Approach

Vaccination in Adult Patients with Inflammatory Bowel Disease: An Updated Practical Approach

Authors: Christian Karime, Francis A. Farraye
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE OF REVIEW: Due to both disease-mediated and therapy-induced immunosuppression, patients with inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) are at increased risk of infections, including from vaccine preventable diseases (VPDs). As such, prevention through vaccination is a key aspect of longitudinal care to decrease infection-associated morbidity. In this review, we aim to outline a practical approach to vaccination in patients with IBD. RECENT FINDINGS: The importance of vaccination for prevention of VPDs has been highlighted in multiple societal guideline updates. In recent years, these guidelines have expanded to include updated recommendations for coronavirus disease 2019, respiratory syncytial virus, and hepatitis B virus immunization. Additional guidance regarding use of live vaccines has also been provided. Having a practical and patient-centered approach to vaccination in patients with IBD is an important part of longitudinal care and prevention of morbidity. Herein we have highlighted risks of infection, the contributing role of immunosuppressive therapy, and comprehensively reviewed recommendations for and approach to vaccination.

Scope and question

Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
Additional guidance regarding use of live vaccines has also been provided.
Research and practice implications
Herein we have highlighted risks of infection, the contributing role of immunosuppressive therapy, and comprehensively reviewed recommendations for and approach to vaccination.

母体 RSV 疫苗接种 在预防婴儿 RSV 相关初级保健就诊方面的有效性:系统评价和荟萃分析

EFFECTIVENESS OF MATERNAL RSV VACCINATION IN PREVENTING RSV-RELATED PRIMARY CARE ATTENDANCE IN INFANTS: A SYSTEMATIC REVIEW AND META-ANALYSIS
作者： Bethany Lambert, Christopher Towriss, Jessica Flanagan, Evangeline Pratt, Martin Oliver Edwards, John Watkins
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疾病给初级保健带来了沉重负担。我们调查了母亲 RSV 疫苗接种 对 RSV 相关初级保健就诊率的影响。关键信息: · 母体 RSV 疫苗接种 减少了婴儿严重 RSV 疾病和住院治疗,但其对初级保健(许多 RSV 疾病在初级保健中得到管理)的影响却知之甚少。
证据基础与方法
这项系统评价和荟萃分析已在 PROSPERO 前瞻性注册(CRD420251169070)。检索了 MEDLINE、Embase、MIDIRS、CINAHL、Cochrane CENTRAL 和 Scopus 2000 年 1 月至 2026 年 1 月期间发表的研究。进行了随机效应荟萃分析。
共识与结论
Maternal vaccination reduced medically attended RSV lower respiratory tract infection (RSV-LRTI) at 90 days (OR 0.51, 95% CI 0.38–0.69; p<0.001), with protection persisting to 180 days (OR 0.53, 95% CI 0.35–0.80; p<0.003). RSV-LRTI hospitalisation was also reduced (OR 0.56, 95% CI 0.42–0.76; p<0.001).
争议与缺口
由于没有研究具体报告初级保健就诊情况,因此使用医疗就诊的 RSV-LRTI 作为替代指标。两项研究的偏倚风险较低,另外两项研究存在一些担忧。初级保健特定证据的缺乏凸显了需要进行现实世界研究,评估孕产妇 RSV 疫苗接种 对初级保健咨询和转诊至二级保健的影响。
科研与实践启示
需要进行现实世界的研究来确认其对初级保健就诊率的影响。孕产妇 RSV 疫苗接种 减少了出生后六个月内就诊的 RSV-LRTI 和住院次数,从而可能减轻初级保健负担。

EFFECTIVENESS OF MATERNAL RSV VACCINATION IN PREVENTING RSV-RELATED PRIMARY CARE ATTENDANCE IN INFANTS: A SYSTEMATIC REVIEW AND META-ANALYSIS

Authors: Bethany Lambert, Christopher Towriss, Jessica Flanagan, Evangeline Pratt, Martin Oliver Edwards, John Watkins
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease places a substantial burden on primary care. Maternal RSV vaccination reduces severe RSV disease and hospitalisation among infants; however, its effects on primary care, where most RSV infections are managed, remain poorly understood. We investigated the effect of maternal RSV vaccination on RSV-related primary care attendance. Methods: This systematic review and meta-analysis was prospectively registered with PROSPERO (CRD420251169070). MEDLINE, Embase, MIDIRS, CINAHL, Cochrane CENTRAL and Scopus were searched for studies published between January 2000 and January 2026. Eligible studies included pregnant women receiving an RSV vaccine, infants born to vaccinated mothers, a placebo or unvaccinated comparator, and RSV-related infant outcomes including medically attended illness and/or hospitalisation. Random-effects meta-analyses were undertaken. As no studies specifically reported primary care attendance, medically attended RSV lower respiratory tract infection (RSV-LRTI) was used as a proxy. Results: Four randomised controlled trials involving 17,289 infants born to mothers receiving RSV vaccination or placebo met inclusion criteria. Maternal vaccination reduced medically attended RSV-LRTI at 90 days (OR 0.51, 95% CI 0.38–0.69; p<0.001), with protection persisting to 180 days (OR 0.53, 95% CI 0.35–0.80; p<0.003). RSV-LRTI hospitalisation was also reduced (OR 0.56, 95% CI 0.42–0.76; p<0.001). Two studies had low risk of bias and two had some concerns. Conclusions: Maternal RSV vaccination reduces medically attended RSV-LRTI and hospitalisation during the first six months of life, potentially reducing primary care burden. Real-world studies are needed to confirm its impact on primary care attendance. Key messages: · Maternal RSV vaccination reduces severe RSV disease and hospitalisation in infants, but its impact in primary care, where much RSV illness is managed, is poorly understood. · Maternal RSV vaccination reduces medically attended RSV-LRTI at 90 and 180 days; however, no studies reported primary care attendance specifically, requiring medically attended RSV-LRTI to be used as a proxy. · The lack of primary care-specific evidence highlights the need for real-world studies assessing the impact of maternal RSV vaccination on primary care consultations and referrals to secondary care.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease places a substantial burden on primary care. We investigated the effect of maternal RSV vaccination on RSV-related primary care attendance. Key messages: · Maternal RSV vaccination reduces severe RSV disease and hospitalisation in infants, but its impact in primary care, where much RSV illness is managed, is poorly understood.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis was prospectively registered with PROSPERO (CRD420251169070). MEDLINE, Embase, MIDIRS, CINAHL, Cochrane CENTRAL and Scopus were searched for studies published between January 2000 and January 2026. Random-effects meta-analyses were undertaken.
Consensus and conclusions
Maternal vaccination reduced medically attended RSV lower respiratory tract infection (RSV-LRTI) at 90 days (OR 0.51, 95% CI 0.38–0.69; p<0.001), with protection persisting to 180 days (OR 0.53, 95% CI 0.35–0.80; p<0.003). RSV-LRTI hospitalisation was also reduced (OR 0.56, 95% CI 0.42–0.76; p<0.001).
Controversies and gaps
As no studies specifically reported primary care attendance, medically attended RSV-LRTI was used as a proxy. Two studies had low risk of bias and two had some concerns. The lack of primary care-specific evidence highlights the need for real-world studies assessing the impact of maternal RSV vaccination on primary care consultations and referrals to secondary care.
Research and practice implications
Real-world studies are needed to confirm its impact on primary care attendance. Maternal RSV vaccination reduces medically attended RSV-LRTI and hospitalisation during the first six months of life, potentially reducing primary care burden.
