病毒学中的球体:弥合传统 2D 培养物和体内模型之间的差距

Spheroids in virology: Bridging the gap between conventional 2D cultures and in vivo models
作者： Feyisayo O. Adepoju, Yu. S. Khramtsova, Іван Анатолійович Іващенко, Anastasia A. Shutova, Polina A. Nevostrueva, Anna V. Ostapchuk, Alexander V. Semenov, Ali Sadek, Ivan A Ivashchenko, И. А. Иващенко
摘要中文翻译二维细胞培养物长期以来一直作为病毒学研究的标准工具,能够在受控条件下研究病毒复制、细胞病变效应、抗病毒药物 活性和 宿主 与病原体相互作用。然而,传统的单层细胞通常无法重现天然组织的结构复杂性、细胞极性、细胞外基质 (ECM) 相互作用和扩散梯度特征。这损害了它们的生理相关性和预测价值,特别是对于依赖组织微环境进行复制和 发病机制 的病毒。三维 (3D) 细胞培养系统(包括球体)在简化的 2D 培养物和动物模型之间提供了一个中间平台,因为它们可以更好地概括细胞间相互作用、空间组织、营养和氧气梯度以及对感染的组织样反应。本综述研究了病毒学研究中使用的球体形成技术,考虑了球体结构对病毒传播和复制的影响,并研究了临床相关病毒中使用的球体模型,包括流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、鼻病毒、冠状病毒、博卡病毒、肝炎病毒和疱疹病毒。审查的证据表明,球体系统可以增强病毒传播、模拟 宿主 反应、支持 抗病毒药物 药物筛选,并提供对病毒 发病机制 的新见解。尽管在标准化、可扩展性、感染动力学分析和组织复杂性的不完整再现方面存在技术挑战,但球体代表了一个可访问且有前途的平台,可推进实验病毒学并缩小体外和体内模型之间的差距。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),研究病毒学研究中使用的球体形成技术、球体结构对病毒传播和复制的影响,以及球体模型在临床相关病毒(包括 RSV)中的使用。
证据基础与方法
The review is a general overview of spheroid systems in virology, discussing their advantages over conventional 2D cultures and their potential for modeling viral behavior. It does not report specific databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or included-study counts.
共识与结论
审查的证据表明,球体系统可以增强病毒传播,模拟 宿主 反应,支持 抗病毒药物 药物筛选,并提供对病毒 发病机制 的新见解,特别是对于 RSV 和其他临床相关病毒。
争议与缺口
该评论指出了标准化和缩放球体系统、分析感染动态以及完全再现组织复杂性方面的技术挑战,这可能会限制它们当前对 RSV 研究的适用性。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要进一步研究来解决球体系统的技术挑战,例如标准化、可扩展性和改进的组织复杂性建模,以更好地支持 RSV 研究。

Spheroids in virology: Bridging the gap between conventional 2D cultures and in vivo models

Authors: Feyisayo O. Adepoju, Yu. S. Khramtsova, Іван Анатолійович Іващенко, Anastasia A. Shutova, Polina A. Nevostrueva, Anna V. Ostapchuk, Alexander V. Semenov, Ali Sadek, Ivan A Ivashchenko, И. А. Иващенко
Original abstract (English)
Two-dimensional cell cultures have long served as a standard tool in virological research, enabling the study of viral replication, cytopathic effects, antiviral activity, and host-pathogen interactions under controlled conditions. However, conventional monolayers often fail to reproduce the structural complexity, cellular polarity, extracellular matrix (ECM) interactions, and diffusion gradients characteristic of native tissues. This compromises their physiological relevance and predictive value, especially for viruses that depend on tissue microenvironments for replication and pathogenesis. Three-dimensional (3D) cell culture systems, including spheroids, provide an intermediate platform between simplified 2D cultures and animal models, as they better recapitulate cell-cell interactions, spatial organization, nutrient and oxygen gradients, and tissue-like responses to infection. This review examines spheroid formation techniques used in virological studies, considers the influence of spheroid architecture on viral dissemination and replication, and looks at spheroid models used in clinically relevant viruses, including influenza viruses, respiratory syncytial virus, rhinoviruses, coronaviruses, bocavirus, hepatitis viruses, and herpesviruses. The evidence reviewed suggests that spheroid systems can enhance viral propagation, model host responses, support antiviral drug screening, and offer new insights into viral pathogenesis. Spheroids represent an accessible and promising platform to advance experimental virology and close the gap between in vitro and in vivo models despite technical challenges in standardization, scalability, analysis of infection dynamics, and incomplete reproduction of tissue complexity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), examining spheroid formation techniques used in virological studies, the influence of spheroid architecture on viral dissemination and replication, and the use of spheroid models in clinically relevant viruses, including RSV.
Evidence base and method
The review is a general overview of spheroid systems in virology, discussing their advantages over conventional 2D cultures and their potential for modeling viral behavior. It does not report specific databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or included-study counts.
Consensus and conclusions
The evidence reviewed suggests that spheroid systems can enhance viral propagation, model host responses, support antiviral drug screening, and offer new insights into viral pathogenesis, particularly for RSV and other clinically relevant viruses.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes technical challenges in standardizing and scaling spheroid systems, analyzing infection dynamics, and fully reproducing tissue complexity, which may limit their current applicability for RSV research.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for further research to address the technical challenges in spheroid systems, such as standardization, scalability, and improved modeling of tissue complexity, to better support RSV studies.

用于传染病的脂质纳米颗粒 – mRNA 疫苗:打造可预测的有效平台

Lipid Nanoparticle–mRNA Vaccines for Infectious Diseases: Making a Potent Platform Predictable
作者： Yun-Wang Zhao, 程宏献, Shi-Yi Zhou, Lei He, Chen-Lin Xiu, Bai-Hai Huang, Xiong-Xiong Lu, Hong-Xian Cheng
摘要中文翻译脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP)-mRNA 疫苗 将从病原体序列到许可产品的路径压缩到不到一年的时间,现已被批准用于三种病毒性疾病(COVID-19、呼吸道合胞病毒 和季节性流感),有 20 多种适应症处于临床开发中。该平台的效力已毋庸置疑;其可预测性是。我们认为,目前的进展受到三个很大程度上独立设计的界面的限制:脂质-RNA界面,它决定封装、内部结构和储存稳定性;颗粒-细胞界面,它设定向性和内体逃逸的效率;以及粒子-免疫界面,它设定佐剂性和反应原性之间的平衡。第一个界面存在定量设计规则,第二个界面仍然是经验性的,而第三个界面几乎不存在,这就是为什么制剂开发仍然通过筛选而不是通过计算进行。我们回顾了 mRNA 负载的分子设计和结构抗原的选择;LNP 的化学、制造和细胞内运输;将先天激活和树突状细胞交叉呈递与生发中心输出联系起来的免疫链;以及 COVID-19、呼吸道合胞病毒、流感、巨细胞病毒、HIV、个性化癌症 疫苗 和其他目标的临床证据。我们通过确定测量、药典质量属性和设计规则得出结论,这些规则将使下一代 LNP-mRNA 疫苗 可预测,而不是凭经验得出。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注脂质纳米颗粒(LNP)-疫苗(RSV)的mRNA 疫苗,包括其开发、分子设计和临床应用。核心问题是如何使这些 疫苗 变得可预测而不是经验性的,特别是在 RSV 和相关病毒性疾病的背景下。
证据基础与方法
该综述对 mRNA 有效负载的分子设计、结构抗原选择、LNP 化学和制造、细胞内运输、免疫机制以及包括 RSV 在内的各种病毒靶点的临床证据进行了全面分析。审查方法涉及综合多项研究的结果并强调配方开发的经验性质。
共识与结论
审查的结论是,该平台的效力已确立,但其可预测性仍然是一个挑战。它确定了对测量、药典质量属性和设计规则的需求,以使下一代 LNP-mRNA 疫苗 可预测而不是经验。
争议与缺口
该综述强调,脂质-RNA 界面存在定量设计规则,但颗粒-细胞界面仍然是经验性的,颗粒-免疫界面几乎不存在。后两个界面缺乏可预测性是一个关键的证据差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,未来的研究应侧重于开发颗粒-细胞和颗粒-免疫界面的定量设计规则,以提高 LNP-mRNA 疫苗 对 RSV 和其他靶标的可预测性和效率。

Lipid Nanoparticle–mRNA Vaccines for Infectious Diseases: Making a Potent Platform Predictable

Authors: Yun-Wang Zhao, 程宏献, Shi-Yi Zhou, Lei He, Chen-Lin Xiu, Bai-Hai Huang, Xiong-Xiong Lu, Hong-Xian Cheng
Original abstract (English)
Lipid nanoparticle (LNP)–mRNA vaccines compressed the path from pathogen sequence to licensed product into less than a year and are now approved for three viral diseases (COVID-19, respiratory syncytial virus and seasonal influenza), with more than twenty indications in clinical development. The platform’s potency is no longer in doubt; its predictability is. We argue that progress is now limited by three interfaces that are engineered largely independently: the lipid–RNA interface, which sets encapsulation, internal structure and storage stability; the particle–cell interface, which sets tropism and the efficiency of endosomal escape; and the particle–immune interface, which sets the balance between adjuvanticity and reactogenicity. Quantitative design rules exist for the first interface, remain empirical for the second, and are almost absent for the third, which is why formulation development still proceeds by screening rather than by calculation. We review the molecular design of the mRNA payload and structural antigen selection; the chemistry, manufacture and intracellular trafficking of LNPs; the immunological chain that links innate activation and dendritic-cell cross-presentation to germinal-centre output; and the clinical evidence across COVID-19, respiratory syncytial virus, influenza, cytomegalovirus, HIV, personalised cancer vaccines and other targets. We conclude by identifying the measurements, pharmacopoeial quality attributes and design rules that would make the next generation of LNP–mRNA vaccines predictable rather than empirical.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on lipid nanoparticle (LNP)–mRNA vaccines for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), including their development, molecular design, and clinical applications. The central question is how to make these vaccines predictable rather than empirical, particularly in the context of RSV and related viral diseases.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive analysis of the molecular design of mRNA payloads, structural antigen selection, LNP chemistry and manufacturing, intracellular trafficking, immunological mechanisms, and clinical evidence across various viral targets, including RSV. The review method involves synthesizing findings from multiple studies and highlighting the empirical nature of formulation development.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the platform’s potency is established, but its predictability remains a challenge. It identifies the need for measurements, pharmacopoeial quality attributes, and design rules to make the next generation of LNP–mRNA vaccines predictable rather than empirical.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that quantitative design rules exist for the lipid–RNA interface, but remain empirical for the particle–cell interface and almost absent for the particle–immune interface. This lack of predictability in the latter two interfaces is a key evidence gap.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on developing quantitative design rules for the particle–cell and particle–immune interfaces to improve the predictability and efficiency of LNP–mRNA vaccines for RSV and other targets.

一致的证据,一致的指导:AAFP、AAP、ACOG 和 IDSA 的 2026-2027 年呼吸道病毒免疫建议。

Aligned Evidence, Aligned Guidance: 2026-2027 Respiratory Virus Immunization Recommendations from The AAFP, AAP, ACOG, AND IDSA.
作者： Margot Savoy, Corban Wehr, LaTasha Seliby-Perkins
摘要中文翻译联邦 疫苗 咨询流程的中断促使采取协调一致的、社会主导的 2026-2027 季节呼吸道病毒免疫指南。明尼苏达大学传染病研究和政策中心的 疫苗 完整性项目 (VIP) 与美国医学会合作,并在医学专业协会理事会的协调支持下,完成了流感、COVID-19 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫的系统审查。在 299 项研究中,审查证实可以防止住院和死亡,并且在比较研究中没有发现新的安全问题。2026 年 9 月 2 日,美国家庭医师学会 (AAFP)、美国儿科学会、美国妇产科医师学会和美国传染病学会根据这一证据发布了一致的建议。该报告解释了共享的证据基础,总结了按年龄和人口划分的建议,描述了 AAFP 审查和采用过程,并指导初级保健临床医生实施和患者资源。

范围与问题
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和相关人类 呼吸道合胞病毒(人正肺病毒)、RSV-A 和 RSV-B。核心问题是 2026-2027 年季节呼吸道病毒免疫的证据基础和建议。
证据基础与方法
明尼苏达大学传染病研究和政策中心的 疫苗 诚信项目 (VIP) 与美国医学会合作,并在医学专业协会理事会的协调支持下,完成了 299 项研究中流感、COVID-19 和 RSV 免疫的系统评价。
共识与结论
在 299 项研究中,审查证实可以防止住院和死亡,并且在比较研究中没有发现新的安全问题。美国家庭医师学会 (AAFP)、美国儿科学会、美国妇产科医师学会和美国传染病学会根据这一证据发布了一致的建议。
争议与缺口

科研与实践启示
初级保健临床医生负责实施和患者资源。该报告解释了共享证据基础,总结了按年龄和人口划分的建议,并描述了 AAFP 审查和采用过程。

Aligned Evidence, Aligned Guidance: 2026-2027 Respiratory Virus Immunization Recommendations from The AAFP, AAP, ACOG, AND IDSA.

Authors: Margot Savoy, Corban Wehr, LaTasha Seliby-Perkins
Original abstract (English)
Disruption of the federal vaccine advisory process prompted a coordinated, society-led approach to respiratory virus immunization guidance for the 2026-2027 season. The Vaccine Integrity Project (VIP) at the University of Minnesota Center for Infectious Disease Research and Policy, in collaboration with the American Medical Association and with coordination support from the Council of Medical Specialty Societies, completed systematic reviews of influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunization. Across 299 studies, the reviews confirmed protection against hospitalization and death and identified no new safety concerns in comparative studies. On September 2, 2026, the American Academy of Family Physicians (AAFP), American Academy of Pediatrics, American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists, and Infectious Diseases Society of America issued aligned recommendations informed by this evidence. This report explains the shared evidence base, summarizes recommendations by age and population, describes the AAFP review and adoption process, and directs primary care clinicians to implementation and patient resources.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and related human respiratory syncytial virus (Orthopneumovirus hominis), RSV-A and RSV-B. The central question is the evidence base and recommendations for respiratory virus immunization for the 2026-2027 season.
Evidence base and method
The Vaccine Integrity Project (VIP) at the University of Minnesota Center for Infectious Disease Research and Policy, in collaboration with the American Medical Association and with coordination support from the Council of Medical Specialty Societies, completed systematic reviews of influenza, COVID-19, and RSV immunization across 299 studies.
Consensus and conclusions
Across 299 studies, the reviews confirmed protection against hospitalization and death and identified no new safety concerns in comparative studies. The American Academy of Family Physicians (AAFP), American Academy of Pediatrics, American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists, and Infectious Diseases Society of America issued aligned recommendations informed by this evidence.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence base does not report any heterogeneity or conflicting findings. However, the reviews did not identify any new safety concerns in comparative studies, and the evidence does not report any unresolved mechanisms or concrete evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Primary care clinicians are directed to implementation and patient resources. The report explains the shared evidence base, summarizes recommendations by age and population, and describes the AAFP review and adoption process.

对某区总医院儿科急诊科使用 毛细支气管炎 的 2 岁以下儿童不必要的胸部 X 射线检查

AN AUDIT OF UNNECESSARY CHEST X-RAY USE IN CHILDREN UNDER 2 YEARS WITH BRONCHIOLITIS IN A DISTRICT GENERAL HOSPITAL PAEDIATRIC EMERGENCY DEPARTMENT
作者： Tim Carr, Reshma Razack
摘要中文翻译背景:毛细支气管炎 是一种常见的下呼吸道感染,最常见的原因是 RSV,在一岁以下的婴儿中峰值为 发病率,男孩的发病率是女孩的 1.5-2 倍。它通常是自限性的,仅需要支持性治疗,但在某些情况下,可能会出现细菌感染、呼吸窘迫、缺氧和呼吸暂停等急性并发症,需要入院或进行医疗治疗。还可能出现后遗症,例如反复喘息和哮喘。经验不足或消息灵通的卫生专业人员可能会像成年人一样进行调查和管理,这可能导致儿童过度使用胸部 X 光检查以及本可避免的辐射暴露。英国国家临床卓越研究所 (NICE) 发布了 毛细支气管炎 管理指南,但缺乏遵守情况。“不要对患有 毛细支气管炎 的儿童进行常规胸部 X 光检查。” - 好的。方法:对 2026 年 1 月至 2026 年 4 月期间首次就诊且临床症状为 诊断 的临床 毛细支气管炎 到皇家阿尔伯特爱德华医院急症室儿科急诊科就诊的 0-24 个月婴儿进行回顾性临床审核。171 人中有 130 人符合标准。排除标准包括需要补充氧气、严重呼吸窘迫、患有慢性肺病、免疫缺陷、神经肌肉疾病、先天性心脏病等重大合并症以及两周内曾因相同疾病接受过 CXR 检查的患者。评估接受 CXR 的患者及其原因、谁进行了检查、是否入院以及临床不确定性。结果:130 名患者中有 16 名(12.3%)接受了胸部 X 光检查,130 名患者中有 12 名(9.2%)入院。根据 NICE 指南规定的标准,在接受胸部 X 光检查的人中,16 人中有 11 人(68.8%)没有正当理由。20% 的病例存在诊断不确定性。81.4%的病例第一评估级别为住院医生。在进行的胸部 X 光检查中,75% 的结果显示正常。81.8% 的不合理胸部 X 光检查报告为正常,同时 60% 的合理 CXR 报告为正常。在不合理的类别中注意到两个异常: 1. 双侧支气管周围壁轻度增厚。否则,无局灶性肺部病变。右心旁心旁小片状不明确的斑片状混浊可能提示早期感染过程。否则,肺部无局灶性病变。合理类别中注意到两个异常: 1. 右心后和右下区混浊轮廓增加 右半膈可能感染。轻微的支气管周围袖带提示小气道炎症。接受 CXR 的患者与入院患者之间存在很强的正相关性 φ = 0.528,p=0.0000031。接受 CXR 的患者与诊断不确定性 p=0.0006 之间存在统计显着的中等相关性。虽然第一位评估员通常是居民,但不存在统计相关性 (p=0.1915)。请参阅 CXR 表的原因(表 1)。结论:87.7% 的人没有接受胸部 X 光检查。这反映了大多数情况下合理的临床判断,但这低于 100% 的目标。一些潜在的原因可能是临床不确定性,在这种情况下 CXR 可以帮助区分。此次审计显示,信心级别更有可能是 CXR 请求的原因,而不是评估员级别。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 2 岁以下 毛细支气管炎 儿童不必要的胸部 X 光检查,特别检查胸部 X 光检查的使用频率、使用原因以及与入院和诊断不确定性的相关性。核心问题是胸部 X 光检查是否适合该儿科人群。
证据基础与方法
本次回顾性临床审计审查了2026年1月至2026年4月期间在皇家爱德华医院急诊科就诊的0-24个月婴儿,包括临床诊断为毛细支气管炎的患者,排除了需要补充氧气、严重呼吸窘迫、有显著合并症或过去2周内有胸部X光检查记录的患者。审计方法涉及评估胸部X光检查请求的原因、评估者级别、入院状态和诊断不确定性。
共识与结论
审计发现,12.3%的患者接受了胸部X光检查,其中68.8%的患者没有按照NICE指南的正当理由接受了胸部X光检查。此外,75% 的胸部 X 光检查结果显示正常,并且胸部 X 光检查的使用与入院之间存在很强的正相关性 (φ = 0.528,p=0.0000031)。信心水平更有可能是要求进行胸部 X 光检查的原因,而不是评估者水平。
争议与缺口
胸部 X 光检查的使用与诊断不确定性之间存在统计学上显着的中度相关性 (p=0.0006)。然而,该综述并未提供有关具体诊断不确定性或这些不确定性对临床决策影响的详细信息。该研究也没有探讨不必要的胸部 X 光检查在辐射暴露或成本效益方面的潜在后果。
科研与实践启示
此次审计强调需要更好地遵守 NICE 指南,以减少 毛细支气管炎 儿童不必要的胸部 X 光检查。它表明进一步的研究应侧重于了解诊断不确定性的原因并制定策略以改善此类病例的临床判断。此外,需要对卫生专业人员进行更好的教育和培训,以确保在这一人群中适当使用成像技术。

AN AUDIT OF UNNECESSARY CHEST X-RAY USE IN CHILDREN UNDER 2 YEARS WITH BRONCHIOLITIS IN A DISTRICT GENERAL HOSPITAL PAEDIATRIC EMERGENCY DEPARTMENT

Authors: Tim Carr, Reshma Razack
Original abstract (English)
Background: Bronchiolitis is a common lower respiratory tract infection most commonly due to RSV with peak incidence in infants less than a year old, 1.5-2 times more common in boys than girls. It is typically self-limiting and requires solely supportive management, however, in some cases, acute complications such as bacterial infection, respiratory distress, hypoxia and apnoea may occur, requiring admission or medical management. Sequelae can also arise such as recurrent wheeze and asthma. Less experienced or informed health professionals may be tempted to investigate and manage like adults, and this can lead to the over-use of chest x-rays in children and avoidable exposure to radiation. The National Institute of Clinical Excellence (NICE) in England have published guidelines in bronchiolitis management however adherence is lacking. “Do not routinely perform a chest x-ray in children with bronchiolitis.” - NICE. Methods: Retrospective clinical audit of infants aged 0-24 months who presented to Paediatric Emergency Department at Royal Albert Edward Infirmary A&E between January 2026 and April 2026 in first presentation of illness with a clinical diagnosis of bronchiolitis were included. 130 out of 171 met the criteria. Exclusion criteria included those with supplemental oxygen requirements, severe respiratory distress, significant comorbidity such as chronic lung disease, immunodeficiency, neuromuscular disorders, congenital heart disease and previous CXR for the same illness within 2 weeks. Assessing those who received CXR and why, who reviewed, whether they were admitted and clinical uncertainty. Results: 16 out of 130 ( 12.3%) of patients received chest x-rays, 12 out of 130 ( 9.2%) were admitted. Of those who received chest x-rays,11 out of 16 ( 68.8% ) had no justifiable reason as per criteria set out by NICE guidelines. There was diagnostic uncertainty in 20% of cases. First assessor level was resident doctor in 81.4% of cases. Of the chest x-rays performed, 75% were reported normal. 81.8% of unjustified chest x-rays were reported as normal, meanwhile 60% of justified CXR’s were normal. Two abnormalities noted within the unjustified category: 1. Bilateral mild peri bronchial wall thickening. Otherwise, No focal lung lesions. Small ill-defined right paracardial patchy opacity could suggest early infective process. Otherwise, no focal lung lesions. Two abnormalities noted within the justified category: 1. Right retrocardiac and Right lower zonal increased opacification silhouetting Right hemidiaphragm likely infective. Mild peri-bronchial cuffing suggesting small airways inflammation. There was a strong positive correlation between those who received CXR and those who were admitted φ = 0.528, p=0.0000031. A statistically significant moderate correlation existed between those who received a CXR and diagnostic uncertainty p=0.0006 . While first assessor was commonly resident, there was no statistical correlation (p=0.1915). See Reason for CXR table (Table 1). Conclusions: 87.7% did not receive chest x-rays. Which reflects sound clinical judgment in the majority of cases, but this is below the 100% target. Some potential reasons may be clinical uncertainty, in which case a CXR can help differentiate. This audit revealed level of confidence was more likely the reason for CXR request than assessor level.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the unnecessary use of chest x-rays in children under 2 years with bronchiolitis, specifically examining the frequency of chest x-ray use, reasons for its use, and the correlation with admission and diagnostic uncertainty. The central question is whether chest x-rays are appropriately used in this pediatric population.
Evidence base and method
The review conducted a retrospective clinical audit of infants aged 0-24 months who presented to the Paediatric Emergency Department at Royal Albert Edward Infirmary A&E between January 2026 and April 2026. The audit included patients with a clinical diagnosis of bronchiolitis, excluding those with supplemental oxygen requirements, severe respiratory distress, significant comorbidity, or previous CXR within 2 weeks. The methods involved assessing the reasons for chest x-ray requests, assessor levels, admission status, and diagnostic uncertainty.
Consensus and conclusions
The audit found that 12.3% of patients received chest x-rays, with 68.8% of these having no justifiable reason according to NICE guidelines. Additionally, 75% of chest x-rays were reported as normal, and there was a strong positive correlation between chest x-ray use and admission (φ = 0.528, p=0.0000031). The level of confidence was more likely the reason for chest x-ray requests than the assessor level.
Controversies and gaps
There was a statistically significant moderate correlation between chest x-ray use and diagnostic uncertainty (p=0.0006). However, the review does not provide detailed information on the specific diagnostic uncertainties or the impact of these uncertainties on clinical decision-making. The study also does not explore the potential consequences of unnecessary chest x-ray use in terms of radiation exposure or cost-effectiveness.
Research and practice implications
The audit highlights the need for improved adherence to NICE guidelines to reduce unnecessary chest x-ray use in children with bronchiolitis. It suggests that further research should focus on understanding the reasons for diagnostic uncertainty and developing strategies to improve clinical judgment in such cases. Additionally, there is a need for better education and training for health professionals to ensure appropriate use of imaging in this population.

粘膜 疫苗 对抗儿童呼吸道病毒感染:当前进展、挑战和未来前景

Mucosal Vaccines Against Respiratory Viral Infections in Children: Current Advances, Challenges and Future Perspectives
作者： Marta Druszcz, Zuzanna Grabowska, Krzysztof Domański, Klaudia Kośla, Aleksandra Trzyna, Martyna Śliwińska, Anna Rakuś-Kwiatosz
摘要中文翻译呼吸道病毒感染仍然是全世界儿童发病、住院和医疗负担的主要原因。虽然传统的注射剂 疫苗 可有效预防严重疾病,但它们在病毒进入初始部位诱导强烈粘膜免疫的能力往往受到限制。因此,粘膜 疫苗接种 被认为是改善局部和全身 免疫应答 并潜在减少病原体 传播 的有前途的策略。本研究的目的是回顾儿科人群粘膜 疫苗 发育的最新进展,重点是粘膜保护的免疫学基础、与免疫系统发育年龄相关的特征以及最相关的 疫苗 平台,包括鼻内、口服、病毒载体和基于纳米颗粒的方法。我们讨论了粘膜 疫苗 针对影响儿童的主要呼吸道病毒病原体(包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感病毒和 SARS-CoV-2)的最新进展,并回顾了现有的临床前和临床证据以支持其使用。最后,我们提出了限制粘膜 疫苗 在儿科人群中广泛实施的主要挑战,包括安全性、免疫应答 的变异性和监管考虑。最后,我们强调未来的方向和新兴技术可能有助于开发安全、有效和持久的粘膜 疫苗接种 策略来预防儿童呼吸道病毒感染。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
呼吸道病毒感染仍然是全世界儿童发病、住院和医疗负担的主要原因。本研究的目的是回顾儿科人群粘膜 疫苗 发育的最新进展,重点是粘膜保护的免疫学基础、与免疫系统发育年龄相关的特征以及最相关的 疫苗 平台,包括鼻内、口服、病毒载体和基于纳米颗粒的方法。
证据基础与方法
我们讨论了粘膜 疫苗 针对影响儿童的主要呼吸道病毒病原体(包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感病毒和 SARS-CoV-2)的最新进展,并回顾了现有的临床前和临床证据以支持其使用。
共识与结论
因此,粘膜 疫苗接种 被认为是改善局部和全身 免疫应答 并潜在减少病原体 传播 的有前途的策略。
争议与缺口
提供的证据没有报告与粘膜 疫苗 相对于 呼吸道合胞病毒 相关的具体争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
最后,我们强调未来的方向和新兴技术可能有助于开发安全、有效和持久的粘膜 疫苗接种 策略来预防儿童呼吸道病毒感染。

Mucosal Vaccines Against Respiratory Viral Infections in Children: Current Advances, Challenges and Future Perspectives

Authors: Marta Druszcz, Zuzanna Grabowska, Krzysztof Domański, Klaudia Kośla, Aleksandra Trzyna, Martyna Śliwińska, Anna Rakuś-Kwiatosz
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory viral infections remain a leading cause of morbidity, hospitalization, and healthcare burden among children worldwide. While traditional injectable vaccines have been effective in preventing severe disease, they are often limited in their ability to induce strong mucosal immunity at the initial sites of viral entry. Mucosal vaccination has therefore been proposed as a promising strategy to improve local and systemic immune responses and to potentially decrease pathogen transmission. The aim of this study is to review recent advances in mucosal vaccine development for pediatric populations, with an emphasis on the immunological basis of mucosal protection, features related to the age of the developing immune system, and the most relevant vaccine platforms, including intranasal, oral, viral-vectored, and nanoparticle-based approaches. We discuss recent advances in mucosal vaccine development against the main respiratory viral pathogens affecting children, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza viruses, and SARS-CoV-2, and review available preclinical and clinical evidence to support their use. Finally, we present major challenges limiting the widespread implementation of mucosal vaccines in pediatric populations, including safety, variability of immune responses, and regulatory considerations. We conclude by highlighting future directions and emerging technologies that may aid the development of safe, effective, and long-lasting mucosal vaccination strategies for the prevention of respiratory viral infections in children.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory viral infections remain a leading cause of morbidity, hospitalization, and healthcare burden among children worldwide. The aim of this study is to review recent advances in mucosal vaccine development for pediatric populations, with an emphasis on the immunological basis of mucosal protection, features related to the age of the developing immune system, and the most relevant vaccine platforms, including intranasal, oral, viral-vectored, and nanoparticle-based approaches.
Evidence base and method
We discuss recent advances in mucosal vaccine development against the main respiratory viral pathogens affecting children, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza viruses, and SARS-CoV-2, and review available preclinical and clinical evidence to support their use.
Consensus and conclusions
Mucosal vaccination has therefore been proposed as a promising strategy to improve local and systemic immune responses and to potentially decrease pathogen transmission.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report specific controversies or evidence gaps related to mucosal vaccines against respiratory syncytial virus.
Research and practice implications
We conclude by highlighting future directions and emerging technologies that may aid the development of safe, effective, and long-lasting mucosal vaccination strategies for the prevention of respiratory viral infections in children.

儿科呼吸道感染的肠道微生物群模式:微生物特征的系统评价

GUT MICROBIOTA PATTERNS IN PEDIATRIC RESPIRATORY INFECTIONS: A SYSTEMATIC REVIEW OF MICROBIAL SIGNATURES
作者： Yasmin Petter Pires, Guilherme da Silva Machado, Bruna Daniel Rabelo, Karen Conte Kreusch, Nathália Coral Galvani, Geovana D. Savi
摘要中文翻译生命早期的肠道微生物群在免疫成熟中发挥着至关重要的作用,并且与 宿主 对传染病的反应越来越相关。新的证据强调肠肺轴是儿科呼吸健康的重要组成部分,但以儿童为中心的综合仍然有限。这篇带有定性分析的系统综述总结了最近评估急性呼吸道感染儿童肠道微生物群概况的原始研究,提供了与结合年龄组或关注单一病原体的综述不同的针对儿童的视角。对 PubMed 和虚拟健康图书馆进行了系统搜索,并使用结构化矩阵提取数据,以确定微生物组成的模式以及方法学的优势和差距。12 项研究(2019-2025)包括年龄为 0-18 岁、患有 COVID-19、呼吸道合胞病毒 感染、毛细支气管炎 和复发性呼吸道感染等疾病的参与者,这些研究符合预定的纳入标准。各项研究中,患有呼吸道感染的儿童始终表现出肠道菌群失调,其特征是有益类群的消耗和促炎属的富集。虽然方法上的差异,包括研究设计、采样时间和混杂因素的处理,限制了直接定量比较,但微生物改变的模式是可识别的。总的来说,这篇综述强调了肠道微生物群改变与儿童呼吸道感染之间的一致关联,支持了早期生命中肠-肺轴的相关性。然而,方法论的异质性阻碍了对因果关系的推断,凸显了需要精心设计的纵向和干预性研究,以更好地理解这些关联的方向性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染儿童的肠道微生物群模式,特别检查与 RSV 毛细支气管炎 和 RSV 感染相关的微生物特征。核心问题是肠道微生物群的改变如何与儿童呼吸道感染(特别是 RSV 相关疾病)相关,以及它们在 免疫应答 中的作用。
证据基础与方法
该综述对 PubMed 和虚拟健康图书馆进行了系统检索,涵盖 2019 年至 2025 年的研究。使用结构化矩阵提取数据,以确定微生物组成模式以及方法学优势和差距。该综述包括 12 项研究,参与者年龄为 0-18 岁。
共识与结论
在这 12 项研究中,RSV 感染的儿童始终表现出肠道菌群失调,其特征是有益类群的消耗和促炎菌属的富集。这支持了生命早期肠肺轴的相关性,并强调了肠道微生物群改变与儿童呼吸道感染之间的一致关联。
争议与缺口
方法学异质性,包括研究设计、采样时间和混杂因素处理的差异,限制了直接定量比较。这阻碍了对因果关系的推断,表明需要进行更标准化和纵向的研究,以更好地理解这些关联的方向性。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调了精心设计的纵向和介入研究的重要性,以探索肠道微生物群与 RSV 感染之间的因果关系。它还表明需要对儿科呼吸道感染的微生物特征进行进一步研究,以便为有针对性的干预措施提供信息并改善临床结果。

GUT MICROBIOTA PATTERNS IN PEDIATRIC RESPIRATORY INFECTIONS: A SYSTEMATIC REVIEW OF MICROBIAL SIGNATURES

Authors: Yasmin Petter Pires, Guilherme da Silva Machado, Bruna Daniel Rabelo, Karen Conte Kreusch, Nathália Coral Galvani, Geovana D. Savi
Original abstract (English)
Early-life gut microbiota plays a crucial role in immune maturation and has been increasingly associated with host responses to infectious diseases. Emerging evidence highlights the gut–lung axis as an important component of pediatric respiratory health, yet child-focused syntheses remain limited. This systematic review with qualitative analysis summarizes recent original studies evaluating gut microbiota profiles in children with acute respiratory infections, providing a child-specific perspective distinct from reviews that combine age groups or focus on single pathogens. PubMed and the Virtual Health Library were systematically searched and the data were extracted using a structured matrix to identify patterns in microbial composition and methodological strengths and gaps. Twelve studies (2019–2025) including participants aged 0–18 years with conditions such as COVID-19, respiratory syncytial virus infection, bronchiolitis, and recurrent respiratory tract infections met predefined inclusion criteria. Across studies, children with respiratory infections consistently exhibited gut dysbiosis, characterized by depletion of beneficial taxa and enrichment of pro-inflammatory genera. While methodological differences, including study design, sampling timing, and handling of confounders, limited direct quantitative comparisons, patterns of microbial alterations were identifiable. Overall, this review highlights a consistent association between gut microbiota alterations and pediatric respiratory infections, supporting the relevance of the gut–lung axis in early life. Nevertheless, methodological heterogeneity prevents inference about cause-and-effect relationships, highlighting the need for well-designed longitudinal and interventional studies to better understand the directionality of these associations.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the gut microbiota patterns in children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, specifically examining microbial signatures associated with RSV bronchiolitis and RSV infection. The central question is how gut microbiota alterations are linked to pediatric respiratory infections, particularly RSV-related conditions, and their role in immune responses.
Evidence base and method
The review conducted a systematic search of PubMed and the Virtual Health Library, covering studies from 2019 to 2025. Data were extracted using a structured matrix to identify patterns in microbial composition and methodological strengths and gaps. The review included twelve studies with participants aged 0–18 years.
Consensus and conclusions
Across the twelve studies, children with RSV infections consistently exhibited gut dysbiosis, characterized by depletion of beneficial taxa and enrichment of pro-inflammatory genera. This supports the relevance of the gut–lung axis in early life and highlights a consistent association between gut microbiota alterations and pediatric respiratory infections.
Controversies and gaps
Methodological heterogeneity, including variations in study design, sampling timing, and handling of confounders, limited direct quantitative comparisons. This prevents inference about cause-and-effect relationships, indicating a need for more standardized and longitudinal studies to better understand the directionality of these associations.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the importance of well-designed longitudinal and interventional studies to explore the causal relationships between gut microbiota and RSV infections. It also suggests the need for further research on microbial signatures in pediatric respiratory infections to inform targeted interventions and improve clinical outcomes.

癌症之外的免疫检查点调节:小分子 宿主 导向的 抗病毒药物 治疗的概念框架

Immune checkpoint modulation beyond cancer: a conceptual framework for small-molecule host-directed antiviral therapy
作者： Xiaowei Tian, Shuoxiang Gao, Yingzhuo Dou, Jiyan Cui, Mengyi Li, Ruixin Yang, Wen Wang, Xianfeng Hui
摘要中文翻译免疫检查点是抗病毒药物免疫的重要调节因子,但其在病毒感染中的作用比传统的免疫抑制模型更为复杂。虽然持续的检查点信号传导会损害 抗病毒药物 T 细胞功能并导致病毒持续存在,但它也可以限制免疫介导的组织损伤。这种双重作用对治疗干预提出了重大挑战,因为不加区别的检查点抑制可能会以增强免疫病理学为代价来改善病毒控制。在这篇综述中,我们研究了病毒感染如何通过 抗病毒药物 信号传导、蛋白质稳态、细胞代谢和组织特异性免疫调节重塑免疫检查点网络。我们讨论这些机制对慢性病毒感染的影响,包括人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型和丙型肝炎病毒,以及甲型流感病毒、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2和呼吸道合胞病毒引起的急性呼吸道感染。特别关注小分子检查点调节剂及其调节蛋白质相互作用、稳定性、定位和信号传导超越传统受体-配体阻断的潜力。我们将支持这些化合物的药理学证据(主要来自肿瘤学相关研究)与其直接 抗病毒药物 活性的更为有限的证据区分开来。然而,表明这些化合物可以实现有效的检查点调节,同时改善病毒控制和限制组织损伤的直接证据仍然有限。因此,我们讨论了严格的目标验证以及更精确的患者选择和 治疗 设计方法的必要性。整合病毒负荷、抗病毒药物 T 细胞功能、检查点活性和炎症状态可能有助于识别最有可能从干预中受益的患者,并指导 治疗 的时机、强度和停药。如果病毒抑制和免疫恢复能够适当平衡,那么将检查点调节与直接作用的 抗病毒药物 或其他 宿主 定向疗法相结合可能会带来额外的好处。从持续的检查点封锁转向上下文相关的免疫调节可能为开发更精确的 宿主 导向的 抗病毒药物 策略提供基础。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
免疫检查点是抗病毒药物免疫的重要调节因子,但其在病毒感染中的作用比传统的免疫抑制模型更为复杂。
证据基础与方法
在这篇综述中,我们研究了病毒感染如何通过 抗病毒药物 信号传导、蛋白质稳态、细胞代谢和组织特异性免疫调节重塑免疫检查点网络。
共识与结论
这种双重作用对治疗干预提出了重大挑战,因为不加区别的检查点抑制可能会以增强免疫病理学为代价来改善病毒控制。
争议与缺口
我们将支持这些化合物的药理学证据(主要来自肿瘤学相关研究)与其直接 抗病毒药物 活性的更为有限的证据区分开来。然而,表明这些化合物可以实现有效的检查点调节,同时改善病毒控制和限制组织损伤的直接证据仍然有限。
科研与实践启示
因此,我们讨论了严格的目标验证以及更精确的患者选择和 治疗 设计方法的必要性。整合病毒负荷、抗病毒药物 T 细胞功能、检查点活性和炎症状态可能有助于识别最有可能从干预中受益的患者,并指导 治疗 的时机、强度和停药。

Immune checkpoint modulation beyond cancer: a conceptual framework for small-molecule host-directed antiviral therapy

Authors: Xiaowei Tian, Shuoxiang Gao, Yingzhuo Dou, Jiyan Cui, Mengyi Li, Ruixin Yang, Wen Wang, Xianfeng Hui
Original abstract (English)
Immune checkpoints are important regulators of antiviral immunity, but their roles in viral infections are more complex than the conventional model of immune inhibition. Although sustained checkpoint signaling can impair antiviral T-cell function and contribute to viral persistence, it can also limit immune-mediated tissue damage. This dual role presents a major challenge for therapeutic intervention, as indiscriminate checkpoint inhibition may improve viral control at the expense of increased immunopathology. In this review, we examine how viral infections reshape immune checkpoint networks through antiviral signaling, protein homeostasis, cellular metabolism, and tissue-specific immune regulation. We discuss the implications of these mechanisms for chronic viral infections, including human immunodeficiency virus and hepatitis B and C viruses, as well as acute respiratory infections caused by influenza A virus, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2, and respiratory syncytial virus. Particular attention is given to small-molecule checkpoint modulators and their potential to regulate protein interactions, stability, localization, and signaling beyond conventional receptor–ligand blockade. We distinguish the pharmacological evidence supporting these compounds, which is derived primarily from oncology-related research, from the much more limited evidence of their direct antiviral activity. However, direct evidence that these compounds can achieve effective checkpoint modulation while improving viral control and limiting tissue injury remains limited. We therefore discuss the need for rigorous target validation and a more precise approach to patient selection and treatment design. Integrating viral burden, antiviral T-cell function, checkpoint activity, and inflammatory status may help identify patients most likely to benefit from intervention and guide treatment timing, intensity, and discontinuation. Combining checkpoint modulation with direct-acting antivirals or other host-directed therapies may offer additional benefits if viral suppression and immune restoration can be appropriately balanced. A shift from sustained checkpoint blockade toward context-dependent immune modulation may provide a basis for developing more precise host-directed antiviral strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Immune checkpoints are important regulators of antiviral immunity, but their roles in viral infections are more complex than the conventional model of immune inhibition.
Evidence base and method
In this review, we examine how viral infections reshape immune checkpoint networks through antiviral signaling, protein homeostasis, cellular metabolism, and tissue-specific immune regulation.
Consensus and conclusions
This dual role presents a major challenge for therapeutic intervention, as indiscriminate checkpoint inhibition may improve viral control at the expense of increased immunopathology.
Controversies and gaps
We distinguish the pharmacological evidence supporting these compounds, which is derived primarily from oncology-related research, from the much more limited evidence of their direct antiviral activity. However, direct evidence that these compounds can achieve effective checkpoint modulation while improving viral control and limiting tissue injury remains limited.
Research and practice implications
We therefore discuss the need for rigorous target validation and a more precise approach to patient selection and treatment design. Integrating viral burden, antiviral T-cell function, checkpoint activity, and inflammatory status may help identify patients most likely to benefit from intervention and guide treatment timing, intensity, and discontinuation.

上呼吸道感染与儿童急性中耳炎发病率的关系文献综述

The Relationship Between Upper Respiratory Tract Infections and the Incidence of Acute Otitis Media in Children: A Literature Review
作者： Dina Fitriyani Rusdin Vantah, Muh. Alfian Jafar, Fransina Rande Sarungallo
摘要中文翻译上呼吸道感染(URTI)是儿童最常见的疾病之一,可能导致并发症,其中之一是急性中耳炎(AOM)。儿童更容易患AOM,因为他们的咽鼓管相对较短且更水平,引流功能不成熟。URTI 可引起鼻咽部炎症和水肿,损害咽鼓管功能并增加 AOM 的风险。本研究是一项文献综述。对 2021 年至 2025 年期间出版的总共 10 种期刊进行了审查和综合,以确定儿童 URTI 与 AOM 之间的关联,包括相关危险因素和病原体。该综述显示儿童 URTI 和 AOM 之间存在关联。(2025) 报道了统计上显着的相关性,p 值为 0.019,而 Azzahra 等人。(2025) 报告的 p 值为 0.033,PR 为 1.35。多种病毒,特别是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和鼻病毒,与 AOM 相关。年龄、咽鼓管解剖结构、接触香烟烟雾和空气污染也可能增加 AOM 的风险。URTI 通过损害咽鼓管功能和中耳通气的炎症过程与儿童 AOM 的发生相关。需要早期检测 URTI、免疫接种和控制环境危险因素,以降低儿童 AOM 的风险。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述调查了儿童上呼吸道感染 (URTI) 与急性中耳炎 (AOM) 发病率 之间的关系,重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和鼻病毒作为潜在病原体的作用。核心问题是 URTI,尤其是由 RSV 和鼻病毒引起的 URTI,是否会增加儿童 AOM 的风险。
证据基础与方法
该综述对 2021 年至 2025 年间发表的 10 种期刊进行了文献分析。它综合了研究结果,以确定儿童 URTI 和 AOM 之间的关联,包括相关的危险因素和病原体。该方法涉及回顾和总结现有研究,而不进行新的实验。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,URTI 通过损害咽鼓管功能和中耳通气的炎症过程与儿童 AOM 的发生有关。它还确定 RSV 和鼻病毒与 AOM 特别相关。
争议与缺口
证据仅限于文献综述,研究结果基于之前发表的研究。没有提及用于巩固结果的具体统计方法或荟萃分析技术。该综述没有详细讨论不同病原体的相对贡献或炎症机制。
科研与实践启示
建议早期检测 URTI、免疫接种和控制环境危险因素,以降低儿童 AOM 的风险。需要进一步研究来探索 RSV 和鼻病毒在 AOM 发展中的具体作用并确定有效的干预措施。

The Relationship Between Upper Respiratory Tract Infections and the Incidence of Acute Otitis Media in Children: A Literature Review

Authors: Dina Fitriyani Rusdin Vantah, Muh. Alfian Jafar, Fransina Rande Sarungallo
Original abstract (English)
Upper respiratory tract infection (URTI) is one of the most common illnesses in children and may lead to complications, one of which is acute otitis media (AOM). Children are more susceptible to AOM because their Eustachian tubes are relatively shorter and more horizontal, with immature drainage function. URTI can cause inflammation and edema of the nasopharynx, impairing Eustachian tube function and increasing the risk of AOM. This study was a literature review. A total of 10 journals published between 2021 and 2025 were reviewed and synthesized to determine the association between URTI and AOM in children, including relevant risk factors and pathogens. The review showed an association between URTI and AOM in children. (2025) reported a statistically significant association with a p-value of 0.019, while Azzahra et al. (2025) reported a p-value of 0.033 with a PR of 1.35. Several viruses, particularly respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and rhinovirus, were associated with AOM. Age, Eustachian tube anatomy, exposure to cigarette smoke, and air pollution may also increase the risk of AOM.URTI is associated with the occurrence of AOM in children through an inflammatory process that impairs Eustachian tube function and middle ear ventilation. Early detection of URTI, immunization, and control of environmental risk factors are needed to reduce the risk of AOM in children.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review investigates the relationship between upper respiratory tract infections (URTI) and the incidence of acute otitis media (AOM) in children, focusing on the role of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and rhinovirus as potential pathogens. The central question is whether URTI, particularly those caused by RSV and rhinovirus, increases the risk of AOM in children.
Evidence base and method
The review is a literature analysis of 10 journals published between 2021 and 2025. It synthesizes findings to determine the association between URTI and AOM in children, including relevant risk factors and pathogens. The methodology involves reviewing and summarizing existing studies without conducting new experiments.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that URTI is associated with the occurrence of AOM in children through an inflammatory process that impairs Eustachian tube function and middle ear ventilation. It also identifies RSV and rhinovirus as particularly associated with AOM.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited to a literature review, and the findings are based on previously published studies. There is no mention of specific statistical methods or meta-analytic techniques used to consolidate the results. The review does not address the relative contributions of different pathogens or the mechanisms of inflammation in detail.
Research and practice implications
Early detection of URTI, immunization, and control of environmental risk factors are recommended to reduce the risk of AOM in children. Further research is needed to explore the specific roles of RSV and rhinovirus in AOM development and to identify effective interventions.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

国家流感和 COVID-19 监测 报告:2026 年 11 月 19 日(第 47 周) - GOV.UK

National flu and COVID-19 surveillance report: 19 November 2026 (week 47) - GOV.UK
新闻简报2026 年 11 月 19 日(第 47 周)的全国流感和 COVID-19 监测 报告强调,RSV 活动有所增加,并且在英格兰的流通水平较低。急性 毛细支气管炎(通常与 RSV 相关)的急诊就诊人数有所上升,但低于季节性预期水平。截至 2026 年 11 月 15 日星期日,第 46 周 RSV 阳性率达到 5.1%,而前一周为 3.2%。0至4岁儿童的阳性率最高,为28.8%。报告指出,用于确定活动水平的方法,例如移动流行病法(MEM)和平均标准差(MSD),都是实验性的,可能会进行修订。它还提到数据基于哨点实验室,包括 SARS-CoV-2 检测结果的统一样本数据集 (USD)。该报告是更广泛的 监测 系统的一部分,该系统跟踪流感、RSV 和其他季节性呼吸道病毒,数据截至 2026 年第 46 周。研究结果旨在为公共卫生应对措施提供信息,并有待进一步分析和更新。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该报告基于截至 2026 年第 46 周(2026 年 11 月 9 日至 11 月 15 日)的数据[N5]。
地点与对象
该报告涵盖英格兰,重点关注不同年龄组和人群的流感、RSV 和 COVID-19 活动 [N4]。
事件
该报告概述了 RSV 活动增加且流通水平较低,急性 毛细支气管炎 的急诊就诊人数上升并低于季节性预期水平 [N17、N18]。
规模、影响与风险
RSV 阳性率在第 46 周有所上升,截至 2026 年 11 月 15 日星期日的 7 天滚动阳性率为 5.1%,而上周日为 3.2%。0 至 4 岁儿童的阳性率最高,滚动 7 天阳性率为 28.8% [N43、N44、N45]。
应对、状态与不确定性
该报告基于哨点实验室的数据,包括 SARS-CoV-2 检测结果的统一样本数据集 (USD)。它还提到所使用的方法(MEM 和 MSD)是实验性的,可能会在未来进行修订 [N21、N24、N25]。
A high priorityPublic-health news

National flu and COVID-19 surveillance report: 19 November 2026 (week 47) - GOV.UK

News Brief
The National Flu and COVID-19 Surveillance Report for 19 November 2026 (week 47) highlights that RSV activity increased and is circulating at low levels in England. Emergency department attendances for acute bronchiolitis, which is often associated with RSV, rose and were below seasonally expected levels. RSV positivity rates in week 46 reached 5.1% up to Sunday 15 November 2026, compared to 3.2% the previous week. The highest positivity rates were observed in children aged 0 to 4 years, with 28.8% recorded. The report notes that the methods used to determine activity levels, such as the Moving Epidemic Method (MEM) and Mean Standard Deviation (MSD), are experimental and may be revised. It also mentions that data is based on sentinel laboratories and includes a unified sample dataset (USD) for SARS-CoV-2 test results. The report is part of a broader surveillance system that tracks influenza, RSV, and other seasonal respiratory viruses, with data up to week 46 of 2026. The findings are intended to inform public health responses and are subject to further analysis and updates.
Original source text (English)
This publication is licensed under the terms of the Open Government Licence v3.0 except where otherwise stated. To view this licence, visit nationalarchives.gov.uk/doc/open-government-licence/version/3 or write to the Information Policy Team, The National Archives, Kew, London TW9 4DU, or email: psi@nationalarchives.gov.uk . Where we have identified any third party copyright information you will need to obtain permission from the copyright holders concerned. This publication is available at https://www.gov.uk/government/statistics/national-flu-and-covid-19-surveillance-reports-2026-to-2027-season/national-flu-and-covid-19-surveillance-report-19-november-2026-week-47 This report summarises the information from the surveillance systems which are used to monitor COVID-19 (caused by severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2)), influenza, respiratory syncytial virus ( RSV ) and diseases caused by other seasonal respiratory viruses in England. The report is based on data up to week 46 of 2026 (9 November to 15 November 2026). The main messages of this report are: Annual reports for the 2025 to 2026 season: Influenza in the UK, annual epidemiological report: winter 2025 to 2026 (published 28 May 2026) Epidemiology of COVID-19 in England (published 25 June 2026) Seasonal influenza vaccine uptake in all GP patients: annual report 2025 to 2026 (published 28 May 2026) Seasonal influenza vaccine uptake in school-aged children: annual report 2025 to 2026 (published 28 May 2026) Seasonal influenza vaccine uptake in frontline healthcare workers: annual report 2025 to 2026 (published 28 May 2026) Influenza activity increased and is circulating at low levels. Emergency department ( ED ) attendances for influenza-like-illness ( ILI ) increased and were above seasonally expected levels. The number of influenza-confirmed acute respiratory infection ( ARI ) incidents increased compared with the previous week. A briefing note has been issued to inform the NHS that influenza activity is now above baseline levels. Compared with the equivalent week last season (week 46 data, 2025 to 2026), vaccine uptake is lower among patients aged 65 years and over (65.8% vs 67.8%), patients aged 6 months to under 65 years in clinical risk groups (32.5% vs 33.5%), those aged 2 years (37.6% vs 38.5%), and those aged 3 years (37.5% vs 38.8%). In contrast, vaccine uptake is higher among pregnant women (33.9% vs 32.8%). COVID-19 activity was stable and is circulating at low levels. ED attendances for COVID-19-like illness decreased. The number of COVID-19 confirmed acute respiratory infections ( ARI ) incidents increased compared with the previous week. By the end of week 46 2026, 54.5% of all people aged 75 years and over in England had been vaccinated with an autumn 2026 dose. Similarly, 23.1% of all people aged under 75 years with a weakened immune system in England had been vaccinated with an autumn 2026 dose since 1 October 2026. RSV activity increased and is circulating at low levels. ED attendances for acute bronchiolitis increased and were below seasonally expected levels. Note 1: these indicators use the moving epidemic method ( MEM ) and the mean standard deviation method ( MSD ) to define thresholds to determine their respective levels of activity. Further information on these methods can be found in Influenza surveillance in Europe: establishing epidemic thresholds by the Moving Epidemic Method and Setting thresholds to determine COVID-19 activity levels using the mean standard deviation ( MSD ) method, England, 2022 to 2024 . The MEM approach is well-established for some influenza surveillance indicators, however, for other indicators both the MEM and MSD are experimental and may be subject to future revision. Influenza laboratory surveillance and GP swabbing positivity have transitioned from using MEM to using MSD . These approaches will be considered alongside expert opinion and triangulation of other data sources. The Second Generation Surveillance System ( SGSS ) captures test result information for notifiable infectious diseases, including COVID-19 and influenza, from laboratories in England. The unified sample dataset ( USD ) stores all SARS-CoV-2 test results reported to SGSS , Respiratory DataMart and UKHSA laboratories, and is used to calculate the percentage of tests positive for SARS-CoV-2 among all SARS-CoV-2 tests conducted. As of 17 November 2026, there were a total of 1,826 COVID-19 cases identified in hospital settings in week 46, remaining stable compared with 1,792 cases in the previous week. SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19) PCR positivity in hospital settings remained stable in week 46, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 7.0% up to Sunday 15 November 2026 compared with 7.2% for the same period on the previous Sunday. Positivity rates were highest among those aged 85 years and over with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 9.9% up to Sunday 15 November 2026. This has remained stable compared with 10.4% in the same age group on the previous Sunday. Note 2: data from previous seasons is aligned by day. Note 3: testing policy and practice may change over time which can impact positivity rates, therefore comparisons over time should be interpreted with caution. Notable changes in testing policy occurred during 2022 to 2023, which are outlined in the data quality report . Note 4: the highlighted line corresponds to the age group in the subplot title, grey lines correspond to all other age groups. Influenza positivity increased in week 46, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 11.4% up to Sunday 15 November 2026. This is compared with 8.3% on the previous Sunday. By type, influenza A positivity increased in week 46, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 10.1% up to Sunday 15 November 2026. Influenza B positivity remained stable in the same week, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 0.3% up to Sunday 15 November 2026. Influenza A positivity rates were highest among those aged between 5 and 11 years, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 23.2% up to Sunday 15 November 2026. This has increased from 13.3% in the same age group on the previous Sunday. Influenza B positivity rates for the same period were highest among those aged between 12 and 17 years, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 2.1%. This has increased from 1.4% in the same age group on the previous Sunday. RSV swab-positivity-based PCR test results reported through SGSS have been included as a pilot indicator from week 49 2025 onwards. RSV positivity increased in week 46, with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 5.1% up to Sunday 15 November 2026, compared with 3.2% up to the previous Sunday. RSV positivity rates were highest in those aged between 0 and 4 years with a rolling 7-day positivity rate of 28.8% up to Sunday 15 November 2026 in week 46. This has increased from 19.1% in the same age group on the previous Sunday. Respiratory DataMart is a sentinel laboratory-based surveillance system where participating laboratories report positive and negative test results for a number of respiratory viruses from samples primarily taken in hospital. A small proportion of primary care samples are also included in this reporting. In week 46, data is based on reporting from 9 out of the 14 sentinel laboratories. In week 46, 5,156 respiratory specimens reported through the Respiratory DataMart System were tested for influenza. There were 588 positive samples for influenza: 397 influenza A (not subtyped), 7 influenza A (H3N2), 166 influenza A (H1N1)pdm09, and 20 influenza B. Overall, influenza positivity increased to 11.4% in week 46 compared with 8.0% in the previous week. In week 46, 5,437 respiratory specimens reported through the Respiratory DataMart System were tested for SARS-CoV-2. There were 346 positive samples for SARS-CoV-2. SARS-CoV-2 positivity increased slightly to 6.4% compared with 6.0% in the previous week, with the highest positivity in those aged 80 years and over at 9.3%. RSV positivity increased to 5.2%, with the highest positivity in those aged under 5 years at 26.7%. Adenovirus positivity decreased slightly to 1.6%, with the highest positivity in those aged under 5 years at 5.1%. Human metapneumovirus ( hMPV ) positivity increased to 0.7%, with the highest positivity in those aged under 5 years at 1.7%. Parainfluenza positivity decreased slightly to 2.9%, with the highest positivity in those aged under 5 years at 8.7%. Rhinovirus positivity decreased to 11.3%, with the highest positivity in those aged under 5 years at 28.5%. DataMart data is provisional and subject to retrospective updates. Note 5: shading represents 95% confidence intervals. UKHSA conducts genomic surveillance of SARS-CoV-2 lineages. This section provides an overview of circulating lineages in England, derived from data on sequenced PCR -positive SARS-CoV-2 samples in SGSS . The prevalence of UKHSA -designated lineages among sequenced cases is presented in Figure 13. To account for reporting delays, we report the proportion of lineages within COVID-19 cases that have had a sequenced positive sample between 12 October 2026 and 25 October 2026. Note that low sequencing numbers, especially within the latest reporting period, will impact the accuracy of the prevalence estimates. These most recent figures should therefore be interpreted with caution. Note that lineages will be grouped independently from their parent lineage once they reach sufficient prevalence, and may be re-grouped into their parent lineage if their prevalence subsequently falls. The data quality report contains more information on lineage groupings. Analysis of viruses from primary and secondary care shows that circulating A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses show low genetic diversity and all belong to clade D.3.1.1. Observations published from the World Health Organization (WHO) influenza vaccine composition meeting (VCM) in September 2026 suggest low reactivity of some viruses within the D.3.1.1 subclade with post-infection ferret antisera raised against A/Missouri/11/2025 (H1N1)pdm09-like virus – the A(H1N1)pdm09 component in 2026 Southern and 2026/2027 Northern Hemisphere cell- and egg-based influenza vaccines - and human sera post-vaccination with the Southern Hemisphere vaccine 2026. It is not yet clear how vaccine effectiveness against clinical disease may be affected by these observations. Vaccines typically still provide protection against severe clinical disease when drifted strains are seen with only a modest reduction in effectiveness. The characterisation of circulating A(H3N2) viruses shows that A(H3N2) viruses are currently showing low genetic diversity. All sequenced viruses to date belong to genetic subclade K. To date, only a small number of influenza B viruses have been detected, which show some genetic diversity. Between week 35 2026 (week ending 30 August 2026) and week 46 2026 (week ending 15 November 2026), the UKHSA respiratory virus unit ( RVU ) has genetically characterised 477 seasonal influenza viruses, and identified 343 influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses, 100 influenza A(H3N2) viruses, and 34 influenza B viruses. Details of the characterised viruses by subtype are shown in Tables 1, 2 and 3. The RVU has confirmed by genome sequencing the detection of live attenuated influenza vaccine ( LAIV ) viruses in 17 influenza A/B positive samples, collected from children aged between 2 and 16 years. UKHSA RVU performs antigenic characterisation of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, influenza A(H3N2) and influenza B viruses using haemagglutination inhibition ( HI ) assays. Data from these assays are used to compare how similar the currently circulating influenza viruses are to the strains included in seasonal influenza vaccines, and to monitor for changes in circulating influenza viruses. Similarity of currently circulating influenza strains to vaccine strains is defined as having an antibody titre within 4-fold when compared with reference viruses representative of the vaccine strain. Antigenic characterisation of viruses in the current reporting period is underway. A(H1N1)pdm09: 13 A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses have been antigenically characterised and 11 were similar to reference viruses representative of the A/Missouri/11/2025 (H1N1)pdm09-like Northern Hemisphere 2026/27 (H1N1)pdm09 vaccine strains. A(H3N2): 6 A(H3N2) viruses have been antigenically characterised and 6 were similar to reference viruses representative of the A/Darwin/1454/2025 (H3N2)-like and A/Darwin/1415/2025 (H3N2)-like Northern Hemisphere 2026/27 (H3N2) vaccine strains. Influenza positive samples are screened for mutations in the virus neuraminidase ( NA ) and the cap-dependent endonuclease of the polymerase acidic protein (PA) genes known to confer neuraminidase inhibitor (oseltamivir and zanamivir) or baloxavir resistance, respectively. Results from this surveillance are given in Table 4. There have been 9 detections with mutations related to reduced susceptibility/resistance to oseltamivir and/or reduced susceptibility to baloxavir. Data is presented on viral ARI incidents in different settings that are reported to UKHSA health protection teams ( HPTs ). Please note that reporting practices are known to vary between seasons and between regions. Any interpretation of temporal and regional trends should consider the likelihood of differences in reporting of ARI incidents over time and between regions. There were 147 new ARI incidents reported in week 46 in England. These included: 119 incidents from care homes, of which 26 were due to influenza A, 17 were due to SARS-CoV-2, 17 were due to influenza (no type information available), 7 were due to multiple pathogens, 5 were due to other pathogens and 3 were due to RSV . No pathogen was reported in 44 incidents 1 incident from hospitals, of which 1 was due to SARS-CoV-2 21 incidents from educational settings, of which 6 were due to influenza A, 5 were due to RSV and 1 was due to influenza (no type information available). No pathogen was reported in 9 incidents 1 incident from prisons, of which 1 was due to influenza A 5 incidents from other settings, of which 3 were due to SARS-CoV-2 and 1 was due to influenza (no type information available). No pathogen was reported in 1 incident FluSurvey is an internet-based participatory surveillance system based on the InfluenzaNet platform. It monitors trends of influenza-like illness ( ILI ) in the community using self-reported respiratory symptoms from registered participants. As a participatory surveillance system, new signups can occur throughout the season and participation can vary in each week. The European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control ( ECDC ) ILI case definition of sudden onset of symptoms with at least one of fever (chills), malaise, headache, muscle pain and at least one of cough, sore throat, shortness of breath is used for reporting. Please note that ILI is a broad definition and can include other respiratory illnesses such as COVID-19. Healthcare use is presented as self-reported use of health services among participants meeting the ILI ECDC case definition. Where a person reports use of more than one health care service, secondary care will be indicated over primary care use and physical attendance to primary care will be indicated over use of remote services (for example, online NHS services, telephoning their GP or 111). During week commencing 9 November 2026: there were 2,522 participants who completed the weekly symptoms questionnaire 396 (15.7%) reported fever or cough and 129 (5.1%) met the ILI case definition, in the past week 15.5% of participants meeting the ILI case definition reported contact with healthcare services as a result of self-reported symptoms; the most frequently reported contact was a visit to the GP the proportion of participants meeting the ILI case definition remained stable compared with the previous report week (5.1% compared with 5.1% in week 45) data submitted in the current week (commencing 16 November) is available on the UKHSA data dashboard to support more timely insights. For further details see the data quality report Syndromic surveillance collects data from various healthcare sources where presentations are classified by patterns of symptoms compatible with specific infections. In some settings, the syndromic diagnosis can be supplemented by (rapid) testing. In this report, ED attendances are displayed. Further details and data from other syndromic surveillance systems can be found in the syndromic surveillance weekly summaries . During the week ending on 15 November 2026, ED attendances for acute respiratory infections increased and were similar to the seasonally expected level. ED attendances for COVID-19-like illness increased. ED attendances for influenza-like illness increased and were above seasonally expected levels. ED attendances for acute bronchiolitis (a syndrome related to RSV infection) increased and were below seasonally expected levels. NHS 111 triaged calls and online assessments for acute respiratory infections increased in children under the age of 15 years, and remained at seasonally expected levels. GP in-hours consultation rates for upper and lower respiratory tract infections increased, notably in children aged 1 to 4 and 5 to 14 years but remained at seasonally expected levels. Consultation rates for influenza-like illness also increased and are above expected levels. GP out-of-hours daily contacts for respiratory conditions increased, including acute respiratory infections, influenza-like illness and acute bronchitis/bronchiolitis. Contacts for acute respiratory infections and acute bronchitis/bronchiolitis remained at or below baseline levels, while influenza-like illness was above expected levels. 每周呼吸道病毒更新,2026 年 10 月第 46 周 - 欧洲疾病预防和控制中心 (ECDC)

Weekly respiratory virus update, week 46, October 2026 - European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC)
新闻简报欧洲疾病预防和控制中心 (ECDC) 于 2026 年 11 月 20 日发布了 2026 年 10 月第 46 周的每周呼吸道病毒更新。该报告表明,整个欧盟/欧洲经济区的呼吸道疾病活动仍处于基线或较低水平。虽然一些国家的 SARS-CoV-2 病例数不断增加,但流感活动较低且缓慢上升,RSV 活动仍然非常低。该更新作为一般的 监测 报告,没有提供 RSV 的具体病例或死亡人数。ECDC 强调对呼吸道病毒的持续监测,但本报告中没有概述针对 RSV 的详细应对措施或风险评估。

时间
2026年10月第46周的每周呼吸道病毒更新于2026年11月20日发布。该报告涵盖2026年10月1日至2026年11月20日期间。
地点与对象
该报告重点关注欧盟 (EU) 和欧洲经济区 (EEA) 国家,这些国家共同构成了 监测 下的人口。
事件
报告指出,欧盟/欧洲经济区的呼吸道疾病活动仍处于基线或较低水平。报告还提到,一些国家的 SARS-CoV-2 病例数量持续增加,而流感和 RSV 活动总体仍然较低。
规模、影响与风险
据报告,呼吸道疾病活动处于基线或较低水平,没有提供 RSV 的具体病例或死亡人数。多个国家的 SARS-CoV-2 病例数量正在增加,但没有给出具体数字。流感活动虽然较低,但正在缓慢增加。
应对、状态与不确定性
该报告是欧洲疾病预防和控制中心(ECDC)的官方更新,表明正在监测情况。没有提及 RSV 的具体应对措施或风险级别,但一般 监测 正在进行中。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Weekly respiratory virus update, week 46, October 2026 - European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC)

News Brief
The European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC) released a weekly respiratory virus update for week 46, October 2026, on 20 November 2026. The report indicates that respiratory illness activity remains at baseline or low levels across the EU/EEA. While SARS-CoV-2 case numbers are increasing in several countries, influenza activity is low and slowly rising, and RSV activity remains very low. The update serves as a general surveillance report, with no specific case or death counts provided for RSV. The ECDC emphasizes ongoing monitoring of respiratory viruses, but no detailed response measures or risk assessments are outlined for RSV in this report.

叙述审查详细信息沙特阿拉伯儿科 呼吸道合胞病毒 负担 - Bioengineer.org

Narrative Review Details Saudi Arabia Pediatric Respiratory Syncytial Virus Burden - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A comprehensive narrative review published in the Journal of Infection and Public Health highlights the significant burden of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in Saudi Arabia, particularly among young infants. The review, conducted by researchers Ammar A. Alabdali, Chu Guat Yeap, and Thamir A. Alandijany, synthesizes observational studies, hospital-based surveillance, molecular genotyping, and health-economic models to assess RSV's impact on pediatric respiratory health. The findings indicate that RSV is a major cause of hospital admissions, with detection rates ranging from 20 to 30 percent in general pediatric cohorts. In Riyadh, 28.9 percent of 1,429 screened children under five tested positive for RSV, accounting for 79.1 percent of all virus-positive samples. In an 885-patient tertiary cohort, 60.3 percent of confirmed cases required hospital admission, with 26.2 percent needing intensive care and an overall mortality rate of 2.4 percent. In Jazan, 31.8 percent of hospitalized cases were admitted to intensive care, with a mortality rate of 8.7 percent. The authors emphasize that variations in detection rates are linked to clinical severity and testing criteria rather than geographic differences. They also note that young age, especially under twelve months, is the primary determinant of severe disease and hospitalization. The review underscores the need for continued genomic tracking of RSV-B isolates, as monoclonal antibodies targeting specific epitopes are being developed for broader use. The study highlights the seasonal nature of RSV, with peak activity between December and January and lower circulation during the summer months (June through August).
查看新闻五要素
时间
该评论于 2026 年 11 月 23 日发布。引用的事件日期包括秋季和冬季,12 月和 1 月强度最高,夏季(6 月至 8 月)流通量最低。
地点与对象
该审查重点关注沙特阿拉伯,特别是利雅得和吉赞,数据来自东部省。它检查了十八岁或以下的儿科人群,包括两岁以下的婴儿和十二个月以下的儿童。
事件
《感染与公共卫生杂志》上发表的一篇综合叙述性评论详细介绍了沙特阿拉伯 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的负担,强调了其在儿科呼吸道疾病发病率和入院率中的作用。该审查基于观察性调查、基于医院的 监测 报告、分子基因分型研究和健康经济模型。
规模、影响与风险
RSV was detected in 28.9 percent of 1,429 screened children in Riyadh, accounting for 79.1 percent of all virus-positive samples in that cohort. In an 885-patient tertiary cohort in Riyadh, 60.3 percent of confirmed pediatric patients required hospital admission, with 26.2 percent needing intensive care support and an overall mortality rate of 2.4 percent. In Jazan, 31.8 percent of hospitalized cases were admitted to intensive care, with a mortality rate of 8.7 percent.
应对、状态与不确定性
作者手动筛选出版物,并根据主要来源交叉检查提取的临床背景、观察期、诊断方法、季节模式和经济参数。由于区域研究分母、临床病例选择和诊断方案的异质性,他们选择了叙述综合而不是统计汇总。该综述还讨论了新兴单克隆抗体和母体免疫方案对未来婴儿疾病预防的潜在影响。
